# Ethiopic Version of the History of Bsoy (La version éthiopienne de l’histoire de Bsoy)

**Author**: Anonymous  
**Series**: PO Tome 49  
**Columns**: pp. 296–360  

[p. 296] {#po.49.p296}
አመ ፡ቿ ፡ ለኀምሌ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። ዜናሁ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ወሠናይ ፡ ባሕታዊ ፡ ወንጹሕ፤ ፡ዘነበረ ፡ በምድረ ፡ ግብጽ ። ወተጽሕፈት ፡ ዛቲ ፣ መጽሐፍ" ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ዕረፍቱ ፡ በእደ ፡ ቀሲስ ፡ ንጹሕ' ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ሐጺር ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ እጉሁ ፡ በመንፈስ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውእቱ" ፡ ገዳም ። ወኮኑ ፡ ክልኤሆሙ ፡ አርድእተ ፡ ተአዛዝያነ ፡ ወገባርያነ ፡ ፈቃዱ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወዓቢይ ፡ በመዊእ ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ትዕቀበነ ፡ ለኩልነ ፡ አሜን ። ወኮነ ፡ ሱራሬ ፡ ዜናሁ' ፡ ወሥርዐቱ ፡ ትሩፈ ፡ ወዝኅ ። ወሕይወቱ ፡ ሠናይተ ፡ ወብፅዕተ ፡ እንተ ፡ ድኅነት ፡ እምኀጣውእ ፡ ወስሕተት"' ። ወቀርበት ፡ ኅበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡፤ በተልእኮ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ጽርዐት ፤ ወአስትቶ ፡ ከመ ፡ መላእክት ። ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ይትባረክ ፡ ስሙ ። ' ወኮነ ፡ ፅረፍቱ'! ፡ አመ ፡ ፳ ለኀምሌ!፡ ። ሰላም” ፡ ወጸጋ ፡ 'ይኡድዎ ፡ ወይክድንዎ” ፡ ወይምላእ ፡ 'ላዕሌሁ ፡ ለዘይጽሕፍ ፡ ወያነብብ ፡ ወያፈቅር ፡ ገድሎ ፡ አሜን" ።
The eighth of Hamle: Abbä Bsoy. In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. History of our holy father Bsoy, the great and beautiful solitary, the pure, who dwelt in the land of Egypt. This book was written after his death by the hand of the pure priest John (Yohannes) the Little, who was his brother in the Spirit when he (= Bsoy) entered the desert. Both were the obedient disciples and executors of the will of our holy and great father Bämoy (Bamawi'). May their prayer guard us all, amen. The foundation of his history and his conduct was virtuous and sweet. His life (was) beautiful and happy, it which was saved from sin and fault. It was close to God through service (performed) without interruption or negligence like the angels - blessed be the name of God! His death took place on the eighth of Hamle. May peace and grace surround, protect, and fill him who will write, read, and love his life, amen.
"ሰላም ፡ አኅዊነ” ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ፍቁራኒሁ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ሕያው ። አጽምኡ!' ፡ ኅቤየ ፣ ወስምዑ ፡ እምኔየ ፡ 'ከመ ፡ እንግርክሙ" ፡ በእንተ ፡ ገድሉ ፤ ወሥነ” ፣ ሥርዐቱ ፡ ለክቡር ፤ ወቅዱስ ፡ ወንጹሕ ፡ 'አቡነ ፡፤ ብሶይ" ፡ ዘኀረዮ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወገደፈ”፤ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዓለም” ፡ ወፈጸመ ፡ መልእክቶ”" ፡ ወምንኩስናሁ ፡ በንጽሕ ፡፤ ወፍድፍድና ። ወኮነ ፡ ሰማዕተ” ፡ በገድሉ ፡ ዓቢይ ። ወአቅ ረበ ፡ ነፍሶ” ፡ ወሥጋሁ ፤ ውኩፈ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ወነበረ ፡ እንዘ ፡ የአውድ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ ወበዐታተ ፡ ወውስተ ፡ ከርሠ ፡ ምድር ። 'ወሐነጸ ፡ በንጽሕ"' ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ "ወኮነ ፤ ዐቃቤ ፡ ትእዛዛተፆ ፡ አምላኩ ፣ ወይትፌሣሕ ፡ በገድሉ ። ወመሀረ” ፡ ወአድ ኀነ ፡ ነፍሰ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ወአስተጋብኦሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ በአእምሮ ፡ ኀይለ” ፡ መለኮት ። ሰማዕትየ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዘጸሐፈ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ዜናሁ ። አነ!፡ እነግረክሙ ፡ ዘርእያ ፡ አዕይንትየ ፡ እምኅቤሁ ፡ ጊዜ ፤ እምድኅረ ፡ "ጊዜ ። ወጸሐፍኩ” ፡ ዘንተ ፡ 'ለበቆሞዔተ ፡ ነፍስክሙ” ፣ ወለእለ ፡ ይፈቅዱ ፡ ይት
Greetings, my brothers in our Lord Christ, friends of the living God. Pay attention to me and listen to me so that I may speak to you of the (spiritual) combat and the beauty of the conduct of our illustrious, holy, and pure father Bsoy whom God chose. || [He] rejected everything that is in the century and accomplished his task and his monastic duty in purity and fullness. He was a witness (of God) through his great (spiritual) combat. He offered his soul and his body accepted to God. He remained traversing the desert and the caves (which) are in the heart of the earth. || [He] built up his body in purity; he was a guardian of the commandments of his God and was rejoiced by his (spiritual) combat. He taught and saved the soul of many and gathered them into the kingdom of heaven through the knowledge of the power of the divinity. My witness is God, says he who wrote this book of his history. I, I am going to tell you what my eyes saw regarding him moment after moment. I wrote this for the utility of your soul and for those who want to follow this teaching.
ልዉ ፡ዘንተ ፡ ትምህርተ ። "ወለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ወእሙ” ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ጌራን ፡ ጸድቃን ፡ ንጹሐን ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ መፍቀርያነ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወያፈደፍዱ ፡ ምጽዋተ ፡ ተሩፈ ። ወፍጥረቶሙ” ፡ ምድረ ፡ ኅጓዝ ፡ ቅሩበ ፡፤ ለምድረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ እምሀገር ፡ እንተ ፡ ስማ ፡ ተስተልብታ”"' ። ሰብእ ፡ ፍሱሐን ፡ ወንጹሐን ፡ በሥጋሆሙ ። ወሶበ ፡
The father and mother of this holy Abbä Bsoy (were) virtuous, just, and pure people before God, loving the Church and giving in abundance an excellent alms. (The land of) their family (was) the land of Hegwaz (= Hedjaz?) near the land of Egypt; (it was) from the city whose name (is) Tastalbetä.


[p. 298] {#po.49.p298}
ርእየ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፤ ሥርአቶሙ ፣ ወሀቦሙ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ ተባዕተ ፡ ወባረከ” ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ንዋዮሙ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ መዋዕል" ፡ አፅረፈ ፡ አቡሆሙ፤ ፡ ወተረፈት ፡ እሞሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ታልህቆሙ ፡ ወትሕፅኖሙ” ፡ በተወክሎ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወአባ ፡ ብሶይ” ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ይንእስ ፡ እምነ ፡ አኃዊሁ ። ወበአሐቲ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ አስተርአያ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚ አብሔር ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ ምስሌሃ ፡ ደቂቃ ፡ ንውማን ። ወይቤላ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ለአከኒ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኀቤኪ ፡ ወይቤለኪ ፡ ሀብኒአ ፡ ፳ እምደቂቅኪ ፡ ዘይቄ ድስ ፡ ስምየ ፡ በማዕከለ ፡ አሕዛብ ። ወትቤ" ፡ እሙ ፡ ለሕፃን ፡ እግዚእየ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ዙሎሙ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወዘፈቀደ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ይንሣእ ። ወሰፍሐ” ፣ መልአክ ፡ ወአኀዘ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ያመልኮ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ አም ልኮ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ወለዝንቱ ፡ የኀሥሦ ። ወትቤሎ”፤ ፡ ለመልአክ ፡ ንሣእ ፡ ዘይልህቅ ፡ ወይጸንፅ ፡ እምኔሁ” ፡ ወዝንቱሰ”፤ ፡ ንኡስ ፡ ወድኩም ። ወይቤላ ፤ መልአክ ፡ 'ዝን ቱኬ ፡ ዘይደሉ ፡ ለተልእኮ” ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወጽንዐ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ታስተርኢ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ድኩማን ። ዘንተ ፡ ይቤላ ፡ ተሰወረ ፡ እምኔሃ” ።
(They were) joyful and pure people in their body. When God saw their conduct, he gave them male children and blessed their goods. After days, their (= the children's) father died and their mother remained (alone) to raise them and feed them in trust in God. Abbä Bsoy the saint was smaller than his brothers. One night, the angel of God appeared to her (= the mother) while her children were asleep with her. The angel said to her: "Behold, God has sent me to you and he says to you: Give me one of your children who will sanctify My name among the nations." The mother of the child said: "My lord, behold, all (are) God's: whichever of them he wants, let him take (him)." The angel reached out (his arm), seized the hand of Abbä Bsoy, and said: "This one will worship God with a beautiful worship and he will seek him." (The woman) said to the angel: "Take (another) who is bigger and stronger than him: this one is small and weak." The angel said to her: "It is this one who is suitable for the service of God: the strength of God is manifested on the weak." The angel said this to her and vanished from her (sight).
ወሶበ ፡ ልህቀ ፡ ወጸንዐ"' ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ 'አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ሖረ ፣ በተወክሎ ፡ እግዚአ ብሔር ፡ ወያፈቅርፇ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይቅረብ ፡ በሥርዐተ”ሞ ፡ ምንኩስና ። ወሶበ ፡ ፈቀደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር"! ፡ ከመ” ፡ ይፈጽም ፡ ዘአሰፈዎ” ፡ ሰሐቦ ፡ ከመ ፡ በግዕ ፡ ንጹሕ ፡ ዘአልቦ ፡ ነውረ ፡ ወአምጽኦ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ዘአስቄጢዎኖ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ ግብጽ ። ወአቅረቦ ፡ ወአማሕፀኖ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ኖላዊ ፡ ር ፡ ኀዳፌ” ፡ ነፍሳት ፡ አቡነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በምዊ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ 'ሊተ ፡፤ ቤዛዌ ፡ ወመራሔ ፡ ሕይወትየ ፡ 'ሊተ ፡ ለንኡስ” ፡ ዮሐንስ ። ወተወክፎ ፡ "በፍሥሐ ፡ አቡነ ፡ በምዊዎ ፡ ወአልበሶ ፡ አርአያ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዘምንኩስና” ። ወኩሎ ፡ ዘአዘዞ” ፡ ይገብር ፡ ወይፌጽም ፡ 'በትሕትና ፡ ዓቢይ'! ። "ወርእዮ ፡ ንጹሐነ ፡ እለ ፡ ርቱዐ ፡ የሐውሩ ፡ ይጤይቅ”፤ ፡ ገድሎሙ ፡ ወሥርዐቶሙ ፡ ወይትፌሣሕ” ፡ በሠ ናይ ፡ ሕይወቶሙ ፡ ወምናኔሆሙ ፡ ዘሥጋት ፡ ወኩሎ ፡ 'ፍትወታተ ፡ ዘዝዓለም” ፡ ወተመሰሎቶሙ ፡ በመላእክት ። ወበኩሉ ፡ ያአኩቶ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወይሴብሖ ፡
When the holy Abbä Bsoy had grown and had become strong, he went away in trust in God; he wished to devote himself to the monastic rule. When God wanted to accomplish what he had promised him, he led him like a pure sheep without blemish. He led him to the desert of Scetis ('Asqëti), the desert of Egypt. He brought him and entrusted him to the virtuous pastor, governor of souls, our holy father Bämoy (Bamwi). This is he who was for me a redeemer and the guide of my life, to me, little John (Yohannes). Our father Bämoy welcomed him with joy and clothed him in the holy habit of the monastic life. Everything he commanded him, (Bsoy) did and accomplished with great humility. Seeing the pure ones who walked straight, he studied their (spiritual) combat and their conduct and rejoiced in their beautiful life, in their contempt for the things of the flesh and the desires of this world, and in their resemblance to the angels. In everything he gave thanks to God, glorified him, and praised him.
ወይዌድሶ ። ወአሐተ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ አቡነ” ፡ በምዊ ፡ መምህርነ ፡ ኢይደሉ ፡ ለአኃው ፡ ያልዕሉ ፡ ንጻሬሆሙ ፡ ወያክፍኡ ፡ ገጸ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ንጹሐን ፣ ወኢይርአዩ ፡ ምንተኒ ፡ ካልአ ። ወይኩን ፡ ንጸሬከ” ፡ ታሕተ?” ፡ ምድር ፡ ወኢትነጽር ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ምክያደ ፡ እገሪከ ። ወአዕይንቲከ” ፡ ያንቃዕድዋ” ፡ ኀበ ፡ ዋሕድ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወሕሊናከ ፡ ይስግድ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ጊዜ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዐ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ እምአብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አት ሐተ ፡ ንጸሬሁ” ፡ ኮ ዓመተ ፡ ወኢርእየ፤ ፡ ገጸ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ በድፍረትዎ ፡ ፍጹመ ። ውእ
One day, our father Bämoy, our master, said to him: "It is not fitting that the brothers lift their gaze; pure men make (their) face blind and see nothing else (but the earth?). Let your gaze be toward the earth and look only at your feet. Let your eyes contemplate the unique God and let your thought prostrate itself before him at all times." When (Bsoy) heard this from the holy father, he kept his gaze lowered for three years and never looked at the

ቱሰ ፡ ይተሉ ፡ መጻሕፍተ ፡ ቅዱሳተ” ፡ ወይጽሕቅ ፡ ለአንብቦቶን ፡ 'ወትረ ፡ ይዜ ከሮን ፡ በውስተ ፡ ልቡ ። ወያረውያ ፡ ለነፍሱ ፡ ነገራተ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ ቃላተ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በከመ ፡ ተብህለ ፡ እስመ ፡ ጠቢብ ፡ በልቡ ፡ የዐቅብ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ትእዛ ዛተ ፡ ወይገብሮን ። ወይጹሊ ፡ ወትረ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ጽርዐት ፡ ወዘእንበለ ፡ ሀኬት ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዘ ፡፤ ጳውሎስ ፡ ሐዋርያ ። ወኮነ ፡ ጽኑዐ ፡ በጾም ፡ ወቀዋሜ ፡ በትጋህ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ለያልይ ። ወቃለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በውስተ ፡ አፉሁ፡ ይጥዕም ፡ እመዓር ፡ ወእምሶከር ። ወይትዌሰክ”' ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ዕለት ፡ በቀሩዔተ ፡ ትሩፋተ ። ወይልህቅ ፡ 'ከመ ፡ ፅፅኞ ፣ እንተ ፡ ትክልት ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሙሐዘ ፡ ማይ ፡ እንተ ፡ ትሁብ ፡ ፍሬሃ ፡ በበጊዜሃ ፡ ወቄጽላኒ ፡ ኢይትነገፍ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በምዊ ፡ ሥርዐቶ ፡ 'ሠናየ ፡ ወትሕ ትናሁዎ ፡ አፍቀሮ ። ወያነክር” ፤ ወይትፌሣሕ ፡ በመንፈስ ፡ ወሰመዮ” ፡ ስያ ፡ ወትር ጓሜሁ” ፡ ምሥራቅ ፡ ብሂል ፡ በእንተ ፡ ብርሃነ ፡ ነፍሱ ። ' ወአርአየ ፡ ዝጉኃተ” ፡ ግብሮ ፡'ወፍድፍድና ፡ ገድሉ” ። ወፈቀደፆ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ 'የኀውጾ ። ወእምብዝኅ ፡ ያፈ ቅሮ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ነሥኦ ፡ እንተ ፡ ባሕቲቱ ፡ ወአብኦ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዘወትረ ፡ ቀዊሞ ፡ ይጹሊ ፡ ወሐነጾ ፡ በሠናይ ፡ ሥርዐት ።
face of a man with insolence. He followed the holy Scriptures and applied himself to reading them constantly and to retaining them by heart. He refreshed his soul with the words of life, the words of the Holy Spirit, as it is said: The wise man keeps all the commandments in his heart and executes them. He prayed constantly without respite and without idleness, as Paul the apostle (had) ordered. He was firm in fasting and standing in vigil every night; the word of God in his mouth was sweeter than honey and sugar. Every day increased the merit of (his) virtues. He grew like a tree that (is) planted by a stream of water, which gives its fruits in each of its seasons and whose foliage does not fall. When the holy Bämoy saw his beautiful conduct and his humility, he loved him. He was astonished and rejoiced in spirit and called him Seyä - its (= of this word) translation is "Orient" - because of the light of his soul. (Bsoy) showed the perfection of his acts and the excellence of his (spiritual) combat. God wanted to visit him. As he loved the holy Bsoy much, he took him alone, brought him to where he prayed constantly standing, and established him in a beautiful conduct.
ወእምዝ ፡ ተፈጸመ ፡ አቡነ ፡ በምዊ” ፡ ወተረፍኩ ፡ አነ ፡ ንኡስ ፡ ወምኑን ፡ ምስለ ፡ እጐ ፡ ቡሩክ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ብሶይ ። ወነበርነ ፡ ክልኤነ ፡ በፍቅር ፡ መንፈሳዊ ፡ ኀበ ፡ መካን ፡ እንተ ፡ ኀረያ ፤ እግዚአብሔር” ። ወአብሰ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ወዝኅ” ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ይተሉ ፡ ጾመ ፤ ወጸሎተ'”" ። ወኢይጥዕም ፡ ምንተኒ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ በበሰሙን ። ወየኀሥሥ፤፤ ፡ መጸሕፍተ ፡ ቅዱሳተ ፡ ወይትመሀር ፡ ወያጸነግል!” ። ወወትረ'" ፡ ይጹሊ ፡ ትንቢተ ፡ ኤርምያስ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ወሶበ'” ፡ ያነብብ ፣ ይመጽእ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ኤርምያስ ፡ ነቢይ'" ፡ ወይተረጉም ፡ ሎቱ ፡ መጽሐፎ ፡ ወይነግሮ ፡ ወይትፌሣህ ፡ በርእየቱ'” ። ወይከሥት ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ብዙኀ ፡ ትምህርተ ፡ ወይነግሮ ፣ መንፈሳዌ" ። "ወይትዌሰክ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ዕለት!" ፣ ወይትመክአብ'” ፡ በሥርዐት ፡ ወበግብር ። ወይጸውም”"" ፡ ክልኤ ፡ ክልኤ ፡ ሱባዔ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ይነሥእ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ውእቶን ፡ መዋ ዕል ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጥዕም''፤ ፡ ይነሥእ ፡ ኅብስተ ፡ ይቡሰ ፡ ወጹወ ፡ ዐካረ ። ወኩሎ ፡ ዘይገ ብር ፡ ሥርዓቶ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ወገድሎ ፣ ልዕልተ'" ፣ በሰውር ። ወውእቱሰ ፡ ይፈቅድ ፡ ብሕታዌ ፤ ወጽሙና'' ። ወያፈቅር ፡ ይንበር'" ፡ ኀበ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ባሕቲቱ ፡ እስመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ጸውዖ ፡ ለጽሙና ፡ ወመርሖ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩን ፡፤ ምምሕፃነ ፡ ጌረ ፡ ለመድኀኒተ ፡ ነፍሰ ፡ ብዙኃን ።
After that, our father Bämoy passed away and I remained, I, small and despicable, with the blessed brother, the holy father Bsoy. We both remained in spiritual love in a place that God had chosen. As for the holy and overflowing (with virtues?) father Bsoy, he devoted himself to fasting and prayer. He ate nothing except once a week. He explored the holy Scriptures, learned (them), and meditated (on them). He read constantly as prayer the prophecy of Jeremiah the prophet and, when he read (it), Jeremiah the prophet came to him, explained his book to him, spoke to him, and rejoiced at his sight. He revealed to him many teachings and spoke to him of (the) spiritual (life). (Bsoy) grew every day and redoubled (in virtue) in (his) conduct and (his) actions. He fasted one week out of two and, when he took (something) after these days to eat (it), he took dry bread and refuse salt. Everything he did - his beautiful conduct and his elevated (spiritual) combat - (was) in secret. He wanted solitude and effort. He loved to dwell in the desert alone, for God had called him to effort and had guided him so that he would be an excellent refuge for the salvation of the soul of many.


[p. 302] {#po.49.p302}
ወእምድኅረ» ፡ መዋዕል ፡ እቤሎ ፡ ለእጉየ ፡ ቅዱሱ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ናሁ ፡ እሬእየከ ፡ ታፈቅር ፡ ጽሙና"" ፡ ወነቢረ ፡ ባሕቲትከ ፡ ወአነሂ" ፡ እፈቱ ፡ ከማሁ ። ነዓ ፤ ከመ ፡ ንትጋህ ፡ በዛቲ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ ወንግበር'" ፣ ጸሎተ ፡ ወስኢለ ። ወለእመ ፡ ፈቀደ!” ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በሥምረቱ'” ፤ ውእቱ”! ፡ ይከሥት ፡ ለነ ፡ ወያርእየነ ፡ ዘይበቀዓነ ፡ ወያስተዳልወነ ። ወይቤለኒ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ትቤ ፡ ወቆምነ ፡ ለጸሎት” ፡ እስከ ፡ ደኅሪ ፡ ሌሊት ። ወሶበ ፡ ቀርበ ፡ ለጸቢሕ ፡ አስተርአየነ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወይቤለነ' ፡ ከመዝ ፣ ይቤለክሙ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ንበሩ ፡ በበ ፡ ባሕቲትክሙ"” ፡ በመካናቲክሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትፈጽሙ ፡ ሀብተ ፡ ዘጸገወክሙ" ፡ ለአድኅኖ ፡ ነፍሳተ ፡ ብዙኃን ። ወአንተሰ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ንበር ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ወአነ ፡ እፌኑ ፡ ኀቤከ ፡ ሕዝበ ፡ ብዙኀ ፡ ይቤ ፡ እግዚእ' ። ወእምዝ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ለአብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ወአ ንተኒ ፡ ረሐቅ ፤ ንስቲተ ፡ ወግበር ፡ ለከ ፡ ማኅደረ ፡ ወንበር ፤ ባሕቲትከ ። ወአነ ፡ አስተጋብእ ፡ 'ብዙኀ ፡ ሕዝበ ፡ ወአመጽእ ፡ ኀቤከ'”' ፡ ይቤ ፡ እግዚእ ። ወይከውን ፡ ምኔተ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ገዳም'ዎ ፡ ወይዜከሩ ፡ ስመከ ፡ በውስቴታ ። ወይቄድሱ ፡ 'ስመ ፡ አምላኮሙ”” ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቄድሱ ፡ ልዑላን ። ወይትፌጸም"” ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ለዘ ፡ ነበረ'” ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ገዳም፤” ፡ ያስተጋብእ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሕዝበ ፡ ኅሩየ ። ወዝ ንቱ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ቅዱስ" ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ። ወዘንተ ፡ ብሂሎ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ተሰ
After days, I said to him: "O my brother, the saint of God, behold, I see you loving effort and the fact of being alone. I too have the same love. Come, let us keep vigil this night and make prayer and orison. If God (wills it) in his good pleasure, he will reveal to us and show us what is useful to us and (what) he prepares for us." He said to me: "You have spoken well," and we stood for prayer until the end of the night. When the morning was near, the angel of God appeared to us and spoke to us thus: "God says to you: 'Dwell each alone in your place of habitation to accomplish the gift with which he has graced you to save the souls of many. As for you, John, dwell in this desert and I, I will send to you a numerous people,' says the Lord." Then he said to the holy father Bsoy: "As for you, move away a little, make yourself a dwelling and dwell alone; I, I will gather numerous people and will bring (them) to you, says the Lord. There will be a monastery in this desert and (the monks) will mention your name there. They will sanctify the name of their God as the beings from above sanctify (it), and it will be accomplished, (the word) which says: As for him who will dwell in this desert, God will gather for him a chosen people. This is what (God) says regarding the holy Abbä Macarius (Magäres)." Having said that, the angel vanished from our (sight).
ወረ ፡ እምኔነ” ። ወአሜሃ ፡ ተሌለየ ፡ እምኔየ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ። ወሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ጎረ ፡ ማኅደርየ ፡ ወገብረ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ማኅደረ! ፡ ምኅዋረ ፡ ፪ምዕራፍ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ህየ ፡ ወሀሎ ፡ እግ ዚአብሔር ፤ ምስሌሁ!?›' ። ወተለዐለ' ፡ ወበዝኀ ፡ ፍድፍድናሁ፤” ። ወተሰምዐ ፡ ዜናሁ! ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ አህጉር"! ፡ ወብዙኀን!” ፡ ይስእልዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያልብሶሙ ፡ ልብሰ ፤ ምንኩስና ። ውእቱኒ ፣ ይትቄበሎሙ ፡ ወይሬስዮሙ” ፡ ላእካነ ፡ ጽኑዓነ ፡ ወተባዕያነ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወልህቁ ፡፤ እምአርዳኢሁ ፡ አዕጹቅ"" ፡ ቡሩካን" ፡ መፍቀርያነ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፤ ወመፍቀርያነ ፡ አኀዊሆሙ” ። ወይፈትዉ ፡ ይፈጽሙ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ርእዩ ፡ ሥርዐቶ"" ፡ ወይገብሩ ፡ በከመ ፡ ገድሉ ፡ 'ወይትኤዘዙ ፡ ለቃላቲሁ! ፡ ቅዱሳት!” ። ወአነሂ ፡ እመጽእ ፡ ወትረ ፤ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወእከሥት ፡ ሎቱ ፡
Then, the holy Abbä Bsoy separated from me. He went near my dwelling and made himself a dwelling two miles away. He dwelt there and God was with him. His excellence rose and multiplied. His fame spread in all the regions and many (people) asked him to clothe them in the monastic garment. He welcomed them and made them solid and valiant servants for the King Jesus Christ. He raised among his disciples blessed branches loving God and loving their brothers. They desired to follow his conduct as they saw (it), they acted according to his (spiritual) combat and obeyed his holy words. I too came constantly to him and revealed my thought to him. Likewise, he too came to me and described his thought to me.
ሕሊናየ" ። ወከማሁ ፡ ውእቱኒ ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ ኀቤየ ፡ ወይዜንወኒ ፡ ሕሊናሁ ፡ ወእምድኅረ"! ፡ መዋዕል ፡ እንዘ ፡ ንቡር ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ በዐቱ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ሀቤሁ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፤ ክርስቶስ ፡፤ ወይቤሎ ፡ ሰላም ፡ ለከ ፡ 'ኦብሶይ ፡ ኅሩይየ'” ። "ወደንዝ ፡ ወተንሥአ! ፡ ወሰገደ ፡ ሎቱ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ እግዚእነ' ፣ ኢትሬኢኑ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ ልዑለ ። ' ወእምይእዜ ፡ ይበዝኅ ፡ ልዕልናሁ፤ ፡ ወይነብሩ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ እለ ፤ ይቄድሱ ፡ ለስምየ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ቅዱስ'" ፡ ብሶይ ፡ እግዚእየ ፣ በዝንቱ" ፡ ገዳም ፡ ትሴስዮሙኑ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ አማን ፡ እብ
After days, while he was sitting in his cell, our Lord Jesus Christ came to him and said to him: "Greetings to you, Bsoy my chosen one." (Bsoy) trembled, rose, and prostrated himself before him. Our Lord said to him: "Have you not seen this high desert? Henceforth, its altitude will be increased and monks who will sanctify my name will dwell there." The holy Bsoy said to him: "My Lord,

ለከ ፡፤ ሶበ ፡ እረክብ ፡፤ ፍቅረ ፤ በሀቤሆሙ ፡፤ ወይትጋደሉ ፡ በዐቂበ ፡ ትዕዛዝየ ፡ ወየአምኑ' ፡ ብየ ፤ ወይጸምዉ ፡ እምድኅረ' ፡ ንስቲት ፡ አነ ፡ አስተዳሉ ፤ ሎሙ ፣ በዝየ ፡ ሲሳዮሙ ፡ ወእጸግዎሙ!" ፡ ሠናያተ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ኦእግዚእየ ፡ ወታድኅኖሙኑ ፡ እመከራ ፡ ወእምቀትለ ፡ ጸላኢ ፡ ዘይመጽእ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ እግዚእነ"! ፡ ለእመ ፡ ዐቀቡ ፡ ትእዛዝየ ፡ አነ ፡ አድኅኖሙ ፡ እመከራት ፡ ወእምጸላኢ!” ። ወለኩሉ ፡ ዘየአምን" ፡ ብየ ፡ ወይትዌከል ፡ በስምየ" ፡ አወርሶ"" ፡ መንግሥተ ፡፤ ሰማያት ። ዘንተ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ወዐርገ ፤ ውስተ ፡ ሰማይ' ፡ በዐቢይ ፡ ስብሐት ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ አብዝኅ ፡ ተዓቅቦ ፡ ቅዱስ" ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይእቀብ ፡ ነፍሶ ። ወኮነ ፡ ይቴሐት" ፡ ወይትለአክ ፡ ወይትጌሠጽ'” ፡ ወይጸንዕ ፡ በተወክሎ ፡ አምላኩ ። ወመልአከ'” ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ 'ወትረ ፡ ይጌውጸ፤”' ። ወብሶይሰ ፡ ያፈቅር'” ፡ ብሕ ትና ። ወሰይጣንሂ!” ፡ ይተክል ፡ ሎቱ ፡ መሳግራተ”” ፡ ከመ ፡ ያስግሮ ፡ ወየኀይሎ”” ፡ ወኢክህለ ፡ መዊአ ፡ ጽንዑ ። 'ወአኀዘ ፡ ያመክሮ"” ፡ በፍቅረ ፡ 'አክብሮ ፡ ወለእመ ፡ በዝኒ''' ፡ ያደክሞ ፡ ወያወድቆ፤” ፡ እምጽንዑ ። ወሖረ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ፳መኩ›ንን ፤ ዘስሙ ፡ ሞወመል ፡ ወአስተርአዮ ፡ በአምሳለ ፡፣ መልአክ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ በገዳመ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ሀሎ፤” ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘኢያጠሪ ፡ ምንተኒ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወኩሎ ፡ ዘሰዓለ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይሁቦ ። ሖር ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወአቅርብ" ፡ ሎቱ ፡ አምኃ ፡ ወአስ ትየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይባርክ ፡ ላዕሌከ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ 'ውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ መኑ ፡ ስሙ ። ወይቤሎ ፣ ብሶይ ፡ ስሙ" ። ወተንሥአ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ 'ወጾረ ፡ ንዋየ' ፡ ወአልባሰ ፡ ብዙኅ ፣ ላዕለ ፡ አግማል ፡ "ወካልአ ፡ ክብራተኒ"ኾ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ገዳመ" ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘኩሎ ፡ አሚረ ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ አእምር ፡ እስመ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ናሁ ፡ ተከለ ፡ ለከ ፡ መሥገርተ ፡ በፍቅረ ፡ ክብራት! ፡ ወተቀብሎ ፡ አስትየ ፡ ወአምኃ። ናሁ ፡ "ሰይጣን ፡ ሖረ" ፡ ምድረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አምአበይት?” ፡ ወነገሮ ፡ ከመ' ፡ ያምጽእ ፡ ኀቤከ ፡ ክብራተ! ፡ ወንዋየ ፡ ብዙኀ ። ወተንሥአ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ንጹሕ ፡ ወወ ፅአ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያንሶሱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ። ወተራከቦ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡፤ ታአምርኑ ፡ ኦአረጋዊ'” ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ዘይብልዎ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ቅዱስ”! ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ምንተ ፡ ትፈቅድ ፡ እምኔሁ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ መኩንን ፡ እፈቅድ ፡ ከመ ፡ አሀቦ”” ፡ ዘንተ ፡ አምኃ ፡ ዘምስሌየ ፡ ወዘንተኒ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ከመ ፡ 'ይክፍል ፡ ለአ ኃው” ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩን ፡ በረከቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤትየ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በዝ ንቱ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ኢይፈቅድዎ ፡ ለንዋይ ፡ ወለእለሰ'” ፡ ይነብሩ ፡ ዝየ ፡ ኢይፈቅዱ ፡ ምን ተኒ ፡ እምዝንቱ ፡ አምኅ ፡ ዘምስሌከ ። ወለምንት ፡ አትዐብከ'”ዖ ፡ ነፍሰከ' ። ወእግዚአ ብሔር” ፡ ይትወከፍ ፡ አምኃከ ። ይእዜኒ ፡ ግባእ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አዕጸዳተ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ወክፍል” ፡
will you feed them in this desert?" Our Lord said to Abbä Bsoy: "In truth, I say to you: if I find love in them, that they live in the observance of my commandments, believe in me, and give themselves to effort, after a little time, I, I will prepare for them here their food and will grace them with goods." The holy Abbä Bsoy said to him: "O my Lord, and will you save them from the temptation and the aggression of the Enemy that will come upon them?" Our Lord said to him: "If they observe my commandments, I, I will save them from the temptations and the Enemy; and whoever will believe in me and will trust in my name, I will make him inherit the kingdom of heaven." Our Lord said this to the holy Abbä Bsoy, gave him the greeting, and ascended to heaven in great glory. After that, the holy Abbä Bsoy multiplied the observance (of the commandments) to guard his soul; he humbled himself, made himself a servant, mortified himself, and was firm in the trust in his God. The angel of God visited him constantly. Bsoy loved solitude. Satan set snares for him to take him in the trap and prevail over him, but he could not overcome his firmness. He went into the land of Egypt to a governor by the name of Mowamal and appeared to him in the guise of an angel; he said to him: "In the desert of Egypt, there is a man who possesses nothing, (he is) very holy and, everything he asks, God gives (it) to him. Go to him, offer him a present and a gift so that he may bless you." The man said to him: "What (is) his name?" (Satan) said to him: "Bsoy (is) his name." The man rose and loaded much money and clothing on camels, as well as other objects of value, and he went to the desert. God, who came every day to Abbä Bsoy, came and said to this one: "Know that behold, Satan has set a snare for you by means of the love of riches and the taste for gifts and presents. Behold, Satan has come into the land of Egypt to one of the greats (of it) and has told him to bring you objects of value and much money." The pure old man rose and went out to walk in the desert. The governor met him and said to him: "Do you know, old man, in this desert someone who is called Abbä Bsoy?" The holy Bsoy said to him: "What do you want from him?" The governor said to him: "I want to give him these presents which (are) with me as well as this money so that he may distribute (them) to (his) brothers so that his blessing may be in my house." The holy old man said to him: "In this desert, one has no need of money and those who dwell here have no need of any of these presents which (are) with you. Why have you tired yourself? God will receive your presents. Now, return to the cultivated regions of Egypt and distribute this money which (is) with you to the poor, to the

ዘንተ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ዘምስሌከ ፡ ለነዳያን!” ፡ ወለእጓለ” ፡ ማውታ ፡ ወለመበለታት ፡ ወእግዚ አብሔር ፡ ይባርክ ፡ ላእሌከ ፡ ወላዕለ ፡ ቤትከ ። ወገብአ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ውስተ ፡፤ ቤቱ ፡ ወገብረ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዘ"! ፡ ቅዱስ"” ። ወሶበ ፡ ገብአ ፡ ቅዱስ” ፡ ኀበ ፡ ማኅ ደሩ ፡ ተርእየ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ አዕየይከኒ ፡ ኦብሶይ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ኅሩየ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ብሶይ ፡፤ እምዘ ፤ ተፈጠርከ ፡ ከንቶ ፡ ትጻሙ ፡ ኦጸላኤ ፡ ሠናያት ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ አንሰ ፡ እብል”” ፡ እስመ ፡ አንተ ፡ ኢተፈጠርከ ፡ እምዘ ፡ ምን ትኒ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ያማስንከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወያርሕቀ ። ወሖረ ፡ ወኢገብአ ፡ ወኢተርእየ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ዳግመ ። ወሰበረ ፤ መሣግረ ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ ከሃሊ ፡ ወገብአ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ማኅደሩ ፤ በሰላም ። ወእግዚአብሔር ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ሀሎጆ። ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ግብሩ ፡
orphans and the widows, and God will bless you as well as your house." The man returned to his house and did as the saint had prescribed (to him). When the saint returned to his dwelling, Satan showed himself to him and said to him: "You have deceived me, Bsoy!" The chosen one of God, Bsoy, said to him: "Since you were created, you tire yourself in vain, enemy of the good." Satan said to him: "I, I say that you, you were not created after anything (?)." The holy old man said to him: "May God destroy you and remove you." (Satan) went away, did not return, and did not show himself to him again. The powerful one (= Bsoy?) had broken the trap that he had made and he returned to his dwelling in peace. God was with him in all his actions and saved him from all his enemies.

After that, (Bsoy) rose, entered the inner desert, and dwelt there alone. He strengthened his soul by a hard (spiritual) combat and he became strong like a place fortified by his excellence. God ordered that the intelligence of his spirit be taken into the Garden of delights so that he might see all the goods that are there, and he saw all the goods. He was given spiritual food and he ate; thanks to it, he mastered hunger and thirst. After that, he fasted every week. On Sundays, he took the body and blood of Christ and tasted nothing (else); he tested his body. Everything he did, it did not seem that he did (it) with the body. As our Lord says in the holy Gospel: He who will have dwelt in me and he who loves me, my Father will love him, and I too will love him and will show myself to him. The work that I do, he will do (it). Thanks to that, it will happen that he will not fall nor will he be divided. (God) said this regarding Abimelech ('Abamëlek) who slept seventy years (and) did not eat nor drink. Likewise, the seven children slept three hundred and seventy-five years (and) did not eat nor drink while their soul (had remained) united to their body. Thus (is it) written: The weak among men are the strong. Then, spiritual children gathered to the holy father and surrounded him like bees that surround a pot of honey. He watered them with spiritual food: the word of life that came out of his mouth. They sanctified God constantly. He taught them without respite to love to dwell in the trial in the desert, to be poor and to subject themselves to the vigil, to the service of God, to prayer and supplication night and day; not to cease nor stop nor omit nor neglect (to do it); to perform a

ይትወከፉ ፣ ነገደ ፤ ወይምሐሩ?” ፣ ነዳየ ። ወተፍጸሜቱ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ትእዛዝ” ፣ ከመ ፡ ይኅድጉ ፡ ፈቃዶሙ>23 ፣፡ ወይግበሩ ፣ ፈቃደ ፣ ቢጸሙ ። ወዝንቱ ፡ ወዘይመ ስሎ ፡ ይሜህሮሙ”” ፣፡ ቅዱሰ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ከመ ፡ በዝ ፡ አኃው ፡ እለ ፡ ተጋብኡ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ተርሕቀ ፡ እምኔ ሆሙ ፡ ባሕቲቱ ፡ ወሖረ" ፡ ገዳመ ፤፡ ውሳጢ፤ ። ወቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ በዐት ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ፻ ዓመተ ፡ ወኢርእየ ፡ ወኢአሐዱ ። ወኖኅ ፡ ሥርዐቱ ፡ ወተሰፍሐ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሥጋሁ። ወሀሎ”።፣ ቀዊሞ ፡ ይጴሊ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር” ፡ መዓልተ ፡ ወሌሊተ ፡ ወኢይነውም ፡ በእንተ ፡ ፍቅረ ፡ አምላኩ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተከለ” ፡ መትከለ ፡ ወአሰረ” ፡ ሥዕርቶ ፡ ወቆመ” ፡ በጸሎት ፡ ቅድመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወሶበ ፡ ፈጸመ ፡ ፻ ዓመተ ፡ በዝ ፡ ገድል ፡ መጽአ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ በዐት። ወሶበ ፡ ርእዮ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ደንገፀ ፤ ወፈርሀ፤ ወወድቀ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ምድር ። ወዐጽንዖ ፡ መድኀኒነ ፡ ወአንሥኦ ፡ በእዴሁ ፡ ወአቀሞ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ሰላምየ ፡ ላዕሌከ ፡ ኦጽሩይ” ፡ ብሶይ ። ናሁ ፡ ርኢኩ ፡ ግብረከ ፡ ወጸማ ፡ ዘጸመውከ፡ ። ወሀብኩከ ፡ ሀብተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዘያስተበቀዕ ፡ ወይስእል ፡ እምኔየ ፡ በስምከ ፡ ወያስተሰሪ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ኀጢአቱ ፡ 'እሰሪ ፡ ሎቴ” ፣፡ ወአዐቅቦ ። ወይቤ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኦእግዚእየ ፡ እስእል ፡ በኀቤከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ታጽንዐኒ፣ በስምከ ፡ ግሩም ፡ ወቅዱስ ፡ እስመ ፡ አንተ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ባሕቲትከ ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ እግዚአ ፡ ኩሉ ። ሐመምከ ፡ በእንተጆ፤ ፡ ፍጥረትከ”፤ ፡ ሞትከ ፡ ወተንሣእከ ፡ ወአድኀንከነ ። ወእፎ ፡፤ ይደልወነ ፡ ንሕነ ፡ ኀጥአን ፡ ከመ ፡ ንሕመም" ፡ በእንተ ፡ ስምከ ፣
manual work by which to feed oneself and welcome the stranger; and to be merciful toward the poor. The completion of this commandment (was) that they abandon their (own) will and do the will of their neighbor. This and what resembles it, the saint of God taught them. When he saw that the brothers who had gathered to him had multiplied, he moved away from them, alone, and went into the inner desert. He entered a cave, dwelt there three years, and saw no one. His rule of conduct developed and extended to his body. He prayed standing before God day and night and did not sleep for love of his God. After that, he planted a nail, attached his hair (to it), and stood in prayer before God. When he had accomplished three years in this (spiritual) combat, our Lord Jesus Christ came to him at this cave. When the old man saw him, he trembled, was frightened, and fell to the ground. Our Savior comforted him, raised him with his hand, and set him standing. He said to him: "My peace (is) upon you, Bsoy the pure. Behold, I have seen your actions and the trials that you have endured; I have given you a gift: whoever will supplicate and pray to me in your name, and will ask for grace for his sin, I will forgive him and will preserve him." The saint said: "My Lord, I pray you to make me strong by your terrible and holy name, for you alone (are) God, just Lord of all. You suffered for your creature, you died, you rose, and you saved us. How much must we, we sinners, suffer for your name and praise you!" Our Lord blessed him, gave him the greeting, and ascended to heaven.
ወናእኩትከ ። ወባረኮ ፡ "እግዚእነ ፡ ወወሀቦ ፡ ሰላመ” ፡ ወዐርገ” ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማይ” ። ወሀሎ ፡፳ አረጋዊዎ ፡ ወይትዌከል ፡ እግዚአብሔር” ፡ ወይፈርሆ” ። ወይነብር ፡ ቅሩበ” ፡ ማኅደሩ ፡ ለቅዱስ” ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ” ። ወብዙኀ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ የሐውር ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወይትመሀር ፡ እምኔሁ ። 'ወለዝንቱ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ረድአ ፡ ወበሕቁ ፡ ያፈቅሮ ፡ እስመ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ውእቱ ። ወወገረ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ፀላኤ ፡ ሠናይት ፡ ኅበ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እጐ ፣፤ ሕሊና ፡ እኩየ ፡ ወአውደቆ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኀጢአት ፡ ወሞተ ፡ በኀጢአቱ ፡ ወወደይዎ” ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሲኦል ። ወአኀዘ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ይጹሊ ፡ ወይስእል ፡ ኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፤ ሌሊተ ፡፤ ወመዓልተ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ መንፈሳዊ ። ወጾመ ፡ ጓ'መዓልተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ "የሐዝን ፤ ወይቴክዝኞ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ። ወመጽአ"" ፡ ቃል ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ይነብር” ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሲኦል ፡ እስከ ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ በደመና ። ወካዕበ ፡ ጾመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አረ ጋዊ ፤ ቋመዓልተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይስእል ፤ እምኅበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ያውፅኦ”"” ፡ እምሲኦል ። ወመጽአ ፡ ቃል ፡ በከመ ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ይንበር ፡ እስከ ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ በደመና ። ወሣልስመ ፡ ጾመ ፣ ሻመዓልተ ። ወይጹሊ ፡ ወይስእል ፡ ወያስ ተበቀዕ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እጐ ። ወመጽአ ፡ ቃል ፡ በከመ ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ይን በር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሲኦል ፡ እስከ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ እንተ ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ በደመና ።
There was an old man, he had trust in God and feared him. He dwelt near the dwelling of the holy Abbä Bsoy. Many times, he went to him and was instructed by him. This holy old man had a disciple; he loved him much because he (was) holy. Satan, the enemy of the good, threw into this brother a bad thought and made him fall into sin; he died in his sin and he was thrown into hell. The old man began to pray and implore God night and day for his spiritual son. He fasted forty days while being saddened and afflicted for him. A voice came and said: "He will remain in hell until our Lord comes on a cloud." Again, the old man fasted forty days while imploring God so that he might make him (= the disciple) come out of hell. The voice came as before and said to him: "Let him remain (in hell) until the Lord comes on a cloud." A third time, (the old man) fasted forty days; he prayed, implored, and supplicated for the brother. The voice came as before and said to him: "Let him remain in hell until the day when our Lord will come on a cloud."


[p. 310] {#po.49.p310}
ወተንሥአ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ወመጽአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ወሐተተ”" ፡ በእንተ ፡ ማኅደረ”'፤ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ” ። *! ወአእመረ ፡ ቅዱስ”” ፡ ብሶይ ፡ በመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወመጽአ” ፣ ኅበ”” ፡ አረጋዊ ፤ ወተራከቦ”” ፡ ወሰገደ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወተዋሀቡ ፡ ሰላመ ፣ በበይናቲሆሙ” ። ' ወይቤሎ ፡ ቅዱስ”” ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ኦአቡየ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በእንተ ፤ ምንት ፡ ጸመውከሊ” ፡ ወኀሠሥከ” ፡ ዘከማየ ፡ ንትጋ፤ ። ወዜነዎ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ምጽአቱ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ "ወበእንተ ፡ ዜና፡ ፣፡ ወልዱ ፡ ወበከመ ፡ ጸመወ” ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ ወዘከመ ፡ መጽአ ፡ቃል ፡ እምኅበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ጊዜ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ተንሥእ ፡ ንጴሊ ፡ ወዘፈቀደ ፡፣ እግዚአብሔር ፤ ይከውን ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ዓዲ ፡ እምሕል ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይርሕቅ ፡ እምኔከ ፡ እንዘ ፡ እሰግድ ፡ ቅድሜከ ፡ በብካይ ፡ ወክላሕ ፡ እስከ ፡ ትስእሎ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፤ ወይትመየጥ ፡ ኀቤየ ፡ ወይምሕረሂኒ ፡ ወያስተፈሥሕ ፡ ልብየ ፡ በውእቱ ፡ እጐ ፡ ወያጤይቀነቻ" ፡ ለእመ ፡ አውፅኦ ፡ እም ሲኦል ፤ ወእመ ፡ አልቦ ። ወተንሥአ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በጊዜሃ ፡ ወቦአ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ ውሳጢ ፡ ወጸ ለየ ፡ ወይቤ ፥ መኅር ፡ ተግባረከ ፡ ወአድኀኅን ፡ ገብረከ ፡ እምንዳቤ ። ወአስተርአዮ” ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኦፍቁርየ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ምንተ ፡ ትፈቅድ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እግዚ እየ ፡ እስእለከ ፡ በእንተ ፡ "ዝንቱ ፡ ወልደዖ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትስረይ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ኀጢ አቶ ፡ ወታውፅኦ”፤ ፡ እምሲኦል ። ወይቤሎ ፡ መድኅን ፡ ናሁ ፡ ወፅአ ፡ ቃል ፡ እምአፉየ፣፣ ከመ ፡ ይንበር ፤ ውስተ ፡ ሲኦል ፡ እስከ ፡ እመጽእ ፡ በደመና”። ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እግዚእየ ፡ እመሰ ፡ ፈቀድከ ፡ ትምጻእ ፡ ደመና ፡ ወንበር”" ፡ ላዕሌሃ ፡ 'ወአድኅን ፡ ዛተቻ ፣ ነፍሰ ። ' ወአዘዘ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ደመና ፡ ወመጽአት ፡ ወዐርገ ፡ ላዕሌሃ” ። ወአ ዘዘ ፡ "ከመ ፡ ትምጸእዖ ፡ ነፍሰ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ወመጽአት”' ። ወወሀቦ ፡ ለኅሩዩ ፡ ብሶይ ። ወነሥኦ ፡ 'ወወሰዶ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ብሶይ" ፡ ኀበ ፡ አረጋዊ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ”” ፣ አረጋዊ ፡ አንከረ ፡ ብዙኀ ፡ ወተስእሎ”” ፡ በእንተ ፡ ጸማ ፡ ዘረከቦ”! ፥ ውስተ ፡ ሲኦል ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ብዙኀ ፡ ተመንደብኩ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ኀጢአትየ ፡ ወፈድፋደሰ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘኢተአዘዝኩከ ፡ ኦአቡየ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሶበ ፡ 'አንተ ፡ ጸመውከ”” ፡ ወጸለይከ ፡ ኮነት ፡
The old man rose, came into the desert, and inquired about the dwelling of the holy Abbä Bsoy. The holy Bsoy knew (it) by the Holy Spirit. He came toward the old man, met him, and prostrated himself before him; they gave each other the greeting. The holy Bsoy said to him: "My holy father, regarding what have you tired yourself and have you sought someone insufficient (?) like me?" The old man gave him an account regarding his coming to him and regarding the story of his (spiritual) son; (he told him) how he had labored for him and how a voice had come from God three times. Abbä Bsoy said to him: "Rise, we will pray and what God wants will happen." The old man said to him again: "I swear that I will not move away from you, prostrated before you in tears and groans, until you implore God and he turns toward me, has pity on me, rejoices my heart with this brother, and indicates to us if he has brought him out of hell or not." The saint rose immediately, entered the inner desert, prayed, and said: "Have pity on your creature and save your servant from misfortune." Christ appeared to him and said to him: "My beloved Bsoy, what do you want?" The saint said to him: "My Lord, I implore you for this (spiritual) son of the old man so that you may forgive him his sin and make him come out of hell." The Savior said to him: "Behold, a word has come out of my mouth (saying) that he would remain in hell until I came on a cloud." The saint said to him: "My Lord, if you want (it), let a cloud come, sit on it, and save this soul." Our Lord commanded a cloud: it came and he mounted on it. He commanded that the soul of the (old man's spiritual) son come and it came. He gave him (= the monk) to his chosen one Bsoy. The holy Bsoy took him and brought him to the old man. When the old man saw him, he was very astonished and questioned him about the trials that had touched him in hell. (The monk) said to him: "I was much tormented because of my sin and especially because of what I did not obey you, my father. Then, when you labored and prayed, rest came to me and I was transferred to a place of rest."
ሊተ ፡ 'ፅረፍት ፤ ወአግዐዙኒ ፡ ኅበ ፡ መካነ” ፡ ዕረፍት ። ወተፈሥሐ ፡ ልበ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ውእተ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ እስመ ፡ ጸገውከኒ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ ኦአባ ፡ ቅዱስ ። እስመ” ፡ መሐርኮ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ወአዕረፎ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወተመይጠ ፡ ኀቤየ ። 'ወበጸሎትከ ፡ መሐሮ” ፡ ወሰረየ ፡ ሎቱ” ፡ በከመ ፡ ጽሑፍ ፡ ዘይብል ፡ እስመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ያጸንዖሙ ፡ ለቅዱሳኒሁ ፡ ወይሰምዕ ፡ ስእለቶሙ ። ወይእዜኒ ፡ ኦአቡየ ፡ እፈቅድ” ፡ ከመ ፡ ትንግ ረኒ ፡ ምንተ ፡ "ትገብር ፡ እስከነ” ፡ ጸገወከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ሀብተ ፡ ዓቢየ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ለአረጋዊ ፡ ስረይ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ኦአቡየ ። ዝንቱሰ” ፡ ፈቃደ ፡ እግ
The heart of the old man rejoiced at that moment, he went to Abbä Bsoy and said to him: "You have graced me with a great grace, holy Abbä: you have had pity on this (my) (spiritual) son, God has given him rest and he has returned to me. By your prayer, (God) has had pity on him and has forgiven him, as (are) written (the words) which say: God strengthens his saints and listens to their prayer. Now, my father, I want you to tell me what you did so well that God has graced you with this great gift." Abbä Bsoy said to the old man: "Forgive me, my father. This (is) the will of God: from the beginning, he has

ዚአብሔር ። እምቀዳሚ” ፡ አቅደመ" ፡ ምሕረቶ” ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሰብእ ። ወበየውሐቱ” ፡ ወሀበ ፡ ምሕረቶ ፡ 'ለውእቱ ፡ ወልድ”" ። አኮ ፡ በእንቲአየ ፡ አነ ፡ ምኑን”' ፡ ወአኮ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ግብርየ ፡ ወኢበእንተ ፡ ጸሎትየ ፡ ዘኮነ” ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ነገር ፡ ኢኮነ ፡ ከማሁ” ። አላ" ፡ በእንቲአከ ፡ ዘጾምከ ፡ 'ዳ ዕለተ” ፡ ዞ ጊዜ ፡ ወተከልከ ፡ ነፍሰከ ፡ ለመዊት”፤፣ በእንተ ፡ ፍቅረ ፡ እግዚአብሔር”= ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮንከ ፡ ሕሙመ ፡ በልብከ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እጐ ፡ ወመጠወከ ፡ ነፍሰከ ፡ ህየንቴሁ ፡ ወሀቦ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለከ ። ወአ ልቦ ፡ ፍቅረ ፡ ዘየዓቢ ፡ "ከመ ፡ ዘይሜጡ” ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ህየንተ ፡ ቢጹ ። ወዘንተ ፡ ሀብተ ፡ ነሣእከ ፡ እምኀበ ፤ ክርስቶስ ። ወከማሁ?” ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ፳እምነ ፡ አበው ፡ በእ ንተ ፡ ግብሩ ፡ "ወኩሉ ፡ ፳ በከመ ፡ ጸጋሁ?" ። ወእምዝ ፡ አእኮቱ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወነበሩ፣ ወበልዑ ፡ ኅብስተ ፡ኅቡረ ። ወገብአ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ለማኅደሩ ፡ በሰላም ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ተከለ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ ለገድል ። ወየአውድ”” ፡ ገዳመ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዘይትረከብ”። ፡ በህየ ፡ ዘነፍስ ። ወየኀብእ ፡ ግብሮ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢያእምሮ ፡ መኑሂ”” ። ወያተሉ ፡ ጸመ ፡ ወጸሎተ ። ወሶበ ፡ ይበልዕ ፡ ኢይጥዕም ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ኅብስት ፡ ጽዩእ ፡ ወይሰቲ ፡ ማየ ፡ ፍሉሐ ። ወአስተርአዮ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ወተናገሮ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ፃእ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አኃው ፡ እለ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ከመ ፡ ትኩኖሙ ፡ አበ ፡ ወትምሀሮሙ ፡ ቀኖና ፣ ወሥርዐተ ፡ ምንኩስና ፡ በዘቦቱ ፡ ያጠርዩ ፡ ሕይወተ፤ ። ወሰአሎ ፡ አብ ፡ ለአምላኩ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኦእግዚእየ ፡ አልብየ ፡ ጽንዐ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ መልእክት ፡ ወአነ ፣ እፈርህ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይጥፋእ ፡ ግብርየ ፡ ዘጻመውኩ ፡ በእንተ ፡ 'ዐቢይ ፡ ወቅዱስ ፡ ስምከ?” ። ወይቤሎ ፡፤ እግዚእነ ፡ አነ ፡ እሁበከ ፡ አስበ ፡ ጻማከ ፡ በኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ቅድስት” ፡ ሰማያዊት ፡ 'ወአዐቢ ፡ ስመከ?” ። ብፁዕ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘየዐቅብ ፡ ትእዛዛተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወጹነወ ፡ መዐዛ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ወመ ልአ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ገዳመ ። 'ወመጽኡ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ አኃው ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ መካናት ፡ ወኦድዎ፤ ፡ ወያነክሩ”' ፡ ብዙኃ ፤ ትሕትናሁ ። ወይሜህሮሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ፈሪሀ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወይናዝዞሙ ፡ ወይምዕዶሙ ፡ ወያሜንዮሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ በዘይደሉ ።
put first his pity for men and, in his meekness, he has given his pity to this (your) (spiritual) son; it is not for me, I the despicable, nor for my actions, nor for my prayer that this thing happened, (it) did not happen thus; but it is for you who fasted forty days three times and exposed yourself to death for the love of God. When you suffered in your heart for the brother and (that) you offered your life in his place, God gave him to you. There is no love that is greater than (that of) the man who gives his life in the place of his neighbor. It is this gift that you have received from Christ. Likewise (is it) for each of the fathers for his actions and (for) each according to the grace (which) is imparted to him." After that, they praised God, sat down, and ate bread together; and each of them returned to his dwelling in peace. After that, the old man (= Bsoy) established himself in the (spiritual) combat; he traversed the desert (going) to the men who were there. He hid his actions so that no one would know them; he devoted himself to fasting and prayer. When he ate, he took only moldy bread and he drank brackish water. Our Lord appeared to him, spoke with him, and said to him: "Go out toward the brothers who are in the desert to be a father for them and to teach them the rule and the monastic conduct with which they gain (eternal) life." The father implored his God and said to him: "O my Lord, I do not have (enough) strength for this office and I fear that the actions that I have done with effort for your great and holy name will be reduced to nothing." Our Lord said to him: "I will give you the reward of your efforts in the holy heavenly Jerusalem and I will make your name great. Happy the man who keeps the commandments of God!" The name of our holy father Bsoy exhaled a pleasant perfume and filled the whole desert with it. Brothers came to him from everywhere and surrounded him; they were astonished at his great humility. He taught them all to fear God; he encouraged them, exhorted them, and made them all better, according to what is fitting.
ወእምድኅረ ፡ መዋፅል ፡ አነ ፡ ኅጹጽ” ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ሖርኩ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ አእምር ፡ ህላዌሁ” ። ወሶበ ፡ በጻሕኩ ፡ አንቀጸ ፡ በዐቱ ፡ ሰማዕኩ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘይትናገር ፡ ምስለ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ወይበኪ ። ወጐድጐድኩ” ፡ ወአርኀወ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ወቦእኩ ፣ ወጸለይነ ፡ ኅቡረ ፡ ወነበርነ ። ወእቤሉ” ፡ አቡነ ፡ መኑ ፡ ዘይትናገር”" ፡ ይእዜኒ ፡ ዘሰማዕኩ ። ወይቤለኒ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ 'እምድኅረ ፡ ሰማዕከሰ” ፣ እነግረከ” ። ቄስጠን ጢኖስ ፤ ውእቱ" ፥ ንጉሠ ፡ ሮሜ ፡፣ መጽአ ፡ ኅቤየ ፡ እምሰማይ ፡ 'እንዘ ፡ ይበኪ ፡ ወሕዙን ፡ ልቡ ። ወይብል ፡ ኢያእመርኩ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ክብራት ፡ ተውህበ”” ፡
After days, I, John the Little, went to him to know how he was. When I reached the door of his cell, I heard the voice of a man who was speaking with Abbä Bsoy and weeping. I knocked, (Bsoy) opened for me, I entered, we prayed together, and we sat down. I said to him: "Our father, who is he who was speaking just now (and) whom I heard?" The saint said to me: "After you heard, I am going to tell you: it is Constantine (Quastantinos), the king of Rome (Romë); he came from heaven to me while


[p. 314] {#po.49.p314}
ለመነኮሳት ፡ በእንተ ፣ ንዴቶሙ ፡ ዘበምድር ፡ ወንግደቶሙ ፡ በእንተ ፡ እግዚእነ ። ወሶበ ፡ አእመርኩ ፡ እንዘ” ፡ ሀሎኩ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡ እምኅደጉ ፡ መንግሥተ ፡ ኀላፌ ፡ ወእምኮንኩ ፡መነኮሰ ። ወእቤሎ ፡ ምንተ ፡ ወሀቡከ”፤ ፡ ክብረ ፡ በሰማይ ፡ ኦንጉሥ”።። ወይቤለኒ ፡ እወ ፡ ተውህበ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ወኢይበጽሕ ፡ ለክብረ ፡ መነኮሳት” ። አነ ፡ ርኢኩ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ፀአቶሙ ፡ እምሥጋሆሙ ። ይሠርጽ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ አክናፈ ፤ ወይሠርሩ ፡ ወይትለዐሉ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ አጽዋነ ፤ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ሰማያዊት ፡ ወይ በውኡ ። ወአልቦ ፡ ዘይከልኦሙ ፡ እምነ ፡ ዐጽውንት” ፡ እለ ፡ ሀለዉ” ፡ ህየ ። ወእቤሎ ፡ ይደሉኑ ፡ በኀቤከ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ፍትሕ ። ወአንተሰ ፡ ብከ ፡ ብእሲተ ፡ ወውሉደ ፡ ወአግብርተ ፤ ወመዛግብተ ፡ ወመንበረ ፡ ልዑለ ። ወያስተዓሪከነ” ፡ ምስለ ፡ እለ ፡ ኀደጉ ፡ 'አብያተ ፡ ወንዋየ ። ወኮኑ ፡ ዘአልቦሙ ፡ ምንተኒ ፡ ነዳያነ ፡ ወምስኪናነ ፡ ግዱፋነ ፡ ወምኑናነ ፡ 'ጸዋምያነ ፤ ወትጉሃነ?” ። ይርኅቡ ፡ ወይጸምዑ ፡ ወይትገደፉ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ገጸ ፡ ምድር” ። ከመዝ ፡ እቤሎ ። ወሶበ ፡ አንተ ፡ ጐድጐድከ”” ፡ ተሰወረ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰማዕኩ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ እምኅበ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እቤ” ፡ ይትባረክ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘከሠተ ፡ ለቅዱሳኒሁ ፡ ተአምራቲሁ ።
weeping, his heart afflicted. He said: 'I did not know that this honor was given to the monks for their poverty which (is) on earth and for their wandering life for our Lord. If I (had known it while I was on earth, I would have abandoned a transitory royalty and I would have become a monk.'" I said to him: "What glory have they given you in heaven, O king?" He said to me: "Certainly, there was (one) given to me, but it does not reach the glory of the monks. I saw people among them at the moment of their exit from their body: wings grew on them, they flew away and rose above the ramparts of the heavenly Jerusalem and entered. No one moved them away from the citadels (?) that are there." I said to him: "Is such a judgment fitting for you: you, you had a wife, children, servants, treasures, and a high throne? (God) should he make you equal to those who have abandoned houses and goods? They were (people) who have nothing, poor and indigent, rejected and despised, fasters and vigilers. They were hungry and thirsty, they were rejected from the earth." Thus I said to him. "When you knocked, he disappeared." When I heard this from the holy father, I said: "Blessed be God who reveals his miracles to his saints!"
ወሀሎ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ባሕታዊ ፡ በሀገር ፡ ዘይብልዋ ፡ በስዊ ፡ በተባይስ ። አሥገረ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ መሥገርተ ፡ ወወደየ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልቡ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይበል ፡ አልቦ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ”" ። ወአግሀደ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ በነገሩ ፡ ወበትምህርቱ” ። ወእግዚአብሔር ፡ መሐሪ ፡ ወመስተሣህል ፡ ኢፈቀደ ፡ ከመ'” ፡ ያማስን ፡ ግብሮ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ አረጋዊ ። ወከሠተ ፡ ዘንተ?” ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ በራእይ ፡ ተንሥእ ፡ ወሖር ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ባስዊ!?! ፡ በተባይስ?”= ፡ ወትረክብ”” ፡ በህየ ፡ ባሕታዌ” ፡ ዘያመስል” ፡ ወያግህድ ፡ በብሂሎቱ ፡ አልቦ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወለአባ””" ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ሠረቀ” ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልቡ ፡ ጥበበ?” ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ 'ወአየነ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ያወሥኦ ፡ ለውእቱ” ፡ ባሕታዊ ። ወአኀዘ ፡ ይግበር ፡ አስፈሪዳተ ፡ ወገብረ ፡ ለለአሐቲ ፡ አእ ዛነ ፡ ፻ በአምሳለ ፡ ሥሉስ፤ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ 'ወነጧኦ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ኀበ፡ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ባሕታዊ ፡ ወቦአ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ በአምሳለ ፡ እንግዳ ። ወአኀዘ” ፡ ይትለአክ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ በከመ” ፡ ሖረ ፡ ባቲ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ወኅሩይ ፡ ገባሬ ፡ ተአምር ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ መንፈሳዊ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ። ውእቱ ፡ዘኮነ ፡ ሰዳዴ ፡ አጋንንት ። ውእቱ ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ ተአምራተ ፡ ወመንክራተ ፡ ዐበይተ ። ውእቱ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ መንጽሔ ፡ ነፍሳት ፡ ወመራሔ ፡ ስሑታን ። “ወቅዱስ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ጸሐፈ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልቡ ። ወአኀዘ ፡ ይሖር ፡ በከመ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወተመሰሎ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ግብር ፡ ዘይገብር' ። ወሖረ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይርከቦ ፡
There was a solitary man in the city that is called Baswi in Thebaid (Tabäyes). Satan set a trap for him and put into his heart (the idea) of saying that there is no Holy Spirit but (only) the Father and the Son, and he made this public by his speech and his teaching. God the merciful who shows mercy did not want to destroy the (beautiful) actions of this old man; he revealed this to the holy Abbä Bsoy and said to him in a vision: "Rise and go to the city of Bäswi in Thebaid, you will meet there a solitary who thinks and makes public by his remarks (that) there is no Holy Spirit." As for Abbä Bsoy, the wisdom of God shone in his heart and he discerned how to answer this solitary. He undertook to make baskets, made for each (of them) three handles in the likeness of the holy Trinity and, having taken (them), went toward the solitary and entered to him in the guise of a stranger. The holy Abbä Bsoy began to act as a servant, according to how our great and chosen father, the wonderworker, the spiritual saint Abbä Macarius had walked in this (way), he who was a hunter of demons, he who performed great miracles and wonders, he who was a purifier of souls and a guide of the lost. [The saint Bsoy wrote all this in his heart. He began to walk as that one had walked and made himself similar to him in all the actions that he accomplished. And he set out to meet


[p. 316] {#po.49.p316}
ለውእቱ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትክሎ ፡ ወያቅሞ ፡ ወያንሥኦ ፡ እምድቀቱ ፡ ወከመ ፡ ያግብኦ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ርትዕ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወለእለ ፡ ይተልዉ ፡ በስሕተቱ ። ወቤተ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ በይእቲ ፡ ሌሊት ። ወሶበ ፡ ጸብሐ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ኅበ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ባሕታዊ ፡ ከመ ፤ ይትባረኩ ፡ እምኔሁ ። "ወሶቤሃ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ አውፅአ” ፡ አስፈሪዳተ ። 'ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፤ ውእቱ ፡ ባሕታዊ ፡ ለለአሐዱ ፡ ፻ አእዛነ" ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ምንትኑ፤ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አቡነ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ እሉ ፡ ግብረ ፡ እዴየ ፡ ከመ ፡ እሚጦሙ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ባሕታዊ ፡ ለምንት ፡ ዘንተ ፡ አስፈሪዳተ ፡ በበ አእዛን ። 'ወኩሉሰ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ በበ ፪ ይገብሩ”፡ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ 'እንዘ ፡ ይሰምዑ ፡ ሰብእ” ፡ አለ ፡ ህየ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ ጉቡአን ። ወሊተሰ ፡ ሥሉስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፤ አሐዱ ፡ አም ላክ ፤ ወሎቱ ፡ እሰግድ ፡ ወበእንተዝ ፡ እገብር ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ግብረ ፡ እደዊየ ፡ አምሳለ ፡ ሥላሴ ፡ ቅድስት ። ወይቤልዎ ፡ እሙንቱ” ፡ ጉቡአን ፡ ኦአቡነ ፡ ቦኑ” ፡ ምስለ ‹ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወፈትሐ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ቦመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አምላክየ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አብ" ፣ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ቀዳማዊ ፡ ዘእምቅድመ ፣
the old man in order to strengthen him (?), to set him (back) up, to raise him from his fall, and in order to bring him back into the straight (path), him and those who followed him in his error. He slept at his (= the old man's) place that night. When it was morning, many (people) came to the solitary to be blessed by him. Then Abbä Bsoy brought out (his) baskets. When the solitary saw that each had three handles, he said to him: "What (is) this, our father?" The holy Abbä Bsoy said to him: "They (are) the work of my hand, (I made them) to sell (them)." The solitary said to him: "Why (have you made) these baskets with three handles each: everyone makes (them) with two each?" The holy Abbä Bsoy said to him, the people who were there listening: "For me, the holy Trinity (is) one God and I worship it; because of that, I do the work of my hands thus in the likeness of the holy Trinity." Those who were gathered said to him: "Our father, is there with the Father and the Son the Holy Spirit?" Our father the saint Bsoy decided and said: "There is the Holy Spirit, my God, with the Father; and the Holy Spirit (is) first before the centuries, he who is not created nor made."
ዓለምዎ ፡ ዘኢኮነ ፡ 'ፍጡረ ፡ ወግቡረ”” ። "ወተመይጠ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ባሕታዊ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ታአምርኑ ፡ እመጸሕፍት ፡ ቅዱሳት ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ባሕታዊ ፡ እወ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኢሰማ ፅከኑ“፤ ፡ ዘይቤ ፣ ሙሴ ፡ በመጽሐፈ ፡ ፍጥረት ። በቀዳሚ ፡ ገብረ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሰማየ ፡ ወምድርሰ ፡ ሀለወት ፡ እምትካት ፡ ወኢታስተርኢ ፡ ወጽልመት ፡ መልዕልቴሃ ፡ ወመንፈሰ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይጹልል ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ ማይ ። ወካዕበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ኢሳይያስ ፡ ነቢይ ፤ በእንተ ፡ ወልደይ”= ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። መንፈሰ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ላዕሌየ ፡ ዘበእንቲአሁ ፡ ቀብአኒ ። ወእዜንዎሙ ፡ ለነዳያን ፡ ፈነወኒ ። ወዳዊትኒ"" ፡ ይቤ ፡ ወትፌኑ ፤ መንፈሰከ ፡ ወይትፈጠሩ ፡ ወትሔድስ ፡ ገጻ ፡ ለምድር ። ወሕዝቅኤልኒ” ፡ ይቤ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ይቤለኒ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ጸውእ ፡ ወበል ፡ በመንፈስ ፡ ወይንፍኀ“ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ እሉ ፡ አዕጽምት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትነሥኡ ፡ ሕያዋን ። ወዮሐንስ ፡ መጥምቅ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ኦደ ፡ ይስብክ ፡ ለወልደ" ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይቤ ፡ አነ ፡ ርኢኩ ፡ መንፈሰ ፡ ቅዱሰ" ፣ ወነበረ ፡ ዲቤሁ ። ወካዕበ ፡ 'ይቤ ፡ መምህርነ ፣ ጳውሎስ"” ፡ 'አልቦ ፡ ዘይነብብ ፡ በመንፈሰ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወይብል ፡ ውጉዝ ፡ ኢየሱስ' ። 'ወአልቦ ፡ ዘይክል ፡ ብሂለ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ዘመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ላዕሌሁ”! ። ወይቤ ፡ ዳንኤል ፡ መስምር ፡ ብእሴ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ። አነ ፡ ርኢኩ ፡ መናብርተ ፡ ንቡረ ። ወነበረ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሠለስቱ ፡ አቃኒምያ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ይነብብ ። ወዝንቱ ፡ ተአምር ፡ ለዋሕድ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡፤ በሥላሴ ፡ ቅድስት ። ወእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ አዘዞሙ ፡ ለሐዋርያቲሁ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፣ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ሖሩ'" ፡ ወጥምቁ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ አሕዛበ ፡ በስመ ፡
Then he turned toward the solitary and said to him: "Do you know the holy Scriptures?" The solitary said to him: "Yes." Our holy father said to him: "Have you not heard what Moses says in the book of Genesis: In the beginning, God created the heaven, and the earth was at the origin and it did not appear and darkness (was) above it, and the spirit of God hovered above the water. Isaiah the prophet also says regarding the Son of God: The spirit of God (is) upon me, he for whom (God) has anointed me. To announce the good news to the poor, he has sent me. David also says: You send your spirit: they are created and you renew the face of the earth. Ezekiel also says: Thus God said to me: Call and speak by the spirit and let the winds blow upon these bones so that they may rise alive. John the Baptist, when he traversed (the country) to announce the Son of God, said: I saw the Holy Spirit and it dwelt upon him. Our master Paul also says: There is no one who speaks by the spirit of God and says: Anathema (be) Jesus, and there is no one who can say 'Lord Jesus Christ', if it is not he upon whom (is) the Holy Spirit. Daniel, the man pleasing to God, says: I saw seats placed and the ancient of days sat on the three (seats) and spoke. This sign (is) for the unique God in a holy Trinity. Our Lord Christ commanded his holy apostles and said to them: Go and baptize all nations in the name of the Father, of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit."


[p. 318] {#po.49.p318}
አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፤ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወይእዜኒ ፡ ኢየአክለክሙነ”" ፡ 'ዝንቱ ፡ ስምዓት” ፡ ዘአምጻእነ ፡ ለክሙ ፡ እመጻሕፍት ፡ ቅዱሳት ፡ እለ ፡ ይሰብኩ ፡ በእንተ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ አምላክየ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ግእዘት ፡ ወኢቡዓዴ”" ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዑ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ እምአፈ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ብሶይ ። ዘንተ' ፤ ስምዓተ ፣ እመጻሕፍት ፡ ቅዱሳት ። አምነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ባሕታዊ ፡ ወእለሂ ፡ ምስሌሁ ። ወነቅሀ ፡ ወረብኅ' ፡ ብዙኀ ፡ ወተመይጠ ፤ ተመይቦ ፡ ርቱዐ ፡ ወአምነ ፡ በአብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡፤ ቅዱስ ። ወአእመረ ፡ ሃይማኖተ ፡ ርትዕተ ፤ 'ወአእኩተ ፡ እግዚአብ ሔር ። ወአቡነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ተመይጠ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካኑ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሴብሖ” ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ወእንዘ” ፡ የሐውር ፡ ለገዳም ፡ በረቀ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ አዕይንቲሁ ፡ ብር ሃን ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ ወርእየ ፡ ገዳመ ፣፤ ኩሎ"! ፡ መላእክተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአንከረ ፡ ብዙኀ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ምንትኑዝ ። ወአውሥኦ ፡ እመላእክት ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ንሕነ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ እለ ፤ ነዐቅብ ፤ ቅዱሳነ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ገዳም ። ወአንከረ”= ፣ ወሰብሖ ፡
Now, does this testimony that we have produced for you from the holy Scriptures which announce the Holy Spirit who is my God without dispute or diversity not suffice for you?" When they heard this discourse from the mouth of our holy father Bsoy - this testimony of the holy Scriptures -, the solitary believed as well as those who (were) with him; he awoke (from his error), had great profit, converted with a straight conversion, and believed in the Father, in the Son, and in the Holy Spirit. He knew the straight faith and gave thanks to God. Our holy father Bsoy returned to his dwelling while glorifying God. While he was going toward the desert, a great light burst before his eyes; he saw the desert (filled?) with all the angels of God. He was very astonished and said: "What (is) this?" One of the angels answered him and said to him: "We are those who guard the saints in this desert." (Bsoy) was astonished and glorified God who thinks of his servants at all times.
ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘይሔሊ ፡ በእንተ ፡ አግብርቲሁ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ጊዜ ። "ወሀሎ ፤ ይነብር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፤ ባሕታዊ ፡ ወይትጌበር ፡ ወይጸንዕ ፡ በሥርዐት ፤ እዙዝ ። ወወፅአ ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ መካን ። ወሰምዐ ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ አባ ፡ በንሙን ፡ ታሞይትኩል ፡ ወፈተወ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይርአዮ ፡ ወይትረከቦ ፡ ወይርአይ ፡ ፍናዊሁ ፡ ወይትወከፍ ፡ በረከቶ”' ። ወተንሥአ ፡ ወመጽአ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ 'ባውሪ ፡ እምጣመዊ ፡ እስመ” ፡ ሰምዖ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይዜኑ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሥርዐተ ፡ ቅዱስ” ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ። እስመ ፡ ባውሪ ፡ ወትረ ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ ወያአምር ፡ ሥርዐቶ። ወይቤ ፤ አባ ፡ በንሙን ፡ ለአባ ፣ ባውሪ ፡ ኢይትከሀለከነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትግበር ፡፣ ፍቅረ ፡ ምስሌየ ፡ ወትንሥአኒ ፡ ምስሌከ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቅዱሰ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አቡነ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትክፍለኒ”" ፡ በረከቱ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አባ ፡ ባውሪ ፡ እፈርህ ፣፡ ወሲ ዶተከ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ እስመ ፡ ንኡስ ፡ አንተ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ኅድገኒ ፡ እግዚእየ ፡ ከመ ፡ እሖር ፡ ምስሌከ ፡ እስከ ፡ አንቀጸ ፡ መካን" ፡ ወእንበር ፡ 'ኀበ ፡ አንቀጹ ፡ እስከ ፡ ትበውእ ፡ አንተ ፡ ወትኤምኖ ፡ ወትወፅእ ፡ ኀቤየ ። ወአነኒ ፡ እኤምኅ ፡ ኪያከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትብጽሐኒ ፡ በረከቱ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አባ ፡ ባውሪ ፡ ግበር ። ወሖሩ ፡ ክልኤሆሙ ፡ኅቡረ ፡ ወወረዱ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ ግብጽ ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ መካነ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ቦአ ፡ አባ ፡ ባውሪ‹ ፡ ወአምኖፕ ፡ ወተአምተ ፡ ወጸለዩ ፡ ወነበሩ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ በእ ንተ ፡ምንት ፡ግብር ፡ ዘኢቦአ ፡ ዘመጽአ ፡ ምስሌከ ፡ ወኀደጎ ፡ ገዳመ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አባ ፡ ባውሪ፤ ፡ ስረይ ፡ ሊተ ፡፤ ኦአቡየ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እስመ ፡ እቤ ፡፤ ንኡስ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወኢይትራከብ ፡ ከመ ፡ አብ ፡ ኀቤከ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኢትበል ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ "ሕፃን ፡ ንኡስ”= ፣ ወይቤ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ለዘከመ ፡ እሉ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ መንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። አማን ፡ እብለከ ፡ እስመ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ጽውዓን ፡ ይበውኡ ፡ 'ለገነተ ፡ ትፍሥሕት ፡ ላዕለ ፡ እደዊሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ሕፃን ። እስመ ፡ ጽንዐ ፡ እግዚአብሔር” ፡
He dwelt in the desert alone, worked, and was firm in a severe discipline. His fame spread in every place. Abbä Banmun (of) Tamoytekul heard speak of him and desired to see him, to meet with him, to see his way of acting, and to receive his blessing. He rose and came to the holy Paul (Bäwri) of Tammah (Tämawi), for he had heard him speak of the conduct of the holy Abbä Bsoy: in effect, Paul came constantly (to Bsoy) and knew his conduct. Abbä Banmun said to Abbä Paul: "Would it not be possible for you to show me friendship and to take me with you (while going) to the saint of God, our father Abbä Bsoy, so that his blessing may come to me as a share?" Abbä Paul said to him: "I fear to bring you to him, for you are young." (Banmun) said to him: "Let me, my lord, go with you to the door of (his) dwelling and stay at his door until you enter, you, embrace him, and come out to me. Then I too will embrace you so that his blessing may come upon me." Abbä Paul said to him: "Do (so)." They both went together and descended into the desert of Egypt. When they reached the dwelling of Abbä Bsoy, Abbä Paul entered and embraced him; they embraced each other, prayed, and sat down. Abbä Bsoy said to him: "For what reason did he who came with you not enter and have you left him in the desert?" Abbä Paul said to him: "Forgive me, my holy father; I said: He is small and he (cannot) present himself as a father to you." The holy father said to him: "Do not say: This one (is) a small child, while our Lord has said: To him who (is) like them is the kingdom of heaven. In truth, I say to you: many called will enter into the Garden of delights by the hands of this small child, for the

strength of God (is) with him; he is not small but great and he is powerful." He ordered Abbä Paul to introduce him (F Banmun) to him, this one made him come and (Bsoy) embraced him and blessed him. He prayed over them, then they went out from him and returned to their dwellings in peace while glorifying God.
' ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ጾመ ፡ አባ ፣ ብሶይ" ፡ ፳ወ8ዕለተ ። ወመጽአ" ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኦኅሩይየ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ አትብዐከ”፤ ፡ ነፍሰከ ፡ ብዙኃ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ እግዚእየ ፡ ላዕሌከ ፤ ቱክልትየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ታጽንዓኒ” ፡ ወበእንተዝ ፡ አልብየ ፡ ድካመ ፡ ወኢምንተኒ ። ወሰሐቦ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ወወሰዶ ፡ ኅበ ፡ "እጐ ፡ ድኩም ፡ ዘጾመ ፡ ክልኤ ፡ ዕለተ ። ወርእዮ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ውዱቀ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወይትገነጳል”' ፡ ለፌ ፡ ወለፌ ፡ ወየኀሥሥ ፡ ሞረ ፡ ወነፋሰ ፡ እምሕማመ ፡ ጾም ። ወዘእንበለ ፡ ይፈጽም ፡ ክልኤ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ያፈቅር ፡ ዕረፍተ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ እግዚእየ ፡ ምንትኑ ፡ ነገሩ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፣ እጉ ። ወይቤሎ” ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ እስመ ፡ ጾመ ፡ ሌሊተ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ 'እስፍንተ ፡ ዕለታተ ፡ እምዘጾመ ፡ እስከ ፡ ወድቀ ፡ ወተገድፈ፤ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ እምዘ ፡ ፈጠርክዎ"” ፡ ኢያኅጣእክዎ ፡ ምሳሐ ፡ አሐተ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ ወናሁ ፡ ፈነውኩ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ረኃበ ፡ ወድካመ ። 'ወአንተሰ ፡ ጸዊመከ”፡ ፡ ፳ ወ ፳ ዕለተ ፡ ቦኑ ፡ ዘረከበከ ፡ ዘከመዝ ፡ ጸማ ። ወይቤ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ 'አልቦ ፡ እግዚእየ" ። ወባሕቱ ፡፤ እፈቅድ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትንግረኒ ፡ ምንተ ፡ "ትሁቦ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ እኅ" ፡ ወምንተ ፡ ፍዳሁ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ እሁቦ ፡ ዐስቦ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘለከ ፡ እሁበከ” ። ወለከሰ ፡ አጽናዕኩከ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ጾም ፡ ወረኃብ”" ። ወዝንቱ ፡ ተከፍለ ፡ ለዘአልቦ ፡ 'ጽንአ ፡ ወይጻሙ ፡ በአምጣኑ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ስምየ"! ። እብለክሙ ፡ ይእዜኒ” ፡ ባኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፍሥሐሁ ፡ ለእግዚእክሙ ።
After that, Abbä Bsoy fasted twenty-one days. Our Lord Jesus Christ came to him and said to him: "My chosen one Bsoy, you have been very courageous." Bsoy said to him: "My Lord, upon you (rests) my trust that you strengthen me; thanks to that, I have no weakness at all." Our Lord led him and brought him to a weak brother who had fasted two days. Abbä Bsoy saw him fallen to the ground, twisting from side to side and seeking coolness and air far from the suffering of the fast. Before having accomplished two days (of fasting), he desired rest. Bsoy said to our Lord: "My Lord, what is the matter with this brother?" Our Lord said to him: "He has fasted the night." Bsoy said to him: "How many days (has he spent) since he began to fast until he fell and was thrown (to the ground)?" Our Lord said to him: "Since I created him, I have not made him miss a meal one single day except this night, and behold, I have sent upon him hunger and weakness. As for you, having fasted twenty-one days, has such a torment touched you?" Bsoy said: "No, my Lord, but I would like you to tell me what you will give to this brother and what (will be) his reward." Our Lord said to him: "I will give him his wage such as that which I will give you. For you, I have strengthened you against fasting and hunger. This is imparted to him who has no strength and who suffers to his measure for my name. I say to you: now, go in the joy of your Lord."
ወእምውእቱ”" ፡ ፅለት ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ተከለ ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ግብር” ፡ ዓቢይ ። ወሰአለ ፡ እምእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወይቤ ፡ እግዚእየ ፣ ኢእፈቅድ ፡ እብላእ ፡ ኅብስተ ፡ ብዙኃ ። ወሰአለ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ እግዚእየ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ኢወፃእኩ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ እደክም ፡ ብዙኃ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ 'ኢትግበር ፡ ፈቃደ ፡ ሥጋከ ፡ ወኢትድክም”” ። ወካ ዕበ ፡ ሰአለ” ፡ ወይቤ ፤ እግዚእየ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ተመዋዕኩ ፡ እመዐት ። ወይቤሎ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢትርግም ፡ ወኢትጽአል!”?' ፡ ወኢትግአዝ” ፡ ወኢትመንን” ፡ መነሂ ። ወኢይመውአከ ፡ መዐት ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ የኀድር ፡ ላዕሌከ ። ወሀሎ ፡ እግዚአብ ሔር ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ወይተልዎ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ወይትናገሮ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይትናገር ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ። ካዕበ ፡ ሰአሎ ፡ ለእግዚኡ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኦእግዚእየ ፡ ምንተ ፡ ይረክብ ፡ ዘይትኤዘዝ ፡ ለስምከ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወይትለአከከ ፡ ወይሳተፍ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወይረብሖሙ ። ወይቤሎ፤ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ በከመ ፡ ገባራዊ ፡ ዘይገብር ፡ ምስሌከ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድርከ ። አኮኑ ፡ 'ትሁብ ፡ ዐስቦ፤ ። ከማሁኬ ፤ እሁቦሙ ፡ ዐስቦሙ ፡ በኢ
From that day, Abbä Bsoy fixed himself on a great action. He asked God and said: "My Lord, I do not want to eat much bread." He asked again and said: "My Lord, if I do not go out to the desert, I will be weak greatly." Our Lord said to him: "Do not do the will of your flesh and do not be weak." Again, (Bsoy) asked and said: "My Lord, many times I have been overcome by anger." Our Lord said to him: "Do not curse, insult, quarrel, or despise anyone; then anger will not overcome you and the Holy Spirit will reside upon you." God was with our father, followed him where he went, and spoke to him as a man speaks with his friend. He questioned his Lord again and said: "O my Lord, what does he obtain who obeys your holy name and serves you but has commerce with men and is useful to them?" Our Lord said to him: "Just as a worker who works with you in your land - do you not give him a wage? -, likewise thus I give them their wage in the heavenly Jerusalem." Again, (Bsoy) asked

የሩሳሌም ፡ ሰማያዊት ። ካዕበ ፡ ሰአለ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ኦእግዚእየ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ቦ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ይነ ብር ፡ ማእከለ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወይትለአኮሙ ፡ ወይበውእ ፡ ወይወፅእ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ በትሕ ትና ፣ ወባሕቱሰ ፡ ኢይጸሙ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ምንተ ፡ ይረክብ" ፡ ዝንቱ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ተአዛዚ ። ወእመሰ ፡ ይገብር ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ይከውን ፡ ወራሴ ። "ካዕበ ፣ ይቤሎ ፡ አባ ፡፣ ብሶይ ፡ እግዚእየ ፡ እመቦ ፡ ዘይገብር ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ወየሐ ዝን ፡ በእንተ ፤ ስምከ ፡ ወይሳተፍ" ፡ ምስለ ፡ አግብርቲከ ፤፣ ወይትለአኮሙ”፤ ፡ ወያሠምር ፡ገጸከ ፡ 'ምንተ ፡ ትሁቦ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ እግዚእነ” ፡ ክብሩ” ፡ ወዓስቡ ፡ ዓቢይ ። ወይከውን ፡ ከመ ፡ ፳-እምቅዱሳንየ ፡ ወእሁቦ ፤ ሠናያተ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። ዘንተ ፡ ይቤ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ወዐርገ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ በዓቢይ ፡ ስብሐት ።
and said: "O my Lord, if a man dwells among people, serves them, goes in and out with them in humility, but does not labor much, what does he obtain?" Our Lord said to him: "This one is a legatee, and if he does much, he will become a (heavenly) heir." Again, Bsoy said to him: "My Lord, if there is someone who does much, is in affliction for your name, has dealings with your servants, serves them, and is pleasing to you, what will you give him?" Our Lord said to him: "His honor and his reward (will be) great; he will be like one of my saints, and I will give him goods in the kingdom of heaven." Our Lord said this and ascended to heaven in great glory.
ወሀሎ ፡ በምድረ ፡ ሶርያ ፤ ባሕታዊ ፡፤ ቅዱስ” ። ወይትለአክ ፡፤ ቅድመ ፡ እግዚአ ብሔር ፡ ሌሊተ ፡ ወመዐልተ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ እዙዘ”" ፡ በሥርዓቱ ፣ ወይገብር” ፡ ግብረ ፣ ዓቢየ ፡ 'ዘበእንተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር” ። ' ወአሐደ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ይቀውም ፡ ውስተ ፤ ጸሎት ፣ በሌሊት ፡ ኀለየ ፡ "በልቡ ፤ ወይቤ” ፡ እረክብኑ ፤ ምሕረተ ፤ በቅድመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ 8እምነ ፡ ቅዱሳን ። ወበጊዜሃ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ቃል ፡ እምሰማይ ፡ ዘይብል ፡ ሀሎ ፡ በገዳመ""” ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ይትለ አኮ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወያመልኮ ፡ 'ሠናየ ፡ አምሌኮ” ፡ ወስሙ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ዘከመዝ ፡ ይረ ክብ ፡ ምሕረተ ። 'እስመ ፣ ተመይጠ” ፡ ርእየቱ ፡ ወበርሀ" ፡ ገጹ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወተንሥአ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ባሕታዊ ፡ በፍሥሐ ፡ ወአርገ ፡ ሐመረ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ወበጽሐ ፡ እስክንድርያ” ። ወተስእለ ፡ መካነ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ይነብር” ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ። ወዜነውዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይነብር” ፡ ገዳመ ፡ ባጢራ ። ወአርትዐ ፡ ሐዊረ ፡ ለህየ ፡ ወበጽሐ ። ወተራከቦ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ወሐቀፎ ፡ ወተዋሀቡ ፡ ሰላመ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ። ወአብኦ ፡ ውስተ ፡ በዐቱ ፡ ወጸለዩ ፡ ኅቡረ ፡ ወነበሩ ። ወኮነ ፡ ነገሩ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ባሕ ታዊ ፡ በሶርያኒ ፡ ወአባ ፣ ብሶይ" ፣ ኢያአምር ፡ ነገረ"! ፡ ሶርያን ። "ወአልአለ ፡ አዕይንቲሁ" ፡ ለሰማይ ፤ ወከሠተ' ፣ ሕሊናሁ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ 'እሰግድ ፡ ለእበይከ ፡ እግዚእየ ፡ ወእፈቅድ” ፡ ከመ ፡ ትክሥት ፡ ሊተ ፡ ዘይትናገር ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ እጐ ። ወበጊ ዜሃ ፡ አብርሀ ፡ 'እግዚአብሔር ፡ ልቦ ፡ በመንፈሰ ፡ መለኮት ፡ ወአእመረ ፡ ነገረ' ፡ ሶርያን ። 'ወአኀዙ ፡ ይትናገሩ!' ፡ እበያቲሁ ፤ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወምሕረቶ ። "ወተስእሎ ፡ ውእቱ" ፡ ሶርያዊ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሥርዐታት ፡ ዘሠርዐ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ሥርዐተ”” ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ር ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ዘነበረ ፡ "ቀዲሙ ፡ ውስተ፣ ዝንቱ ፡ ገዳም” ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘሠርዐ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ሥርዐተ ፡ ወዘንተ ፡ ሕገገ ። ውእቱ ፡ ዘአግሀደ ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ ወመርሐ ፡ ባቲ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ዘይነብር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ። ወነገሮ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ እምግብሩ ፡ ወሥርዐቱ"' ፡ ወዜነዎ ፡ 'ተአምራተ ፡ ዘአግሀደ"”= ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በላዕለ ፡ እደዊሁ ፡ ንጹሐት ። 'ወሰሚዖ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ባሕታዊ ፡ አንከረ ፡
There was in the land of Syria (Soryä) a holy solitary. He served before God night and day, was severe in his conduct, and performed great deeds that (were) for God. One day, while he was standing in prayer at night, he prayed in his heart and said: "Shall I find grace before God like one of (His) saints?" Immediately a voice came from heaven saying: "There is in the desert of Egypt an old man (who) serves God and worships Him with a beautiful worship—his name (is) Bsoy—; he finds such grace: his appearance has changed and his face has shone before God." The solitary arose with joy, boarded a boat, set out, and arrived in Alexandria. He asked for the place where abbä Bsoy dwelt, and he was told that he dwelt in the desert of Nitria (Bätirä). He went straight there and arrived (there). The holy abbä Bsoy came to meet him, embraced him, and they gave each other the greeting. (Bsoy) brought him into his cell, they prayed together, and sat down. The words of the solitary were in Syriac, and abbä Bsoy did not know the language of the Syrians. He lifted his eyes to heaven, opened his mind, and said: "I prostrate myself before your greatness, my Lord, and I would that you reveal to me what this brother is saying." Immediately, God enlightened his heart by the spirit of divinity, and (Bsoy) knew the language of the Syrians. They began to speak of the greatness of God and His mercy. The Syrian questioned him about the rules that abbä Macarius had established as good discipline. The virtuous (and) righteous abbä Bsoy said to him: "He who first dwelt in this desert is the one who established this rule and these laws, is the one who made public the way of life and guided therein whosoever dwells in the desert." And he told him many of his (= Macarius') deeds and his ways of acting, and narrated to him the miracles that God had made known through his pure hands. Having heard (this), the solitary was astonished and did not know (at first) what to say; then he said:


[p. 324] {#po.49.p324}
ወኀጥአ ፡ ዘይብል” ። ወይቤ ፡ እፎ ፡ የዐቢ ፤ ወይትአኩት” ፡ ለዝሉፉ ፡ 'ዘይትማ ሰሎ ፣፤ ለዘከመዝ ፣፡ ብእሲ ፤ ፍጹም ፡ አባ ፣ መቃርስ ። 'ወቅዱስ ፡ ብሶይ”' ፡፣ ይቤሎሙ”ጆ ፣፥ ለአኃው ፡ አፍጥኑ ፡ ተወከፉ ፡ እምበረከተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዓቢይ ። እስመ ፡ አነ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ ከመ” ፡ ጽንዐ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ላዕሌሀ ፡ ወምሉአ ፡ ጸጋ ። ወመ ጽኡ ፡ 'አኃው ፣፤ ከመ ፡ ይትባረኩ ፡ እምኔሁ ። ወነበረ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ፳8ሱባኤ፣ ወርእየ ፡ ሥርአቶ ፡ ወአእመረ ፡ ፍድፍድናሁ ። 'ወበርሀ ፡ ልቡ ፡ በርእየተ ፡ ዚአሁ” ፡ ወአእኩተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ኦእኀየ ፡ ጸሊ ፡ ላዕሌየ ፡ ወትረፍ” ፡ በሰላም ። እስመ ፡ አነ ፡ እፈቅድ ፡ ሐዊረ ፡ ለብሔርየ ። ወተሐቀፉ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡
"How great and worthy of praise forever is he who imitates such a perfect man, abbä Macarius!" The holy Bsoy said to the brothers: "Hasten to receive the blessing of this eminent man, for I (am) a witness that the power of God (is) upon him and (that he is) full of grace." The brothers came to be blessed by him. || remained with our father for a week; he saw his conduct and knew his excellence. His heart shone from his vision, he gave thanks to God, and said to our father: "My brother, pray for me and remain in peace, for I want to go to my country." They embraced, he left him, and went to his country.
ወወፅአ ፡ እምኅቤሁ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ብሔሮ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ወጽአ ፡ 8እምአኃው ፡ ከመ ፡፣ ይትራከብ ፡ አቡነ ። ወይቤልዎ ፡ አኃው ፡ እመሰ ፡ ቀደምከ ፡ እምነሣአከ ፡ በረከተ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ "ትሩፍ ፡፤ ዐቢይ" ፡ ዘመ ጽአ ፡ ኀቤነ ፡ እምሶርያ ። ወእመሰ ፡ ተሐውር ፡ ድኅሬሁ ፡ ፍጡነ ፡ ትረክቦ ፡ እስመ ፣ ሖረ ፡ ምዕራፈ ፡ ፳8። ወአውሥአ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ እጐ ፤ ኢትሖር ፡ ድኅሬሁ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኢትክል ፡ እስመ ፡ ሖረ ፡ "ምሕዋረ ፡ ፲ወቿዕለተ፣ በደመና5 ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዑ ፤ አኃው" ፡ ሰብሑ ፤ እግዚአብሔር” ፡ ዘየኀድር ፡
After that, one of the brothers went out to go find our father. The brothers said to him: "If you (had come) earlier, you would have received the blessing of an excellent (and) eminent man who came to us from Syria. If you go after him quickly, you will find him, for he has covered only one mile." Our holy father Bsoy took the word and said to the brother: "Do not go after him, for you will not be able (to reach him): he has covered a journey of eighteen days on a cloud." When the brothers heard (this), they glorified God who dwells upon His saints.
ላዕለ ፡ ቅዱሳኒሁ ። "ወበአሐቲ ፡ፅለት ፡ መጽአ” ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ማኅደረ ፡ 8እጐ!፡ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያእምር ፡ ግብሮ ። "ወረከቦ ፡ ንውሙ ፡ ወመልአክ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ ርእሱ ፡ የዐቅቦ ። ወይቤ ፡ በአማን ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ የዐቅብ ፡ እለ ፡ ይትዌከሉ ፡ ኪያሁ” ። ወገብአ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሴብሖ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ።
One day, the holy abbä Bsoy came to the dwelling of a brother to know what he was doing. He found him asleep, an angel above his head guarding him. He said: "In truth, God guards those who put their trust in Him," and he returned, glorifying God.
"ካዕበ ፡ ፳እጐ ፡ ወረደ ፡ ምስር ፡ ይሚጥ ፡ ግብረ ፡ እደዊሁ ። ወረከቦ ፡ አይሁዳዊ ፡ ወአኀዘ ፤ ይትዋሥኦ ፣ ወይቤሎ ፡ አንትሙ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ትብሉ ፡ ዘተሰቅለ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ውእቱ ። ኢኮነኬ ፡ አላ ፡ ካልእ ፡ ዘይመስሎ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እጐ ፤ በእበድ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ትቤ ። ወሶበ ፡ ተናገረ ፣ ከመዝ ፡ ርሕቀት ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ጸጋ ፡፤ ጥምቀት ፣ በይእቲ ፤ ቃል ፡ ዘተናገረ ፡ በእበደ ፡ ልቡ ። ወገብአ ፡ ወመጽአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ። ወነጸረ ፣ አቡነ ፤ ቅዱስ ፣ ዘተንሥአት" ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ ጥምቀት ፡ ወአርመመ ፡ ሰዐተ ፡ ዓቢየ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እጐ ፡ ገጾ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢኮነ ፡ ፍቱሐ ፡ ገጹ ፡ ሠሐቀ ፡ በከመ ፡ ልማዱ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኦአቡየ ፡ ለምንት ፡ ትመይጥ ፡ ገጸከ ፡ እምኔየ ፡ ዮም ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ቅዱሰ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ "መኑ ፡ ስምከ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አነ ፡ እገሌ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ አልቦ”፣ ኢኮንከ ፡ እስመ ፡ አልቦ ፣፤ ዘእሬኢ ፡ ላዕሌከ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ጥምቀት ። ወይ ቤሎ ፡ ውእቱ ፡እጐ ፡ በእንተ ፡ምንት ፡ ሖረት ፡ እምኔየ ፡ ኦአባ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አረጋዊ”ዖ ፣ በእንተ ፡ ቃል ፡ ዘትቤሎ ፡ ለአይሁዳዊ ፡ በምስር ። ወሰገደ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ስረይ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ኦአቡየ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እስመ ፡ አበስኩ ፡ በእበድየ ፡ ዘእቤ ፤ ውእተ ፡ ቃለ ። ወይ
Another (time), a brother went down to Egypt (Mesr) to sell the work of his hands. A Jew met him, began to challenge him, and said to him: "You Christians, you say that he who was crucified is the Christ: it was not (him) indeed, but another who resembled him." The brother said to him in folly: "You have spoken well." When he had spoken thus, the grace of baptism departed from him because of the word he had spoken in the folly of his heart. He returned and came (back) into the desert. Our holy father noticed that the grace of baptism had left him, but he remained silent for a long hour. When the brother saw the face of abbä Bsoy—that (the latter) did not have an open face and (that he was not) smiling according to his custom—he said to him: "My father, why do you turn your face from me today?" The saint of God abbä Bsoy said to him: "What (is) your name?" (The brother) said to him: "I (am) So-and-so." The old man said to him: "No, you are not, for I do not see upon you the grace of holy baptism." The brother said to him: "Why has it left me, abbä?" The old man said to him: "Because of the word that you said to the Jew in Egypt." (The brother) prostrated himself before him and said to him: "Forgive me, my holy father, for I have sinned in my folly, I who said this word." The saint said to him: "Watch

ቤሎ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዑቅ ፡ ኦወልድየ ፡ እምይእዜ ፡ ኢትትወከፍ ፡ ቃለ ፡ እንግዳ ፡ ላዕለ ፣ ዛቲ ፤ ሃይማኖት ፡ ርትዕት ፡ ዘእንበለ ፤ ዘተወከፍነ ፤ 'እምአበው ፡ እምቅዱሳን ፡ ኪያሁ ፡ ንእኀዝ ። ወጸለየ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ወይቤ ፣፡ እግዚእየ ፣ ወአምላኪየ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ፳ዘኢአበሰ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ አንተ ፡ ባሕቲትከ ። አንቀዐዱ ፡ ኀቤከ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ምሕረት ፡ ወመስተማይጥ ፡ ስረይ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ኀጢአቶ”” ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ እጐ ፡ 'ዘተስሕተ ፤ በእበዱ ። ወበጸሎቱ ፡ ለአረጋዊ ፡ አግብአ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ጸጋ ፡ ጥምቀት ፡
from now on, my son, not to receive the word of a stranger against this (our) right faith, but only what we have received from the fathers, the saints, let us hold it firm." Then our father prayed over him and said: "My Lord and my God, there is no one who has not faulted except you alone; I lift my eyes toward you, full of mercy and inclined to clemency. Forgive the sin of this brother who has been led astray by his folly." By the prayer of the old man, God returned the grace of baptism to the brother.

Another (time) came to abbä Bsoy the eminent old man by the name of John. He labored much night and day, dwelt in the desert, and neither ate nor drank. He performed great deeds and he himself was oppressed by thirst and by hunger. He said to himself: "Let us go to abbä Bsoy to know his news." He arose, came to his place, and knocked at the door. The holy abbä Bsoy went out to him and brought him into his dwelling. They prayed, sat down, and refreshed themselves. God revealed by the spirit to abbä Bsoy that abbä John had fasted for many days, was burned with thirst, and was in torment. Abba Bsoy ordered his disciple to bring bread and water so that they might eat with abbä John so that he might be strengthened, he who (was) weakened by hunger and by thirst. The disciple brought (this) according to what his (spiritual) father had ordered him. (The latter) said to abbä John: "Arise, eat bread and drink a little water." But abbä John did not want to taste anything and said to him: "Forgive me, I do not eat but I fast for my sins." Abbä Bsoy said to him: "My brother, drink a little water, for you are burned by the heat." Abbä John said to him: "Leave me." When our father Bsoy saw that he did not want to eat or drink, he arose, stretched out his hands, prayed before God, and said: "Hasten, my Lord Jesus Christ, in favor of your servant John, for this one is exhausted and weakened by fasting." At that very moment, the spirit of abbä John was taken up and he saw four (beings) feeding him bread and giving him water to drink until he was satisfied. This vision took place in truth. After he had eaten, drunk, and was satisfied, (John) returned to his dwelling and increased his fasting; he said to his soul: "Fast now, for behold, you have been unburdened of your abstinence."
ጾሙ ፡ ወይብል” ፡ ለነፍሱ ፡ ጹሚ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ እስመ ፡ ናሁ ፡ 'ተወረድኪ ፤ እምትሕ ርምትኪ፤። "ወሀሎ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ መስተጋድል ፡ ወሐዲስ ፡ በምንኩስና ። ወያጽሕብዎ ፡ አጋን ንት ። ወተንሥአ ፡ ወመጽአ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ 'ኦአባ ፡ ብሶይ” ፡ ተዘከረኒ ፡ በጸሎትከ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እስመ ፡ '!አጋንንት ፣ ያጠውቁኒ ፡ ወያማስኑ ፡ ሕይ ወትየ ። ወኢያአርፉ ፡ ተቃትሎ ፡ ምስሌየ ፡ 'በኩሉ ፡ ጊዜ" ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አቡነ” ፡ ቅዱስ ፡፤ ጽናዕ ፡ በመንፈስ” ፣ ወኢታርእዮሙ ፡ ድካመከ ። ወሰአሎ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡
There was a man, an athlete (spiritual) and new in the monastic state; the demons harassed him. He arose, came to abbä Bsoy, and said to him: "Abbä Bsoy, remember me in your holy prayer, for the demons press me and ruin my life; they do not cease to attack me at every moment." Our holy father said to him: "Be strong by the spirit and do not show them your weakness.
