ወኅሥሥ ፡ "እምኅቤሁ ፤ ሣህለ” ፣ ወኢታስትት ፡ ጸልዮ ። ወውእቱ ፣ ይሰድዶሙ ፡፣ እምኔከ ፡ ወያአርፈከ ። ወአእምር ፡ ከመ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ተቃትሎ ፡ ትነሥእ ፡ ዐስበ ፡ እምኀበ! ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወበዝንቱ ፡ ናአምር” ፡ ከመ ፡ አንተ ፡ ጽኑዕ ፡ መስተ ጋድል ፤ ለእመ ፡ ቆምከ ፡ በጽንዕ” ። ወእምዝ ፡ ጸውዖ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ለመንፈስ ፡ ርኩስ ፡ ዘነበረ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እጐ ፡ ወመጽአ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጥሕር ፡ ከመ ፡ አንበሳ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሕመምከኒ” ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ቡሩክ ፡ ረሐቅ ፤ እምዝንቱ ፡ እጐ ፣ ወኢታደንግዖ ። ወይቤ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ እምሕል ፡ ለከ ፡ ኢያእመርኩ ፡ ከመ ፡ 'ቦቱ ፡ ጽንዐ ፡ ለዝመነኮስ” ። ወባሕቱ ፡ እመንፈስ ፡ ድኩም ፡ አሜክር ። ወናሁ ፡ እምይእዜሰ ፡ አነ ፡ እትቃተሎ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ዘእክል ። ወይቤ ፡ አረጋዊ ፣ ያውግዝከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወያማስንከ” ፡ ወይዝሩከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ጢስ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ አቆመ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ መሬተ ፡ ልዑለ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እጐ ። ወቀትላተፆኖ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ጸንዖ ፡ ወአፍርሆ ፡ እስከ ፡ ጐየ ፡ ወወፅአ ፡ ፅራቁ ፣ እማኅደሩ” ። ወመጽአ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኦአቡየ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ "ርድአኒ ፡ ወአሕይወኒየ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ምንተ ፡ ኮንከ ፡ ወልድየ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ውእቱ" ፡ እጐ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ሰደዱኒ ፡ 'አጋንንት ፡ ወአውዕፅኡኒ ፡ ፅራቅየ ፡ እማኅደርየ"፤ ። ወአኀዘ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ በእዴሁ፤ ፡ ወአብኦ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ማኅደሩ ፡ ወኀደጎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያዕርፍ ፤ እምድንጋፄቄ” ። ወቆመ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለጸሎት ፡ ወአንሥአ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ አንጻረ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወአስተበቀሞ'ዐ ፡ ኅበ ፡ እግዚ አብሔር ፡ ወይቤ ፤ እግዚኦ” ፣ አምላክ” ፡ አኀዜ ፣፤ ኩሉ ፡ ወፈጣሬ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ በጥበቡ" ፡ አንተ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ ዐቃቢነ ፡ ወክዳንነ ። ' ኦአምላከ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ለከ ፡ እሰግድ” ፡ ወእስእል ፡ ምሕረተ ፡ ዚአከ ፡ ከመ ፣ ታድኅን”' ፡ ዘንተ ፣ ገብረከ”! ፡ እምሕማማተ ፡ ሰይጣን ፤ ወታጸንዖ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንበር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ወይፈጽም ፡ ፈቃደከ ፡ ወይኩን ፡ ከመ ፡ ዕፅ ፡ እንተ ፡ ትሁብ ፡ ፍሬሃ ፡ ወቄጽላኒ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እዴከ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ መዋዕለ ፤ ሕይወቱ ። አንተ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ ትቤ ፡ እመልእ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ በመነኮሳት ፡ እለ ፡ ይቄድሱ ፡ ስምየ ፡ ቅዱሰ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ንስእል” ፡ ኀቤከ ፡ ወንሰግድ ፡ ታሕተ ፡ እዴከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትኩን ፡ ለነ ፡ ዐቃቢነ ፡ 'ወከዳኒነ ፡ ወቀዋሜ ፡ ለድካምነሞ ፡ እስመ ፡
Implore Christ, ask him for (his) clemency, and do not stop praying: he will drive them far from you and will give you rest. Know that, for this attack, you will receive a reward from God. By this, we will know that you (are) a strong (spiritual) athlete if you resist with strength." After that, abbä Bsoy called the impure spirit that dwelt upon this brother, and (the latter) came roaring like a lion. He said to our holy father: "You have hurt me!" The blessed old man said to him: "Depart from this brother and do not disturb him (anymore)." Satan said: "I swear to you that I did not know that this monk had strength; on the contrary, I (was) having him tempted by a weak spirit. Behold, from now on, I will attack him with everything I am capable of." The old man said to him: "May God curse you, destroy you, and scatter you like smoke." After that, Satan placed a heap of earth upon the brother, overcame him with many assaults, and frightened him until he fled and went out naked from his dwelling. (The brother) came to the old man and said to him: "My holy father, help me and save me." The old man said to him: "What is the matter with you, my son?" The brother said to him: "Behold, the demons have driven me out and have made me go out naked from my dwelling." Abbä Bsoy took him by the hand, brought him into his dwelling, and left him so that he might recover from his trouble. The holy old man stood up to pray, lifted his hands toward heaven, besought God, and said: "Lord God, almighty and creator of all by your wisdom, you, Lord, (are) our guardian and our protection. O God of all, I prostrate myself before you and I implore your mercy so that you may save this servant of yours from the torments of Satan and give him the strength so that he may dwell in this desert, accomplish your will, and be like a tree that gives its fruit and its foliage in your hand all the days of his life. You, Lord, you said: 'I will fill this desert with monks who will sanctify my holy name.' Because of this, we implore you and prostrate ourselves under your hand so that you may be for us our guardian, our protector, and the support of our weakness, for you can do all things, unique Jesus Christ, in the ages of ages, amen."
"አንተ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ትክልዎኖ ፡ ዋሕድ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም”። ፡ አሜን ። ወሶበ ፡ ፈጸመ ፡ ጸሎቶ ፡ 'ጻጸድቅ ፤ ወቴርዎ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ርእየ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚእብሔር ፡ አሰሮ ፡ ለሰይጣን ፡ በሰናስል ፡ ወአቀሞ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ። ወከልሐ ፡ ሰይ ጣን ፡ ወአውየወ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ወይሊተ ፡ እምኔከ ፡ ኦብሶይ ፡ እስመ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ አሕመምከኒ። ሕያው ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘእርዕድ ፡ ወእፈርህ ፡ እምኔሁ ። አነ"' ፡ አኀድግ ፣ ለከ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ ወአለ ፡ ይነብሩ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ወአሐውር ፡ ካልአ ፡ ብሔረ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኦጸላኤ ፡ ርትዕ ፡ ኢታአርፍኑ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትትቃተሎሙ ፡ ለአግብርተ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ እለ ፡ ' ተቤዘዎሙ ፡ በደሙ ፡ ክቡር” ። ወለምንት ፡ ትሰድድ ፡ "ወትትቃተሎ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ እጐጸ› ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ እፈቅድ ፡ ከመ ፡ አውድቆ፤፣
When the righteous and virtuous abbä Bsoy had finished his prayer, he saw the angel of God (who) had bound Satan with chains and had placed him before him (= Bsoy). Satan cried out, lamented, and said: "Woe to me because of you, Bsoy, for you have tormented me much! God is living, before whom I tremble and I am afraid! I leave you this desert and those who dwell in it and am going to another country." The holy old man said to him: "Enemy of what is right, will you not stop attacking the servants of Christ whom he has redeemed with his precious blood? Why do you pursue and attack this brother?" Satan said to him: "I want to make him fall


[p. 330] {#po.49.p330}
ፍጡነ ። "ወለእመ ፡ ኀደግዎ" ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ግዑዘ ፡ ይጸንዐኒ ፡ ወይቴይለኒ ፡ ወይፈደፍድ” ፡ 'ሕማምየ ፡ ወጸማየ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ያውግዝከ ፡ እግዚአብ ሔር ፡ ወያርሕቀ ። ወመሐለ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ወይቤ ፡ እመሰ ፡ ተኀድገኒ ፡ ውስተ፣ ዝንቱ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ኢይገብእ ፡ ወኢያስተሐምም ፡ ዘንተ ፡ እጐ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ለመልአክ ፡ ፍትሖ ፡ ወኅድጎ ፡ እስከ ፡ ዕለት ፡ እንተ ፡ ባቲ ፡ ይኬንኖ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወይፈድዮ ፡ በከመ ፡ ምግበሩ ፡ እኩይ" ። ወበዝንቱ ፡ ኀደጎ ፡ ወመ ጽአ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እጐ ፡ ወኀተመ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ገጹ ፡ በማኅተመ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወናዘዘ” ፡ ወጸንዖ ፡ ወፈነዎ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ማኅደሩ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ያአኩት ፤ ወይሴብሖኖ" ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ወበጸሎቱ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ኢገብኡ ፡ ሰይጣናት" ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እጐ ፡ እምውእቱ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ወኢያሕስሙ ። ወአኀዘ ፡ ይትወሰክ ፡ ገድለ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ወያፈደፍድ ፡ ወይሴብሖ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወያአዙቶ ፡ ወያአምር ፡ ጸጋሁ ፡ ዘላዕሌሁ ፡ 'ወያአኩት ፡ አባ ፣
quickly. If I leave him so that he may be free, he will be stronger than me, will prevail over me, and my sufferings and my pains will redouble." The saint said to him: "May God curse you and drive you away." Satan adjured him and said: "If you leave me in this desert, I will not begin again to torment this brother." After that, abbä Bsoy said to the angel: "Untie him and leave him until the day when God will punish him and will recompense him according to his evil deeds." Upon this, (Bsoy) left him, came to the brother, and signed his face with the sign of the holy cross in the name of the Father, and of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit. He comforted him, strengthened him, and sent him (back) to his dwelling, praising and glorifying God. By the prayer of the holy abbä Bsoy, the demons did not return upon this brother from that day and did not do him any (more) harm. He began to increase his (spiritual) combat every day and to redouble (it): he glorified God and praised him; he knew his grace that (was) upon him and praised abbä Bsoy.
ብሶይሃ”” ። "ካዕበ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ አኃው ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እጐ ፡ምስለ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ከመ፣ ይስምዑ ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ ወተስእልዎ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ትምህርተ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይርብሑ ፡ ነፍሳቲ ሆሙ ። ወአውሥአ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ተጋደሉ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትዕቀቡ ፡ ቀኖና ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ዘውእቱ ፣፡ ጾም ፡ ወጸሎት ፡ 'ወንጽሐ ፡፤ ወትሕትና ፡ ወፍቅር ፡ ምስለ ፡ ኩሉ ። ወዘበእንተ ፡ አሕሥሞሰ ፡ መኑሂ ፡ ኢየሐሊ”" ፡ እስመ ፡ ኢመፍትው ፡ ለክርስቲያን ። ወብዙኃ ፡ ነገሮሙ ፡ ዘይመስሎ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ወከሠተ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ሕሊና ፡ አልባቢሆሙ። ወመዐዶሙ ፡እስከ ፡ ኀጥኡ ፡ ዘይብሉ ፡ ወአንከሩ ፡ ብዙኃ ። ወይቤልዎ" ፡ አኃው ፡ "ለአባ ፡ ዮሐንስ” ፡ አቡነ ፤ በአማን ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ቅዱሰ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እስመ ፡ ከሠተ ፡ ለነ ፡ ሕሊና ፡ አልባቢነ ። ካዕበ ፡ ይቤሎሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ አባ” ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ገበርኩኖ ፡ ግብራተ ፡ ባሕቲትየ ፡ 'ዘኢያአምሮ ፡ ሰብእ ። ወሶበ ፡ ተራከበ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሐንስ" ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘይሴውሮ ። ' ወሶበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ከመዝ ፣ አፈድፈዱ ፡ አሐው ፡ አእኩቶኞ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘየኀድር ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ቅዱሳኒሁ ።
Another (time), some brothers came, while this brother was with abbä Bsoy, to learn his news; they asked him for many teachings so that their souls might profit from them. (Bsoy) took the word and said to them: "Fight (spiritually) to keep the rule of the monks which is fasting and prayer, purity, humility, and love for all. As for doing evil, let no one think of it, for it is not fitting for Christians." He said to them many (things) that are similar to this and revealed to them the thought of their hearts. He exhorted them until they did not know what to say and were very astonished. The brothers said to abbä John: "Our father, in truth, this old man (is) a saint of God, for he has revealed to us the thought of our hearts." (Bsoy) said to them again while abbä John was present: "Many times, I have done alone deeds that men did not know." When he met with abbä John, he hid nothing from him. When he had spoken thus, the brothers redoubled their praise toward God who dwells upon His saints.
"ወሀሎ ፡ ፳እጐ ፡ ወወትረሞ' ፡ ይገብር ፡ 'ፈቃደ ፡ ርእሱኖ፤! ። ወኢይትማከር ፡ ምስለ ፡ አበይት ፡ ወኢይሴአሎሙ ፡ ወኢይገብር ፡ ፈቃዶሙፅ= ። ተንሥአ ፡ ወሖረ ፣ በገቦ ፡ማኅደረ ፤ አኃውዎ ፡ ቅሩበ ፡ ቀስጣጣኖ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንበር ፡ ሕያው ፡ ባሕቲቱ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ቦአ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ቀስጣጣ” ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሚጥ ፡ ግብረ ፡ እደዊሁ ። ወርእየቶ ፡ ብእሲት ፣ አይሁዳዊት ፡ መሠሪት ፡ "እንተ ፤ መስኮት ። ወመልአ" ፣ ሰይጣን ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልባ ፡ ፍቅረ ፡ ዚአሁ! ። ወጸውአቶ ፡ ወአብዐቶ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤታ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘይሣየጥ"”፤ ፡ ግብረ ፡ እደዊሁ ። ወረከበቶ ፡ በመሥገርታ ፡ ወአምሐለቶ ፡ ከመ ፡
There was a brother who always did his own will; he did not take counsel from the elders, did not question them, and did not do their will. He arose and went beside the dwelling of the brothers near Qastätä (?) to dwell (there) while living alone. After some days, he entered the city of Qastätä to sell the work of his hands. A Jewish sorceress saw him from (her) window and Satan filled her heart with desire for him (= the monk). She called him and brought him into her house as someone who (wanted) to buy the work of his hands. She took him in her nets and adjured him to abandon his monastic state


[p. 332] {#po.49.p332}
ይኅድግ ፡ ምንኩስናሁ ፡ ወይንበር ፡፤ ምስሌሃ" ። ወረሰየቶ ፡ አይሁዳዌ ፡ ከማሃ"”" ፣ ወአስከበቶ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ እገሪሃ ። ወነሥአት ፡ ስፃረ ፡ ወነቀሰት ፡ ማእከለ ፡ አስናኑ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይትረፍ” ፣ እምሥጋሁ ፤ ለመድኅን" ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፣ ወረዱ ፤ እምአኃው”” ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሣየጡ ፡ መፍቀዶሙ ፡ ወቦኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወእንዘ ፡ ይሠይጡ" ፡ ነጸሮሙ ፡ ይስሐቅ ፡ አይሁዳዊ ፡ ወተስእሎሙ ፣ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ እምገዳምኑ ፡ አንትሙ ፤ ወይቤልዎ ፡ እወ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ታአምሩኑ ፡ ቅዱሰ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይሃ ። ወይቤልዎ ፡ "አርዳኢሁ ፡ ንሕነ” ። ወይቤሎሙ ፤ አምሕለክሙ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ገባእክሙ ፡ ንግርዎ ፡ ወበ ልዎ” ፡ ይስሐቅ ፡ አይሁዳዊ ፡ ይቤለከ ፡ እትመሐፀን ፡ ኦአባ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትጸሊ ፡ በእንቲአየ ፡ እስመ ፡ ወድቁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መስገርተ ፡ ሰይጣን ። ወይቤልዎ ፡ አኃው ፡ ይኩን ፣ ንነግሮ ፣ በከመ ፣ ትቤለነ፤! ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዐት ፡ አይሁዳዊት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትናገሩ፤ ፡ አእመረት ፣፤ ከመኞ ፡ ይክል ፡፤ አድኅኖቶ ፡ እምእደዊሃ ፡ 'ብሶይ ፤ ቅዱስዎ ። ኀወጸት ፤ እመስኮታ ፤ ወትቤሎሙ ፡፣ ለአኃውዎ ፣ ንግርዎ ፡ ለባሕታዊ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ እመሰ ፡ ርኢክዎዎኖ ፡ ዝየ ፡ አነ ፡ 'አሐምሞ ፤ ወአማስኖመ”" ። ወሶበ ፡ ገብኡ ፡ አኃው ፤ ገዳመ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትባረኩኞ ። ወረስዑ ፡ ወኢነገርዎ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ይስሐቅ ። ወቅዱስ”" ፡ አባ ፡፣ ብሶይ ፤ ዘበአማን”፤ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኤልያስ ፣፤ ወኤልሳዕ ፡ ይቤሎሙ ፡ ለአኃው ፡ ቦኑ ፡ 'ዘነገረክሙ ፡ በምስር ፡ ፳ከመ ፡ ትንግሩኒ””= ፡ ቃለ ። ወይቤልዎ ፡ አልብነ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡፤ አርስዖሙ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡'አባ ፡ ብሶይ” ፡ ይስሐቅ ፡ አይሁዳዊ ፡ አልቦኑ ፡ ዘለአከክሙ”፡፡ ከመ ፡ ትንግሩኒ ፡ ሊተ ። ወይቤልዎ ፡ እወ ፡ ኦአቡነ ፡ ይኤምኀከ ፡ በሰላም ፡ ብዙኀ ፡ ወይትመሐፀነከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትጸሊ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ እስመ ፡ ወድቀ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መሥገርተ ፤ ሰይጣን ። ወሶበ ፡ ይቤለነ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ነጸረት ፡ ብእሲቱ ፡ 'ወሰምዐት ፡ ዘይነግረነ” ። ወትቤለነ ፡፤ በልዎ ፡ ለባሕታዊ ፡ ብሶይ ፡፤ እምርኢኩከ ፡ በዝየ ፡ 'አመነድበከ ፡
and to dwell with her. She made him a Jew like herself and made him lie at her feet. She took a splinter of wood and scraped in the middle of his teeth so that nothing of the body of the Savior would remain. After that, some brothers went down (from the desert) to buy what they needed and entered the city. While they were selling (their products), Isaac (Yeshaq) the Jew saw them; he questioned them and said to them: "Are you from the desert?" They said to him: "Yes." He said to them: "Do you know the holy abbä Bsoy?" They said to him: "We are his disciples." He said to them: "I adjure you: when you have returned, speak to him and say to him: Isaac the Jew says to you: I entrust myself (to you), holy abbä, so that you may pray for me, for I have fallen into the nets of Satan." The brothers said to him: "So be it: we will speak as you have told us." When the Jewess heard them speaking, she knew that Bsoy the saint could save him (= the monk) from her hands. She looked from her window and said to the brothers: "Tell the solitary Bsoy that if I see him here, I will torment him and make him perish." When the brothers had returned to the desert, they came to abbä Bsoy to be blessed (by him). They forgot and did not speak to him about Isaac. The holy abbä Bsoy, a true prophet like Elijah and Elisha, said to the brothers: "Is there someone who spoke to you in Egypt so that you might tell me a word?" They said to him: "No," for Satan had made them forget. The holy abbä Bsoy said to them: "Did Isaac the Jew not charge you to speak to me?" They said to him: "Yes, our father, he sends you great greetings and entrusts himself to you so that you may pray for him, for he has fallen into the nets of Satan. When he spoke to us thus, his wife looked and she heard what he was saying to us; she said to us: Tell the solitary Bsoy: If I see you here, I will torment you and burn you." They recounted everything that had happened to the holy Bsoy.
ወአውዕየከ ። 'ወኩሎዎ' ፡ ዘኮነ” ፡ ነገርዎ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ብሶይ”” ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዐ ፡ ዘንተ” ፣ አንቀልቀለ ፡ ወአስተጋብአ ፡ እገሪሁ ፡ ወተድኅረ”' ፡ ድኅሪተ ፡ ወአንጐርጐረ ፡ በሰለስቱ”” ፡ እስትንፋስ ። ወጸርኀ ፡ በዓቢይ ፡ ቃል ፡ ወይቤ ፤ ኦእግዚእየ ፤ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡፤ ርድአኒ ፡ ወደንገፁ ፡ አኃው ፡ ወአንከሩ ፡ ወይቤልዎ”” ፣ ኦአቡነ ፡ በእንተ ፤ ምንት”” ፡ ትደነግፅ ፡ ወትፈርህ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፣ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ መኑ ፡ ዘይክል ፡፤ ፈቲሐ ፡ መሥገርተ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ዘውዱድ ፡ በአንስት ፡ ዘእንበለ ፣ 'ዘእግዚአብሔር ፡ ላዕሌሁ' ። እስመ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ አዳም”" ፣ ብእሲት ፡ አስሐተቶ ፡ ወአውዕአቶ ፡ እምገነተ ፡ ትፍሥሕት ። ወዮሴፍ ፡ ጸድቅ ፣ በምክንያተ” ፡ ብእሲት ፡ ተወድየ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ። ወኤልያስ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ እምፍርሀተ”” ፡ ኤልዛቤል?” ፡ ጐየ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ። ሶምሶን ፡ ኀያል ፡ ወጽኑዕ ፤ እምብእሲቱ'?" ፡ ተአስረ ። ወበእንተ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ መጽአ?! ፡ ኩናት ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡
When (the latter) heard this, he staggered, steadied his feet, stepped backward, and was indignant (for the time of) three breaths. He cried out with a loud voice and said: "My Lord Jesus Christ, help me!" The brothers were troubled, astonished, and said to him: "Our father, why are you troubled and afraid?" The holy abbä Bsoy said to them: "Who can undo the nets of Satan that (are) spread by women if not he upon whom (is) God? Indeed, our father Adam, a woman led him astray and made him go out of the Garden of Delights. Joseph the righteous, by the fault of a woman, was thrown into prison. Elijah the prophet, for fear of Jezebel ('Élzabel), fled into the desert. Samson (Somson), the powerful and strong, was chained by his wife. It is because of a woman that the spear came upon the sons of Israel and that two hundred and thirty thousand men


[p. 334] {#po.49.p334}
እስራኤል ፡ ወተቀትሉ ፡ ፳ ወ፻፻፻ ብእሲ ፤ በአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ። ዮሐንስ ፡ መጥምቅ ፡ በምክረ" ፡ ብእሲት ፡ ተመትረ ፡ ርእሱ ። ሰሎሞን ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ወጠቢብ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ርእሰ ፡ በመንግሥቱ ፡ ወተለዐለ ፤ እምእለ ፣፤ ቅድሜሁ ፡፣ ነገሥት ፡ አንስት ፡ አጽለማ"' ፥ ልቦ?” ፡ ወአእተታ?" ፡ ጥበቦ”! ። ወጴጥሮስ ፡ ርእሰ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡፤ ብእሲት ፡ አስፈ ጠቶ ፡ ወክሕዶ ፡ ለእግዚኡ ። ወለምንት ፡ እዘልፍ?!' ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ዝልፈት?'፤ ። አላ?! ፡ ከማሁ ፡ ነገርኩ ፡ በምሳሌ ፡ ነገር ። ወእስመ”” ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሀሎ ፡ "ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ለለ ፡ እስከ"?! ፡ አውፅኦሙ ፡ እምባሕር"” ፤ ወእመዋግዲሃ ፡ "ዘዝ ፤ ዓለም'” ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዑ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ አኃው ፡ አንከሩ ፡ 'ብዝኃ ፡ ጥበቡ ፡ ወአበይኖቱ ፡ ለአረጋዊ ። ወሰብሕዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ፈድፋደ ። ወከሠተ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ ከመ ፡ መሠሪት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ወበሥራያ ፡ አስገረቶ ፡ ለይስ ሐቅ ፡ ወአክሐደቶ'”” ።
were killed in a single day. (As for) John the Baptist, his head was cut off on the counsel of a woman. (As for) Solomon, the great and wise who was chief in his kingdom and was elevated above the kings who (had been) before him, women darkened his heart and took away his wisdom. (As for) Peter, the chief of the apostles, a woman reduced him and he denied his Lord. Why would I rebuke the saints with this rebuke? In fact, I spoke thus by giving examples (?). Indeed, God was with each of them until he had brought them out of the sea—and from its waves—of this world." When the brothers heard this, they admired the greatness of the wisdom and the perspicacity of the old man and glorified God much. (Bsoy) revealed to them regarding this woman that she was a sorceress, had trapped Isaac by her magic, and had made him abjure.
ወእምይእቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ 'አሐዘ ፡፤ ይጸሊ" ፡ አቡነ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ሌሊተ ፤ ወመዐ ልተ ። ወይሰአሎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ያድኅኖ ፡ ለይስሐቅ ፡ እምይእቲ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ አይሁዳዊት ። ወተወክፈ" ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ጸሎቶ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ። ወአስ ተርአዮ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኦኅሩይየ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ለምንት ፡ ትጻሙ ፡ በእንተ ፡-ይስሐቅ ። እስመ ፡ እምንእሱ ፡ ተለወ ፡ ይግበር ፡'ፈቃደ ፡ርእሱ ፡ እስከ?›' ፡ ወድቀ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መሥገርተ ፤ ሰይጣን ፡ 'ወክሕደ ፡ ጥምቀተ" ፡ እንተ ፡ ባቲ ፡ ተቀብአ ። ወበከየ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ኢያዐርፍ ፡ እምስኢል ፡ ወአስተብቀፆዖ ፡ ኪያከ ፡ እስከ ፡ ትምሕሮ'” ፡ ወታገ ብዖ ፡ ኀቤነ ፡ ወትሰሪ ፡ ሎቱ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ሰረይኩ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ እንሣእኑ ፣ እምዚአከ ፡ ዐስበ” ፡ ወአሀቦ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ኦእግዚኦ ፡ አንተ ፡ ታአምር" ፡ ዘውስተ ፡ አልባብ ። በከመ ፡ አፈቅር ፡ 'ድኅነተ ፡ ለነፍስየ'” ፡ ከማሁ ፡ አፈቅር ፡ ድኅነተ ፡ ለኩሉ ። ኢይትከሀለኒ ፡ እርአይ ፡ እየ ፡ በምንዳቤ?፤ ፡ ወሕማም ፡ ወአነ ፡ አሀሉ ፡ በዕረፍት ። 'እስመ ፡ እም ፡ 8ሐመድ ፡ ንሕነ' ፡ 'ወኩልነ ፡ ተግባረ ፡ እደዊከ ፡ ቅድስት ። እወ ፡ እግዚእየ ፣ መሐር ፡ ኪያሁ ፡ ወአግብኦ ፡ ለፈቃድከ ፡ ቅድስት ። ወኢያዐርፍ ፡ 'እምስኢል ፤ ወአስተብቀዖት?” ፡ ኀቤከ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ሕይወትየ ፡ እስከ ፡ ታገብኦ ፡ ኀቤየ" ። ወይቤሎ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ገበርከ ፡ ኦፍቁ ርየ ፡ ብሶይ ። አእመርኩ ፣ ከመ'” ፡ አንተ ፡ ትሜጡ ፡ ነፍሰከ ፡ ህየንተ ፡ ቢጽከ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዝክዎሙ ፡ ለሐዋርያትየ ፡ ወእቤሎሙ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ፍቅረ ፡ ዘየዐቢ ፡ እምዝ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘቦ ፡ ዘይሜጡ ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ ቤዛ ፡ አዕርክቲሁ ። ወይእዜኒ ፡ ኢይነሥእ ፡ እምአሥ ብከ ፡ አላ ፡ አፈደፍድ ፡ ለከ ፡ 'ወእሴትከ ፡ ዐቢይ” ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘአፍቀርከ”"፤ ፡ ተግባ ርየ ። ወበእንቲአከ ፡ እምሕሮ ፡ ለይስሐቅ ፡ ዘአበሰ ፡ ወክሕደኒ ፡ ወአገብኦ”” ፡ ኀቤከ ። ወወሀቦ ፡ ሰላመ ፡ ወአርገ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ በዓቢይ ፡ ስብሐት ። ወአው ረደ ፡ መቅሠፍተ ፡ላዕለ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ አይሁዳዊት ፡ ወተመንደበት ፡ ሕዳጠ” ፡ መዋዕለ ፡
From that day, our father abbä Bsoy began to pray night and day; he besought God so that He might save Isaac from the Jewish woman. God accepted the prayer of the holy abbä Bsoy; He appeared to him and said: "My chosen Bsoy, why do you weary yourself for Isaac: since his childhood, he has applied himself to (doing only) his own will so much so that he has fallen into the nets of Satan and has denied the baptism with which he was anointed." Abbä Bsoy wept and said: "I will not cease to implore you and to beseech you until you have had pity on him, brought him back to us, and forgiven him." Our Lord said to him: "If I forgive him, shall I take (his) reward from yours and give (it) to him?" The old man said to him: "O Lord, you know what (is) in the hearts: just as I wish for salvation for my soul, so I wish for salvation for all; it is not possible for me to see my brother in misfortune and suffering and to be, myself, in rest, for we (are made) of one and the same dust and (are) we all the product of your holy hands. Yes, my Lord, have pity on him and bring him back to your holy will. I will not cease to implore you and to beseech you all the days of my life until you have brought him back to me." Our Lord said to him: "You have done well, my beloved Bsoy. I know that you offer your life for your neighbor, according to what I prescribed to my apostles and said to them: || there is no greater love than this: he who offers his life for his friends. Now, I will not take from your reward, but I will multiply (it) for you and your retribution (will be) great because you have loved my work. For your sake, I have pity on Isaac who has sinned and denied me and I will bring him back to you." Then our Lord gave him peace and ascended to heaven in great glory. He brought down a punishment upon the Jewish woman: she was


[p. 336] {#po.49.p336}
ወሞተት ። ወተንሥአ ፡ ይስሐቅ ፡ ወመጽአ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አቡነ” ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወኮነ ፤ ረድኦ ። ወገብአ ፡ ኅበ ፤ ክርስትና” ፡ ወኮነ ፡ መነኮሰ ፡ ፍጹመ ፣ ወአሥመሮ” ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ በኩሉ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ሕይወቱ ።
tormented for a few days and died. Isaac arose, came to the desert to our holy father, and became his disciple. He returned to Christianity, became an accomplished monk, and pleased God during all the days of his life.
"ወእምድኅረ ፡ መዋዕል” ፡ አንሶሰወ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም" ፡ ወቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ በዓት ። ወረከበ ፡ በህየ ፡ ምውተ”” ፡ ብሉየ ፡ ዘበልየ ፡ ጥብሉለ ፡ ወእሱረ”” ። ወጸለየ ፡ ወነበረ ፤ ወይቤ ፡ ኦብእሲ ፡ 'ዘወፃእከ ፡ እምዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ኀላፊ ፡ ወተሌለይከ ፡፣ እምኔሁ” ። ወሶበ ፡ ፈቀደ ፡ ወዒአ ፡ እምኔሃ ፡ ሐለየ ፡ በነፍሱ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ እነሥእ ፡ ምስሌየ ፡ ዛተ ፡ ወልተመ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይብልዑ ፡ ባቲ ፤ አኃው?” ፡ ወይዝክሩ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ምውተ ፡ ወሰፍሐ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ወነሥአ ። ወይቤ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ነሣእክዋ ፡ ወፈቀድኩ ፡ ወአ ፡ መጽአኒ ፡ ቃል ፡ እምነ ፡ ውእቱ?” ፡ ምውት ፡ ዘይብል ፡ ኦብሶይ ፡ አግብእ ፡ ወልተመ፣ ኅበ ፡ መካና ፡ ኢይደሉ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩኑ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ፈያት ። ወበጊዜሃ ፡ ኀደግዋ ፡ ወሖርኩ ፡እንዘ ፡ እሴብሖ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ' ወዘንተ ፡ ከሰተ ፡ ሊተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፤ ኦአኃው?” ፡ እስመ ፡ ኢይደልዎ ፡ ለብእሲ ፡ ይንሣእ ፡ ዘኢኮነ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ ወፈድፋደሰ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ምውት ። 'ወአዘዞሙ ፡ ለአኃው ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ !ኢትኅሥጮሥ ፡ ለክሙ ፥ ብዙኃ ፡ መባልዕተ ፡ ወኢታስተዳልዉ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ማእዳተ ። ወኢትሖሩ ፡ ለባዓላት” ፡ ወኢለተዝካረ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ወጻድቃን ፣ ወኢትትጋብኡ””' ፡ ለጉባኤ ፡ ' ተዝካር ። አላ ፡ በካልእ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ሖሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አብያተ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ቅዱሳን'" ፣ ወጸልዩ ፡ ወበሉ፣ ኦእግዚኦሙ ፡ ለቅዱሳን ፡ ሰማዕት'” ፡ ስረይ ፡ ለነ ፤ ኀጢአተነ ፡ ወመሐረነ ፡ ከመ ፡
After some days, the saint walked in the desert and entered a cave. He found there an ancient corpse that had aged, wrapped and swaddled (in strips of cloth). He prayed, sat down, and said: "Man who has gone out of this transitory world and has separated yourself from it." When he wanted to go out of it (= the cave), he thought in himself and said: "I will take with me this waltama so that the brothers may eat from it and remember this dead man," and he stretched out his hand and took (it). He said: "When I had taken it and wanted to go out, a voice came to me from this dead man saying: 'Bsoy, put the waltama back in its place: it is not fitting that there be thieves in this desert.' Immediately, I left it and went away, glorifying God. This is what God revealed to me, brothers: it is not fitting for a man to take what is not his, all the more so the property of a dead man." He commanded the brothers and said to them: "Do not seek for yourselves abundant food and do not prepare abundant meals. Do not go to feasts nor to the commemoration of the martyrs and the righteous and do not join a commemoration meeting. On the contrary, go on the second day to the houses of the holy martyrs, pray, and say: 'O Lord of the holy martyrs, forgive us our sin and have pity on us so that we may do your will at all times,' and return to your dwellings."
ንግበር ፡ ፈቃደከ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ጊዜ ። ወግብኡ ፡ ኀበ ፡ መኃድሪክሙ”” ። " ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ተፈነዉ ፡ ቀታልያን ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ። ወእቤሎ ፡ አነ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ኦቅዱስ ፡ እጉየ”፤ ፡ ኢየኀብእ ፡ እምኔከ ፡ ዘሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልብየ ። ሶበ ፡ ርኢኩ ፡ በር ባሪ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይቀትልዎሙ ፡ ለአበው ፡ ፈራህኩ”” ፡ ሞተ ፡ ወእቤ ፡ እሖር ፡ እምዝየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይቅትለኒ'”” ፡ በርበሪ ፤ ወኢይባእ"”” ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገሀነም ፡ በእንቲአየ ። ወይቤ ለኒ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እመሰ ፡ አንተ ፡ 'ተሐውር ፡ ወአነኒ ፡ ካዕበ"” ፡ አሐውር ፡ ምስሌከ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ቆምነ ፡ ወጸለይነ ፡ 'ወወዒአነ ፡ ሖርነ”” ፡ ምስረ ፡ ወሖርኩ ፡ አነ ፡ በገቦ ፡ ወረከብኩ ፡ በዐተ ፡ ወቦእኩ ፡ ውስቴታ ። ወአባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ዐርገ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ቃሪስ ፡ ቅሩበ ፡ አንጤኑ ፡ ሀገር ። ወስሙዓቱ”' ፡ ወሥርዐቱ'” ፣ ዐቢይ ። ወይመጽኡ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ እለ ፡ ቅሩበ"” ፡ ሀለዉ ። ውእቱሂ ፡ ይሜህሮሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይዕቀቡ?” ፡ ሀይማኖተ ፡ ርትዕተ ፡ ዘሥሉስ ፡ ቅዱስ”?! ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ?” ፡ አምላክ?” ። "ውእቱ ፡፤ ቀዳማዊ ፡፤ ዘአልቦቱ ፡ ጥንተ ፡ ወኢተፍጻሜተ ፡ እስከ ፡ ለዓለም"'” ። "ወይኤዝዞሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትጋብኡ ፡ ውስተ?” ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን?” ፡ ወይንሥኡ ፡
After that, murderers were sent into the desert. I, John, said to my holy brother: "I do not hide from you what is in my heart: when I saw plunderers killing the fathers (monks), I was afraid of death and said to myself: Let us leave here so that the plunderers may not kill me and enter into hell because of me." The saint said to me: "If you go away, I too will go away with you." Because of this, we arose, prayed, and, having gone out, went to Egypt. I went, for my (part?), I found a cave and I entered it. Abbä Bsoy went up into the South near the city of Antinoopolis ('Antënu). His fame and his rule (were) great. Those who were close to him came to him. As for him, he taught them so that they might keep the right faith of the holy Trinity—the Father, the Son, and the Holy Spirit, one single God—it (which is) first without beginning nor end forever. He commanded them to gather

ሥጋሁ ፡ ወደሙ ፡ ለእግዚእ ። እስመ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ወደሙ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ ዘለዓለም ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ዘይነሥእ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ በሐይማኖት ፡ ርትዕት ። 'ካእበ ፡ ይቤሎሙ ፡ አፍቅሩ?” ፡ ግብረ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ኩሉ ፡ ዘያፈቅር ፡ ትእዛዘ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ 'ውእቱኬ ፡ ዘያፈቅሮ ፡ ለእግ ዚእነ ። ወዘገበርነ ፡ ለአኃዊነ?” ፡ በእንተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ' በህየ ፡ ይጸንሐነ”" ። ወእ ንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ይነብር፤ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አኃው ፡ ወይነግሮሙ ፡ በቀ'ዔተ ፡ ለነፍሳቲሆሙ ፡ ፈቀደ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያመክሮሙ ፡ 'ለእመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ርቱዐ ፤ ሃይማኖቶሙ ፡፤ ወለእመሂ ፡ አልቦ”= ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ያቀርብ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ከመ ፡ እበል ፡ ቃለ" ። ወአ ውሥኦ” ፥ ይእተ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ኀበ" ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ርትዕት ። ወኢታፍቅር ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ከመ ፡ "ትበል ፤ ከመዝ" ። ካዕበ ፤ ለእመ ፣ አስተማሰለ ፡ ምንተኒ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ሥርዐተ ፣ ምንኩስና ። ወምንት ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ሥርዐትነ ፡ ዝንቱ” ፡ ከመ ፡ ንፍረይ ። ይእዜኒ ፡ ዘንተ ፣ ገቢረከ ፡ ኢታግብእ'” ፡ ኀቤየ ። 'ወእስከ ፡ ዝኒ ፡ ገቢረክሙ ፡ ትልዉ ፡ ፈቃድየ” ፡ ቦቱ ። ወይቤልዎ ፡ ሰብእ ፤ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ኦአቡነ ፡ ኢትትሀወክኑ ። ወለ እመ ፡ኮነ ፡ አምልኮ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ንስእለከ ፡ ወንትመሐፀነከ ። ወባዕዳን ፡ እምነ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ይቤልዎ ፡ ኢነአብየከ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ዘአዘዝከነ ፣ ንትኤዘዘከ ። ወባዕዳንሂ ፡ ይቤሉ ፡ ዘትቤለነ ፤ ንገብር”! ፡ ከማሁ ። ወአውሥአ ፡ ፳እምኔሆሙ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ ‹አበው ፡ ወይቤ"”” ፡ አንሰ ፡ ኢመጸእኩ” ፡ ኀቤከ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ከመ ፡ አእምር ፡ ርትዐ ፣ ዘያበጽሕ ፡ ኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወእመሰ ፡ ትናፍቅ ፡ 'እምሀይማኖት ፡ ርትዕት” ፡ ኢይተልወከ ፡ ወኢይነብር” ፡ ምስሌከ ። ወተንሥአ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ወሰዐሞ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘየዐቅብ ፡ ትእዛዛተ ፡ እግዚእ ። 'ዝንቱ ፡ ውእቱ” ፡ ይስሐቅ ፡ ዘኮነ” ፡ አይሁዳዌ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተመይጠ”” ፡ ወኮነ ፡ 'ጽኑአ ፡ ወፍጹመ ፡ በሃይማኖት” ። ወአፈድፈደ ፡ ወቀንአ ፡ ለፍቅረ ፡ አምላኩ ፡ ወአፍቀሮጆኞ" ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ሞቱ ፡ 'ለቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ አበ ፡ ለአኃው”፤ ፡ አርአያ ፡ ወመርሐ” ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ወምክንያተ ፡ መድኀኒትቾ” ። ወይሜህሮሙ ፡ ለአኃው ፡ ወትረ ፡
at the church and to receive the body and the blood of the Lord, for his body and his blood (are) eternal life for whosoever receives them with a right faith. He said to them again: "Love the work of God; whosoever loves the commandment of God, that one indeed (is) the one who loves Our Lord. And what we do for our brothers because of God awaits us in the hereafter." While he was sitting with the brothers and speaking to them of what is useful for their souls, he wanted to tempt them (to see) if their faith was right or not. He said to them: "Satan has approached me so that I might say a word, and to utter this word (is) according to the right faith: Do not wish to say so again. If he assimilates again some action to the monastic rule, what is this rule of ours so that we may bear fruit? Now, having done this, do not return to me. Having acted (thus) until now, follow my will on this subject." People among them said to him: "Our father, are you not troubled? If the worship of God exists, we pray to you and entrust ourselves to you." Others among the monks said to him: "We will not refuse you: in everything that you order us, we will obey you." Others still said: "What you will have told us, we will do likewise." One of them took the word before the fathers and said: "For me, I only came to you to know the right (behavior) that leads to God. But if you doubt the right faith, I will not follow you nor dwell with you." The holy abbä Bsoy arose, kissed his head, and said: "This one is someone who keeps the commandments of the Lord; this one is Isaac who became a Jew." After that, (Isaac) converted and was firm and accomplished in the faith. He redoubled his ardor in the love of his God and the holy abbä Bsoy loved him. After the death of the saint, he became a father for the brothers, an eminent example and guide, a cause of salvation. || taught the brothers constantly and strengthened them for the salvation of their soul.
ወይጹንዖሙ ፡ ለመድኅኀኒተ ፡ ነፍሶሙ ። ወሀሎ ፡ ቀሲስ ፡ ዘይነብረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ 'አባ ፡ ብሶይ” ። ወሶበ ፡ 'ይሜህሮሙ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ለአኃው” ፡ ውእቱሰ ፡ ቀሲስ ፡ ይዜንውዎሙ ፡ ዜናዝ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ወያናፍቆሙ ፡ "ወኩሎሙ ፡ እለ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትመሀሩ ፡ እምቅዱስ ፡ ብሶይ ። ካዕበ ፡ ሰሚዖሙ ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ቀሲስ ፡ ዘትምክሕተዝ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ይትዐቀፉ” ፡ በእንቲአሁ ። ካዕበ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ አኀው” ፡ ኃበ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትራከብዎ ። ወተጋብኡ ፡ አኃው ፡ ኀበ ፡ ካልእ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ወነገርዎ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ቀሲስ ፡ ወሐሰቱ ፡ ወሁከቱ ። ወን ሕነ ፡ ኩልነ ፡፤ ንትሀወክዎኞ ፡ 'ወነሐዝን ፡ በእንተ ፡ ኑፋቄ ፣ ዘይወዲ” ፡ ቅድሜነ ።
There was a priest who dwelt with abbä Bsoy. When the holy Bsoy taught the brothers, this priest gave them news of this world and made them doubt, as well as all those who came to be instructed by the saint Bsoy. Hearing also the priest's story about the brilliance of this world, they were scandalized by him. Some brothers came again to abbä Bsoy to meet with him. Some brothers gathered with another old man and recounted to him the work of the priest, his lying, and the trouble (that) he (brought). "We all (, they said,) are troubled and afflicted because of the doubt that he throws before us and, when we go to our dwellings, Satan brings it back to our memory and upsets us." The old man said to them: "Come, let us go to our father; when he speaks a little, mention me so that I may

ወሶበ ፤ ነሐውር ፡ ኀበ ፣ መኃድሪነ ፡ ይዜክረነ ፡ ሰይጣን'" ፣ ወያደነግፀነ ። ወይቤ ሎሙ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ንዑ ፡ ንሖር ፡ ኅበ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ይትናገር ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ ዝክሩኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ እስአሎ ፡ ያውግዞ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢያደንግፅክሙ ፡ በነገሩ ። ወተንሥኡ ፡ ወመጽኡ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ! ። ወመጽአ ፡ ውእቱኒ ፡ ቀሲስ ፡ በከመ ፡ ልማዱ ፡ ወአኀዘ ፡ ይትናገር ፡ ቅድሜሆሙ ፡ ወሰፍሐዳ" ፡ ነገሩ ። ወኢገሠጾ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ። 'ወነሥኦ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ወአግሐዎሦ ፡ እንተ ፤ ባሕቲቶሙ ። ወነገሮ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ያንጐረ ጐሩ ፡ አኃው ፡ በእንተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ቀሲስ ፤ ወይቤሎ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይፈቅዱ ፡ ይጸግቡ ፡ እምትምህርትከ ፡ ውእቱሰ ፡ ቀሲስ ፡ መርሐ ፡ ዕቅፍት ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሎሙቦ፤ ። ወይቤ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ምንተ ፡ ትፈቅድ ፡ እግበር ፡ ቦቱ ። ወሰይጣንሰ ፡ ይፈቅድ ፡ አሕጉሎቶ ፡ ወአንትሙኒ ፡ ትፈቅዱ ፡ አሕዝኖቶ ፡ ወስደቶ ። 'አኮኑ ፡ ይብለኒ ፡ ኦብሶይ ፡ ወሀብኩከ ፡ ብአሴ፤ ፡ ወኢተዐገስከ ፣፤ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወለእመ ፡ አውገዝክዎ ፡ ወከላእክዎ' ፡ ኢይትናገር ፡ በከመ ፡ ልማዱ ፡ ይደነግፅ ፡ ወየሐውር ፡ እምኔየ ። ወይገብእ ፡ ውስተ ፤ ሀገሩ ፡ ወይከውን ፡ አለማዌ'' ፡ ወያማስን ፡ ነፍሶ" ። ወባሕቱ ፡ ይደሉ ፡፤ ትት ዐገሱ” ፡ ላዕሌየ ፡ ወላዕሌሁ ፡ በፍቅር” ። ወእምዝ! ፡ ታዐርፍ ፡ ነፍሳቲክሙጾ” ። ወአውሥኡዎ፤ ፡ ወይቤልዎኞ ፡ እመሰ ፡ የኀድግኞ ፡ ልማዶ ፡ ወይገብእ” ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቀኖና ፡ ምንኩስና ፡ ወየኀድግ” ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ምግባረ ፡ ወዘንተ” ፡ ትዝልፍተ ፡ ንትዓዔገሶ ፡ ወንጸውሮ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ አርእስቲነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይግባእ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ብርሃን ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ አእ መረ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ቀሲስ ፡ 'ኀጢአቶ ፡ ወእበዶ ፡ ወኀደገ ፡ ነገረ ፡ እኩየ ፡ ወአቅረበ ፡
ask him to curse him (= the priest) so that he may not disturb you (anymore) by his word." They arose and came to the holy father. The priest came also according to his custom, began to speak before them, and his discourse was successful (?). Abba Bsoy did not rebuke him. The old man took abbä Bsoy and drew him aside, (the two of them) being alone; he recounted to him how the brothers were murmuring about the priest and said to him: "When they want to be watered by your teaching, this priest is a guide of scandal to them." Abbä Bsoy said: "What do you want me to do regarding him? Satan wants to make him perish and you, you want his affliction and his expulsion. Did (Satan?) not say to me: 'Bsoy, I gave you a man and you were not patient with him.'? If I curse him and forbid him to speak as is his custom, he will be troubled and will go far from me; he will return to his city, will become a layman, and will lose his soul. On the contrary, it is fitting that you be patient with me and with him in love; after that, your souls will be at rest." They answered and said to him: "If he abandons his custom, returns to the monastic rule, and abandons this way of acting and this temerity, we will be patient toward him and will carry him on our heads (?) so that he may return toward the light." After that, the priest recognized his sin and his folly. He abandoned (his) evil discourse, came to repentance, and had an abundant reward; he was obedient toward the old man all the days of his life.
ንስሐሁ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ "ዐስቦ ፡ ብዙኀ ። ወኮነ ፡ ተአዛዜ ፡ ለአረጋዊ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ መዋ ዕለ ፡ ሕይወቱ። "ወእንዘ ፡ ይነብር” ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ውስተ ፡ በዐት ፡ በገዳመ ፡ ተባይስ፤ ፡ መጽአ ፤ ኀቤሁ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፪ መላእክቲሁ ፡ በከመ ፡ አስተርአዮ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ አብርሃም ፡ በታሕተ ፡ 'ብሉጠ ፡ መንበር ። ወበከመ ፡ ይቄ ድሶ ፡ አብርሃም ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ከማሁ ፡ 'አቡነ ፣ ብሶይ ፡ ይቄድሶ ፡ ለእግዚ አብሔር ፡ ወትረ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ጽርአት ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሰላም ፡ ለከ ፡ ኦጽሩይ ፡ ብሶይ ። ወተንሥአ ፡ ፍጡነ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ቡሩክ› ፡ ወሰገደ ፡ ሎቱ ። ወነሥአ ፡ ማየ ፡ ወአቅረበ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ 'ወአስተብቀ' ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኅፅቦኞቾ ፣ ወእምዝ ፡ ኀፀበ ፡ እገሪሁ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተለዐለ ፡ እምኅቤሁ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ በዓቢይ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ወዐርገ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማ ያት ። ወሰትየ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይኞ ፡ እምውእቱ ፡ ማይ ፡ ወዘተርፈ ፡ ኀደገ ፡ "ውስተ ፡ ሰማያት ። ወነበረ”ኞ፤ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ ረድኡ ፡ እምነ ፡ ምስር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስተይ” ፡ እምበረከተ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፈጽም ፡ ዘጽሑፍ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ አፍቅር ፡ ቢጸከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ነፍስከ ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ረድኡ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ኦወልድየ ፡ ተንሥእ ፡ ስተይ ፡ እምነ ፡
While the holy abbä Bsoy dwelt in a cell in the desert of Thebaid, our Lord Jesus Christ came to him with two of his angels, as he had appeared to our father Abraham under the oak of Mamre. Just as Abraham sanctified God, so our father Bsoy sanctified God constantly, without ceasing. Christ said to him: "Greetings to you, Bsoy the pure." The blessed old man arose quickly and prostrated himself before him. He took water, offered (it) to him, and besought him to (let him) wash him, then he washed his feet. Then, our Lord rose from him in great glory and ascended to the heavens. Abbä Bsoy drank of this water and, what remained (of it), he left in a vessel. It remained (there) until his disciple came from Egypt so that (the latter) might drink of the blessing of Christ so that the Scripture might be fulfilled which says: Love your neighbor as yourself. እልእያና ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ኦአቡየ ፡ አሐውር ፡ እስተይ ። ወከፈለ ፡ ልቡ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ህየንተ ፡ ዘእምይቤለኒ ፡ ስተይ ፡ ማየ ፡ ቄሪረ ፡ እስመ ፡ መጻእኩ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ዋዕይ ፡ ወጸማእኩ ፣ ወይቤለኒ ፡ ስተይ ፡ ማየ ፡ እምልእያና ። ወኢፈቀደ ፡ ይስተይ ፡ እም ኔሁ ። ወካዕበ ፣፤ ይቤሎ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ተንሥእ ፡ ኦወልድየ ፡ ስተይ ፡ ንስቲተ ፤ እምእልእያና" ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ጸሊ ፡ ላዕሌየ ፡ ወእገብር ። ወኢገብረ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ በልቡ ፡፤ እፈቅድሰ ፣ እስተይ ፡ ማየ ፡ ቄሪረ ፡ እምነ ፣ ኩዝ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ሥልሰ ፡ ለከ ፡ እብል ፥ ተንሥእ ፡ ስተይ ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ ማየ ፡ እምኤልእያናኞ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ እሰቲኞ፤ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ 'ወኢሖረ ። ወአርመመ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ አብ ። ወነጸረ ፡ ረድእ ፡ ገጾ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ተመይጠ ፡ ገጹ ፡ በከመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቀዳሚ ። ወተንሥአ ፡ ውእተ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ 'ማየ ፡ እልእያናኞ፤ ፡ ወኢረከበ ። ወገብአ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ አአበድከኒነ ፣ ኦአቡየ ፡ ሖርኩ ፤ ወኢረከብኩ ፡፤ ውስተ ፡ እልእያናኞ ፡ ማየ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አምአኞ' ፣ ኤልያስ ፡ ሐዲስ ፡ ወኤልያስ ፡ ካልእ ። 'ይብል ፡ እንከ” ፡ ምሳሌ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ 'ውእቱ ፡ ማይ ፡ ተረፈ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድር ፡ እስከ ፡ ይእዜ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ረድኡ ፡ ምንትኑ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ማይኞ፤ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ር ፤ እመነኒ= ፡ ኦወልድየ ፡ እስመ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ፳ እምነ ፡ አኃው ፡ ወያፈቅሮ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ገዳም ፡ 'ዘደለዎ ፡ በረከተጾ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኅዕብ ፡ "እግረ ፡ እግዚእነኞ ። ወኀፀቦ ፣ በውእቱ ፡ ማይ ። ' ወአምጽአ ፡ ሊተ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ወሰተይኩ" ። ወኀደጉ ፡ ለከ ፤ እምኔሁ ፤ ወኢተአዘዝከ ፡ ትስተይ ፡ ወነሥእዎ ፡ መላእክት ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዐ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ረድእ ፡ ወድቀ ፡ ታሕተ ፡ እገሪሁ ፡ ለአብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፤ ወጸልመ ፡ ልቡ” ፡ ሰዐተ ፡ ዓቢየ ። ወሶበ ፡ ገብአት ፡ ነፍሱ"! ፡ አንሥኦ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዘበአማን ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ 'አእምርኬ ፡ አዳምኒ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘኢተአዘዘ” ፡ ለፈጣሪሁ ፡ ወጽአ ፡ እምገነተ ፡ ትፍሥሕት ። ' ወአንተኒ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ተአዘ ዝከ ፡ ሊተ” ፡ እምኮንከ ፡ ዘመደ ፡ ለውእቱ” ፡ ሀብት ፡ ወረድኤት ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ እንተ ፡ ተከልአት” ፡ እምኔከ ። ወደንገፀ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ረድእ ፡ ወሐመ ፡ ፈድፋደ ። 'ወአኀዘ ፡ ይብክያ ፡ ለነፍሱ” ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘኢተአዘዘ ፡ ለመምህሩ ። ወኮነ ፡ ሕዙነ ፡ 'በእንተ ፡ ዘኢኮነ ፡ ዘመደ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ ሀብት"'' ። ወአረጋዊ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ይናዝዞ ፤ ወይምዕዶ ፡ ወይንዖ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሐዘንከ ፡ ዮም ፡ ተሰርየ ፤ ለከ ፡ ኀጢአትከ ። "ወኢተናዘዘ ፡ ልቡ” ። ወሰአሎ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ረድእ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ እግዚእየ ፡ ወአቡየ ፡ መሐረኒ ፡ ወፈንወኒ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ፍጹም ፡ ወጽኑዕ ፡ በእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወእስአሎ”፡ ፡ በእንተ ፡ እበድየ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ወሀቦ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ኅብስተ ፡ ተርሙስ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ሖር ፡ እርስናኞ፤ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወበህየ ፡ ትረክብ ፡ በየማነ ፡ ፀወን ፡ ብእሴ ፡ ንቡረ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ግዱፍ ፡
disciple arrived, he said to him: "My son, arise, drink (of the water) from (this) container." (The disciple) said to him: "My father, I am coming to drink." But his heart made a distinction (?) and said: "Instead of him having said to me: 'Drink fresh water,' because I have come through this heat and have been thirsty, he said to me: Drink water from (this) container." And he did not want to drink of it. The old man said to him again: "Arise, my son, drink a little (water) from (this) container." (The disciple) said: "Pray for me and I will do (it)." But he did not do it and said in his heart: I want to drink fresh water from the pot. (Bsoy) said to him a third time: "I tell you: Arise, drink a little water from (this) container." (The disciple) said to him: "I am going to drink of it," but he did not go. The father did not speak to him (anymore). The disciple looked at the face of our father; his face became again as it was before. He arose then and went near the water of the container, but he did not find it. He returned to our father and said to him: "Have you made me mad, my father: I went and did not find water in the container?" (Bsoy) said to him: "The new Elijah, the second Elijah, has given the victory." The holy abbä Bsoy was saying a parable thus. He said to him: "This water has remained in the earth until now." His disciple said to him: "What is this water?" The virtuous (Bsoy) said to him: "Believe me, my son: Christ came to one of the brothers, God loves him in this desert, he (= the brother) to whom fell the blessing of washing the feet of our Lord. He washed them with this water. He brought some to me and I drank. I left some for you, but you did not obey (my order) to drink and the angels took it." When the disciple heard (this), he fell at the feet of the holy father and his heart was dark for a long hour. When his soul returned, the holy old man, a true prophet, raised him up and said to him: "Know (this): Adam, because he did not obey his creator, went out of the Garden of Delights. You too, if you had obeyed me, you would be associated with this gift; but (it is) a great help that has departed from you." The disciple was troubled and suffered much. He began to weep over himself because he had not obeyed his master. He was afflicted because he had not been associated with this gift. The holy old man consoled him, exhorted him, and comforted him. He said to him: "Because of your affliction, today your sin is forgiven you." But the heart of the disciple was not consoled; he besought him (= Bsoy) and said to him: "My Lord and my father, have pity on me and send me to an accomplished man, firm in God, so that I may implore him regarding my folly." After that, our father gave him lupine bread and said to him: "Go to the city of Arsinoe (Ersenä); you will find there to the right of the wall a man sitting on refuse like a madman,
አምሳለ ፡ አብድ ። ወሕፃናት ፡ ይዌግርዎ ፡ በአእባን ። ወሀቦ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ተርሙሰ ፡ በስ ምየ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ይነግረከ ፡ 'ዘይደልወከ ፡ ለገቢርኞ ። ወተንሥአ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ረድእ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ እግዚኡ ። ወረከቦ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ግዱፍ ፡ ወሕፃናት ፤ የአውድዎዎ፤ ፡ ወይወግርዎ ፡ በአእባን ። ወሰደደ ፡ ሕፃናተ ፡ ወወሀቦ ፡ ተርሙሰ ፡ ኅብስተ ፡ ወላዕሌሁዎ ፡ ስመ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ። ወይቤ ፡ "ውእቱ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ብዙኀ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ፈተውኩ ፡ እስማዕ ፡ ዜናሁ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ለረድእ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘኢተአዘዝከ ፡ ለመምህርከ ፡ ወዐለውከ ። ትእዛዞ ፡ ወሀደገ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ 'ሀብት ፡ ዐቢይኞ ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ደንገፅከኞ ፡ ወየበስከ ፤ ወዝንቱ ፡ ድንጋፄከ ፤ ወፍርሀትከ ፡ ስር ዓተ ፡ ይኩንከ ። ተንሥእ ፡ ይእዜኒ ፡ ግባእ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አቡከ ፡ ወተአዘዝ ፡ ሎቱ ። ወአእ ምር ፡ ከመ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዘኢይትኤዘዝ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ኢይትኤዘዞ”፤ ፡ ለክርስ ቶስ ። ወገብአ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ረድእ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ወዜነዎ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡
children throwing stones at him. Give him this lupine in my name; he will tell you what you must do." The disciple arose and went according to what his master had told him. He found the man on the refuse, children were surrounding him and throwing stones at him. He drove away the children and gave him the lupine bread upon which (was) the name of abbä Bsoy. The old man said: "Many times, I have wished to hear his news." He said to the disciple: "Because you did not obey your master, that you transgressed his order and neglected something like this great gift, after that you trembled and withered. May this trembling and fear of yours be your forgiveness. Arise now, return to your (spiritual) father and obey him. Know that whosoever disobeys the holy abbä Bsoy disobeys Christ." The disciple returned to abbä Bsoy and recounted to him how the prophet of God had spoken to him.


[p. 344] {#po.49.p344}
ነቢየ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። "ወነበረ ፡ ምስለ ፤ አቡሁ” ፡ ወእምድኅረ ፣፤ ሕዳጥ”” ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ሐዘነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ረድእ ፡ በልቡ ፡ 'ወብዙኀ ፡ ይበኪ ፡ ወትረ ፣ ይብል ፣፡ ወይሊተሞ ፡ አፈቅር ፤ "መዊተ ፡ እምሐይዎትዎ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኢሰተይኩ ፡ በረከተ ፡ ኅፃበ ፡ እገሪሁ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ። ወቅዱስሰ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ይናዝዞ ፡፤ ወይብሎ ፡ እምድኅረሰ ፡ አርአይከ ፡ ንስሐከ ፡ ወሐዘነከ ፡ ይምሕረከ ፡፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወይሰሪ ፡ እበደከ ። ወኢተናዘዘ ፡ ልቡ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ነገርኞ ። ወሰአለ ፤ እምአቡሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፈንዎ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ቡሩክ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፈውሶኞ ፡ ሕማሞ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ መንፈሳዊ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ውእቱሰ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ዘሖርከ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ግዕዘ ። ወባሕቱ ፡ ሖር ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀግር ፡ እምወሰና ፡ ወትረክብ ፡ በአተ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ 'ርኅወ ፡ አንቀጹ”፤ ፡ ወባእ ፡ ህየ ። ወትረክብ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ እለ ፡ ሞቱ ፡ ወኖሙ ፡ ኅቡረ ። 'ውእቱሰ ፡ በዐት”" ፡ መቃብረ ፡ ነቢያት ፡ እለ ፡ አሥመሩ ፡ ቅድመፆ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በሀገር ፡ ወበምሥያጣት ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ጊዜሁ ፡ ለለ 8እምኔሆሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፍልስ ፡ እምዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ የዓርግ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መካን ፡ ወያገብእ ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር”” ። ባእ ፡ ህየ ፡ ወአንብር ፡ እዴከ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ዘንውም ፡ ማእከለ ፡ ፪ ወውእቱ ፡ ይትነሣእ ፡ ወይትናገር ፡ ምስሌከ ። ወሖረ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ረድእ ፡ ወረከበ ፡ ጅንውማነ ፡ በከመ ፡ ዜነዎ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ። ወአንበረ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ማእከላይ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ይቤለከ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ተንሥ እአ ፡ ከመ ፡ እትናገር ፡ ምስሌከ ። ወተንሥአ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ምውት ፡ ወይቤሎ ፥፡ አኮኑ ፡ ነገርኩከ ፡ እምቅድም ፡ ከመ ፡ ትትአዘዝ ፡ ለእግዚእከ ። ለምንት ፡ ትከውን ፡ ጸላኤ ፡ ሀይማኖት ፡ ከመዝ ። ወእመሰ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ለከ ፡ ሀይማኖተ ፡ 'ርቱዐ ፤ ናሁ” ፡ ነገርኩከ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዘይትኤዘዝ ፡ ለአባ” ፣ ብሶይ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ተአዘዘ ። ወኩሉ ፡ ዘየ አቢ ፤ ተአዝዞ ፡ ለመምህሩ ፡ አበየ ፡ ለክርስቶስ” ፣ ለወልደ ፤ እግዚአብሔር”" ። ተንሥእ ፡ ይእዜኒ ፡ ወሖር ፡ ኀበ ፡ አቡከ፤' ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይስእል ፡ ወያስተበቀዕ ፡ በእንቲአከ ፤ ኀበ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ። ዘንተ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ውእቱ” ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ወኖመ" ።
The disciple remained with his (spiritual) father but, after a few days, he was afflicted in his heart; he wept much and said constantly: "Woe to me, I would prefer to die than to live, for I did not drink the blessing (of the water) of the washing of the feet of Christ!" As for the holy abbä Bsoy, he consoled him and said to him: "After you have shown your repentance and your affliction, God will have pity on you and will forgive you your folly." But his heart was not consoled by this remark. He asked his (spiritual) father to send him to the blessed old man so that he might heal him of his malady. Our holy (and) spiritual father Bsoy said: "The old prophet to whom you went has passed away. However, go to the city, to its edge, and you will find a large cave with an open door; enter it. You will find three men who are dead and have fallen asleep together. This cave (is) the tomb of the prophets who pleased God in the city and in the markets. When the moment has arrived for each of them to leave this world, he goes up to this place and renders his soul to God. Enter it and place your hand upon the one who (is) asleep between the two (others): he will arise and will speak with you." The disciple went and found the three sleepers as the holy abbä Bsoy had told him. He placed his hand upon the one in the middle and said to him: "Thus says to you the holy abbä Bsoy: Arise so that I may speak with you." The dead prophet arose and said to him: "Did I not tell you before to obey your master? Why do you become the enemy of the faith thus? Even if you have the right faith, behold, I have told you that whosoever obeys abbä Bsoy has obeyed Christ, and whosoever refuses to obey his master has refused (to obey) Christ, the Son of God. Arise now and go to your (spiritual) father, for this one implores and beseeches God for you." The prophet said this to him and fell asleep. The disciple returned to abbä Bsoy and recounted how


[p. 346] {#po.49.p346}
ወገብአ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ረድእ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ወዜነዎ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ 'ውእቱ ፣ ነቢይ” ። ወአንከረ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ረድእ ፡ በዘረእየ” ፡ ወሰምዐ ። ወአኀዞ ፡ ፍርሀት ፡ ወረዓድ ፡ እምዕበየ" ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ወይትኤዘዝ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ሕይወቱ ። "ወዘንተ ፡ ሰሚዖ ፡ መኑሂ ፡ ያሰትት ፡ ወይእመን ፡ ከመ ፡ እሙን ፣ ውእቱ' ። ይቤ ፤ እግዚእነ፤' ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዘየአምን ፡ ብየ ፡ ዘአነ ፡ እገብር ፡ ይገብር ፡ ወዘየአቢ”” ፡ እም
the prophet had spoken. The disciple was astonished at what he had seen and heard. Fear and trembling took him before the greatness of abbä Bsoy and he obeyed him all the days of his life. Upon hearing this, who would disdain to believe that (this) is true? Our Lord said: Whosoever believes in me will do what I do and will do what is greater than that.
ኔሁ ፡ ይገብር። ወሀለዉ” ፡ ፪አኃው ፣፤ መነኮሳት”፤ ፡ ወመጽኡ ፡ 'ኅበ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ””= ፡ ወይቤ ልዎ ፡ግበር ፡ ለነ ፡ ፍቅረ ፡ ኦአቡነ ፤ ወተወከፈነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ንኩን ፡ ታሕተ ፡ መንጦላዕተ ፡ ጸሎትከ ። ወምርሖን ፡ ለነፍሳቲነ ፤ ወርብሖን ። ወተወክፎሙ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በፍሥሐ" ፡ ወፍቅር” ፤ ወአንበሮሙ ፤ ምስሌሁ" ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይነግሮሙ ፡ ወይሜህ ሮሙ ፡ እፎ ፡ ያሠምርዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ አቡነ” ፡ ቅዱስ”' ፡ ፈቀደ ፤ ይትገሐሥ ፡ እምሰብእ ፡ ወይንበር ፡ በብሕታዌ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፈጽም ፡ መልእ ክቶ” ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ በጽሙና ፡ ወከመ ፡ ይንቃህ ፡ በሥርዓቱ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ለ፪አኃው ፡ ተንሥኡ ፡ ባኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ወንበሩ ፡ በጽሙና ፡ ወት ልዉጻ” ፣ አንብቦ ፡ መጸሕፍት ። ወአውሥኡ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ እሉ ፡ አኃው ፡ ኅበ ፡ መኑ 0፥፡ ነሐውር ፡ ነገራተ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ ኀቤከ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ኦቅዱሰ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ እወ ፡ ሐሩ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኢይከውኖ”፤ ፡ ለመለኮስ ፡ ይፈጽም ፡ ወይመልእ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ በብሕትና ፡ ወበጽሙና ። ወይቤሉ ፡ አምረነ” ፡ ኦአቡነ ፡ አይቴ ፡ ነሐውር ። ወይቤ ሎሙ ፡ ኅሥሥሠ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ ዳቅዋ ፡ ወጸለየ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ። ወነሥኡ ፡ በረከቶ ፡ ወሖሩ ፡
There were two monk brothers; they came to abbä Bsoy and said to him: "Do us the kindness, our father, to receive us so that we may be under the mantle of your prayer; guide our souls and give them profit." Our holy father received them with joy and love and installed them with him, speaking to them and teaching them how they would please God. After that, our holy father wanted to separate himself from men and dwell alone to accomplish the service of God in effort and be vigilant in his conduct. He said to the two brothers: "Arise, enter this desert, dwell in effort, and apply yourselves to the reading of the Scriptures." The brothers answered and said to him: "To whom shall we go: the words of life are with you, saint of the Lord?" He said to them: "Yes, go, for it does not befit the monk to be completely accomplished except in solitude and in effort." They said: "Indicate to us, our father, where to go." He said to them: "Seek the desert of Däqwä (Qäw?)", and he prayed over them. They received his blessing, went to the desert of Däqwä, and dwelt there as he had prescribed to them.
ገዳመ ፡ዳቅዋ ፡ ወነበሩ ፡ህየ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዞሙ ። ወበ ፳እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ሠረቅት ፡ ወቦኡ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ማኅደረ ፡ ፳እምነ ፤ አኃው ፤ ወነሥኡ ፡ ዮስቃሶዖ፤ ። ወሀሎ ፡ አረጋዊዎ ፡ ዘይነብር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ "ዘአጽለመ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ልቦ”፤ ። ወሶበ ፡ ይጠፍእ ፡ "ንዋዮሙ ፡ ለሰብእ ፡ ይብሎሙ፣ እገሌ ፡ ዘነሥኦ ፣ እስመ ፣፤ መልአክ ፡ ውእቱ ፡፣ ዘይነግረኒ ። ወመጽአ” ፡ አበ ፡ ምኔቶሙ ፥ ኅበ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኦአቡነ ፡ ንግረኒ” ፡ መነ ፣ ዘበ ርበረ ፡ ማኅደረ ፡ እገሌ ። ወይቤሎ ፤ ውእቱ ፡ አረጋዊ” ፣ እሉ ፡ ፪ አኃው ፡ እለ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ዝየ ፡ ወኀደሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ውእቶሙ ፡ ነሥኡ ። ወአምንዎ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ አኃው ፣ ምስለ ፡ አበ ፡ ምኔት ። ወአኃዝዎሙ ፡ ለ ፪አኃው”" ፡ ከመ፣ ሠረቅት ፤ ወወደይዎሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ። ወአብ ፡ቅዱስ ፡ ነቢይ” ፡ ልዑል፣፤ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ አእመረ ፡ በመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዘበጽሖሙ ፡ እከይ ፡ ለእሙንቱ ፡ አኃው ። ወተንሥአ ፡ፍጡነ ፡ ወሖረ” ፡ ገዳመ ፡ ዳቅዋ ፡ ወረከቦሙ ፡ ለ ፪ አኃው ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ፤ ሞቅሕ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ለአበ ፡ ምኔት ፡ እሉ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እለ ፡ ሞቃሕኮሙ”ቦ ፡ ኢኮኑ ፡ ሠረቅተ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አበ ፡ ምኔት ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ዘነገረነ ። ወይቤሎ”! ፣
One day, thieves came, entered the dwelling of one of the brothers, and took his vessel. There was an old man living in the desert whose heart Satan had darkened. When people's property disappeared, he would say to them, "So-and-so took it; an angel told me so." Their abbot came to the old man and said to him, "Our father, tell me who plundered the dwelling of So-and-so." The old man said to him, "The two brothers who came here and settled in this desert, they took it." All the brothers, along with the abbot, believed him. They seized the two brothers as thieves and threw them into prison. The holy father, the eminent prophet, Abba Bsoy, knew by the Holy Spirit the evil that had befallen these brothers. He rose in haste, went to the desert of Daqwa, and found the two brothers in prison. He said to the abbot, "These people you have imprisoned are not thieves." The abbot said to him, "It is that prophet old man who told us so." Abba Bsoy said to him, "Gather all the brothers." They all gathered; when they saw the saint, they were blessed by him and all prostrated before him. The holy Abba Bsoy said to them, "What fault have these foreign brothers committed


[p. 348] {#po.49.p348}
አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ 'አስተጋብኦሙ ፣ ለኩሎሙ ፡ አኃወ ። ወተጋብኡ ፤ ኩሎመ” ። ወሶበ ፡ 'ርእይዎ ፡ ለቅዱስ” ፡ ተባረኩ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ወሰገዱ ፡ ሎቱ ፤ ኩሎሙ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ምንተ ፡ አበሱ ፡ ለክሙ ፡ እለ ፡ እንግዳ ፣፤ አኃው፣ ዘአሰርክምዎሙ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ እስመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ሠረቁ ፡ ወነሥኡ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ አኃው ። ወገዐረ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በሕሊናሁ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ለአረጋዊ ፡ ግበር ፡ ፍቅረ ፤ ምስሌየ ፡ እስመ ፡ አንተ ፡ ጽኑዕ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ርእየት ፡ ዘያስተርኢ ፡ ለከ ። ወይ ቤሎ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ኢሰሐትኩ ፡ አላ ፡ እሙነ ፡ ተናገርኩ”፤ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ግበር ፡ ፍቅረ ፣፤ ወእመን ፡ ከመ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ መከራ ፡ እምሰይጣን ፡ ከመ ፡ ያእምሩ ፡ አኃው ፡ 'ዘንተ ፡ ስሕተተከ ። ወአባ ፡ ብሶይሰ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ራትዕ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ መንፈሳዊ ። ወሰገዱ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ አኃው ። ወለውእቱሰ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ገሠጽዎ ፡ ወአደንገፁ ፡ ልቦ ። ወይቤ ፡ 'ሰይጣን ፡ አስሐተኒ”' ። ወሶበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ወፅአ ፡ እኔምሁ ፡ መንፈሰ” ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ በአምሳለ ፡ ሐራዊያን ፡ ዘገዳም ፡ ወመጓርጺሁ ፡ ነዊኅ ፡ ዘወፅአ ፡ እምአፉሁ ። ወፈቀደ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስጥቆ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ አረጋዊ ። ወከልኦ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ወአውገዞ” ፡ ወተዘርወ ፣ ከመ ፡ ጢስ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእዩ ፡ አኃው ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ሰገዱ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ወሰገደ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አረጋዊ ። ወይቤልዎ ፡ ስረይ ፡ ለነ ፡ ኦአቡነ ፡ ቅዱስ ። በአማን ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ የኀድርም፤ ፡ ላዕሌከ ። ወበጊ ዜሃ ፡ አውፅእዎሙ ፡ ለ ፪አኃው ፡ እምቤተ”” ፡ ሞቅሕ ። 'ወበመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዘተከሥተ ፡ ሎቱ” ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ለአበ ፡ ምኔት ፡ ሖር ፡ መካነ ፡ እገሌ ፡ ወበህየ ፡ ትረክብ ፡ ዘተሰርቀ ። ወኢነገሮ ፡ መኑ ፡ 'ውእቱ ፡ ዘሰረቀ ፡ ወአዘዞ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይንግር ፡ ወኢ ለመኑሂዎሞ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ከሠተ ፡ ሎቱ” ፡ ከመ ፡ ያርሕቅ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ውዳሴ” ።
against you, that you have shackled them?" The old man said to him, "They stole and took the brothers' property." Our holy father groaned in his spirit and said to the old man, "Do me the favor of informing me, for you are certain of this vision that appeared to you." The old man said to him, "I was not mistaken, but I spoke to you truthfully." The holy Abba Bsoy said to him, "Do me the favor of listening to me and believe that this is a temptation of Satan, so that the brothers may recognize this error of yours." Abba Bsoy was a righteous prophet, a spiritual man. The brothers prostrated before him; as for the old man, they reprimanded him and made his heart tremble: he said, "Satan has deceived me." When he had spoken thus, the spirit of Satan went out of him in the form of a wild boar with long tusks that protruded from its mouth. It wanted to tear the old man to pieces. Abba Bsoy prevented it and cursed it, and it dissipated like smoke. When the brothers saw what had happened, they prostrated before Abba Bsoy and the old man prostrated with them. They said to him, "Forgive us, our holy father; in truth, God dwells upon you." And immediately, they brought the two brothers out of prison. By the Holy Spirit that had been revealed to him, Bsoy said to the abbot, "Go to such-and-such a place and you will find there what was stolen." He did not tell him who it was that had stolen, and he ordered him to tell no one that he himself (= Bsoy) had revealed the location to him, in order to distance praise from himself. The abbot went and found the object as the saint had told him. He glorified God who resides upon his saints.
ወሖረ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አበ ፡ ምኔት ፡ ወረከበ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወሰብሖ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘየሐድር ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ቅዱሳኒሁ ። ወኮነ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ጽኑዕ ፤ በሥርዐቱ” ፡ ፈድፋደ ። ወኢመዐትም””" ፡ ወየዋህ ፡ ወሰላማዊ ። ወመጽአ ፡ 'ኅበ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ፳8እኅ:፤ ፡ ወምስሌሁ ፡ ሕፃን”"" ። ወነበረ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ 'ሕዳጠ ፡ መዋዕለ” ። ወሕፃንሰ” ፡ ያንሶሱ ፤ ለፌ ፤ ወለፌ ። ወብ ዙኀ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ይትዐቀፉ ፡ አኃው ። "ወነገርዎ ፡ አኃው ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ እስመ ፡ ናሁ ፡ኮነ ፡ ለነ ፡ መርሐ ፡ ዕቅፍት ። ወይእዜኒ ፡ ገሥጾ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ወልድ” ። ወአቡነሰ ፡ ኢይቤሎ ፡ ምንተኒ ። ካዕበ ፡ ይቤልዎ ፡ አኃው ፡ ኢትጌሥጾኑ”" ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢያሕስም””' ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ኩልነ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ደቂቅየ ፡ ለምንት ፣ ከመዝ” ፡ እሠርዖ ፡ ወእኤዝዞ ። ወሖረ ፡ ፳እምነ ፡ አኃው ፡ ከመ ፡ ይዝብጦ ። ወከልኦ””" ፡ አረጋዊ ፡፤ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ እመሰ ፡ 'ከመዝ ፡ ገበርኩ፤ ፡ አማስንኩ ፡ ጸማየ ፡ ወኩሎ ፡ ዘገበርኩ ፡ በመዋዕለ ፡ ሕይወትየ ። ወይቤልዎ ፡ አኃው ፡ መኑ፤ ፡ ዘያማስን ፡ ለከ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ሎዎ ፡ ዘአጥረይኩ ፡ "ይጠፍእ ፡ ለእመዎ፤ ፡ ተማዕኩ ፡ ወተላኩይኩ ። ወከመዝ፡ ፡ ይሜህረነ ፡ አቡነዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢንገሥጽ ፡ ረድአ ፡ ዘይከውን ፡ ለነ ፡ ወኢንትምዓዕ ፡
Our father was very firm in his conduct, not irascible, gentle and peaceful. A brother came to our father, a small child with him. He stayed with the old man for a few days. As for the small child, he went here and there. The brothers were scandalized by him many times. The brothers spoke to our father, saying, "Behold, a guide of scandal has come to us; now, discipline this child." But our father said nothing to him. The brothers said to him again, "Will you not discipline him so that he does not cause us harm?" He said to them, "My children, why should I give him a rule and an order in this way?" One of the brothers went to strike him (= the child), the old man prevented it and said to them, "If I acted thus, I would ruin all my labor and everything I have done during the days of my life." The brothers said to him, "Who would cause this ruin for you?" He said to them, "Everything I have acquired would be destroyed if I became angry and quarreled. Thus our Father teaches us so that we do not discipline the disciple who comes to us, nor become angry with him."


[p. 350] {#po.49.p350}
ላዕሌሁ› ። ወይብል ፡ መጽሐፍ ፡ ገሥጽ ፡ ወልደከ ፡ በበትር”፤ ፡ እስመ” ፡ ኢይመ ውት ፡ ወልድ ፡ 'በተግሣጸ ፤ አቡሁ”” ። ወነፍሱ ፡ ቡሩከ ፡ 'ወባቀዐ ፡ ይከውን” ፡ ለከ ፡ ወለአምላኩ ። ወበኩሉ”፤ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ወይናዝዞ”። ፡ በእንተ ፥ ንጽሕናሁ ፡ ወድንግልናሁ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ይከውን ፡ ለከ ፡ ምኔት ፡ በዝ ንቱ ፤ ገዳመዎ፤ ፡ አስቄጢ ፤ ወይትጋብኡ ፡ ኀቤሃ ፡ ነፍሳት ፣ ብዙኃት” ፡ ወያአ ርጉ ፡ መሥዋዕተ ፡ ወሞርባነ ፡ ለስምየ ፡ ወትከውን ፡ አበ ፡ ላዕለ” ፡ ብዝኃን ፡ አኃው። ወይቤሎ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ስረይ ፡ ሊተ ፡ እግዚእየ ፡ እስመ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ አነ ፡ ወኢይክል ፤ ቀዊመ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ መልእክት ። ዝንቱሰ ፡ ግብር ፡ ይደሉ ፡ ለሰብእ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ አለ ፡ የዐቅቡ ፡ ትእዛዘከ ፡ ወአንሰ ፡ ኃጥእ ። ወእግዚእነሰ ፡ አስተጋብአ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ብዙኃነ ፡ አኃወ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንበሩ ፡ ኀቤሁ ። 'ወአልበሶሙ ፡ አስኬማ ፡ ዘመነኮሳት ። ወነበሩ” ፡ ውስተ ፡ በዐት ፡ ቅሩበ ፡ ዚአሁ” ። ወኩሎ ፡ አሚረ ፡ ይሜህሮሙ ፡ ፈሪሀ ፡ እግዚአ ብሔር ፡ ወአንብቦ ፡ መጸሕፍት ፡ ቅዱሳት ፡ ወመዝሙረ ፡ ዳዊት ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ደቂ ቅየ ፣ ተገበሩ” ፡ በእደዊክሙ”” ፣፡ ከመ ፡ ተሀቡ፤ ፣ ምጽዋተ ። 'ወይጸንሐክሙ ፡
Scripture says: 'Correct your son with a rod! for the son does not die from his father's correction. His soul will be blessed and useful to you and to his God.' At all times, our Lord came to Abba Bsoy and encouraged him regarding his purity and his virginity. The Lord said to him, "There will be a monastery for you in this desert of Scetis and many souls will gather there; they will offer sacrifice and offering for my name and you will become a father to many brothers." The saint said to him, "Forgive me, my Lord, I am an old man and cannot cope with this ministry. This work is suitable for holy people who keep your commandment; I am a sinner." Our Lord gathered many brothers for him so that they might dwell with him, and Bsoy clothed them in the monastic habit. They lived in cells near his. Every day, he taught them to fear God and to read the Holy Scriptures and the Psalms of David. He said to them, "My children, work with your hands to give alms, and a reward will await you in the heavens."
ፍዳ ፡ በሰማያት ። "ወሀሎ ፡ ፳እጐ ፡ ዘይሰምዖ" ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ እንዘ ፡ 'ይኤዝዞሙ ፡ ለአኃው ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትገበሩ ፡ በእደዊሆሙ ፡ ወሐለየ ፡ በልቡ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ አብ ፡ ይገብር፣፣ በእደዊሁ ፡፡'ወየአዝዘነ ። ወለእመሰ ፡ ኢኮነ ፡ ለምንት ፡ ይኤዝዘነ' ። ወአእመረ ፡ አቡነ ፡ በመንፈስ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ወልድየ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ትሔሊ ፡፤ ወታንጐረኾር ፡ ላዕሌየ ። እስመ ፡ አነ ፡ እሜህሮሙ ፡ ለአኃው ፡ ወእጴጹንዖሙ ፡ ለግብር ፡ ወአንብቦ ፡ መጻሕፍት ፡ ወመዝ ሙራት ፡ ወተልእኮ ፤ ወጸሎታት ። ኢታአምርኑ ፡ ከመ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፤ እትጌብር ፡ በእንተ ፡ ነፍሳቲክሙ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ጊዜ ። ወሰሚዖ" ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እጐ ፡ 'ወገረ ፡ ደጓዕሌ' ፡ እምእዴሁ ፡ 'ወሰገደ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ ለአቡነ'! ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ስረይ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ኦአ ቡየ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ኦወልድየ ፡ 'እግዚአብሔር ፡ ያአምር" ፡እመ ፡ገበርኩ፣ "ወእመ ፡ ኢገበርኩ ። እምዘ” ፡ መጸእኩ ፡ ውስተዝ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ኢያስተትኩ” ፡ ገቢረ ፡ ወተልእኮ ፡ መዓልተ ፡ ወሌሊተ ። ወሶበ ፡ እፌጽም ፡ ጸሎተ ፡ እገብር ፡ በእደዊየ ፡ ወአጻሙ"” ፡ ሥጋየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይመአኒ"'' ፡ ወኢይስድደኒ ፡፤ ውስተ ፡ ገሃነም ። ወዘያጻሙ!፤' ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ወያሐምም ፡ በጾም ፡ ወበጸሎት ፡ ወአንብቦ ፡ ወተልእኮ ፡ "ይድኅን ፡ እምገሀነም” ። ወዘያረብሕ ፡ በዝየ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ይጸንዕ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ፍትወተ ፡ ውርዙት” ፡ ወይወርስ ፡ እሳተ ፡ ገሃነም፤፤ ። "ወዘንተ ፡ ሰሚዖሙ” ፡ አኃው ፡ ሰገዱ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወይቤሉ ፡ ስረይ ፡ ለነ ፡ ኦአቡነ ፡ 'እስመ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘይክል ፡ ገቢረ ፡ዘአንተ ፡ ትገብር፤ ፡ ወነፍስከ ፡ ትትፌሣሕ ፡ በምግባር ፡ መንፈሳዊት ፡ 'ወለብዙኃን ፣ ትጹው
There was a brother who heard our father prescribing to the brothers to work with their hands. He thought in his heart and said, "If the father works with his own hands, let him command us; but if he does not work, why does he command us?" Our father knew this by the spirit and said to him, "My son, you think this and you murmur against me. Now I teach the brothers and strengthen them for work, the reading of the Scriptures and the Psalms, the service of God, and prayers. Do you not know that before God I work for your souls at all times?" Upon hearing this, the brother threw the palm fibers he was holding far from his hand, prostrated before our father, and said, "Forgive me, my father." Abba Bsoy said to him, "My son, God knows whether I have worked or whether I have not worked. Since I came to this desert, I have not ceased to work and to serve God day and night. When I finish prayer, I work with my hands and I tire my body so that it does not overcome me and drive me into hell. He who tires his body and tests it by fasting, by prayer, by reading the Scriptures, and by the service of God is saved from hell. But he who here below lets his flesh profit strengthens against himself the desires of youth and will inherit the fire of hell." Upon hearing this, the brothers prostrated before him and said, "Forgive us, our father; indeed, no one can do what you do, your soul rejoices


[p. 352] {#po.49.p352}
ዖሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሕይወት ። ወብዙኃ ፡ ይገብር” ፡ አቡነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በኅቡእ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ያእምርዎ ። ይቤ ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ጊዜ” ፡ "ኮኑ ፡ ድልዋነ ፡ እምነ ፡ አኃው ፡ ረኪቦሙ ፡ ፍድፍድናሁ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ቅዱስ ። እስመ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ገዳማዊያን ፡ ባሕታዊያን ፡ ጽኑዓን ፡ ነበሩ ፡ ውስተዝ ፡ ገዳም” ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትአመር ፡ ስሞሙ ፡ ወይንበር ፡ ለዓለም ፡ ወነሥኡ ፡ ዐስቦሙ ። ወፈቀደ” ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ” ፡ ይትሐነጽ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ምኔታት” ፡ ለለ” ፡ አስማቲሆሙ ፡ ወአብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያናት ፡ ይትሐነጽ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ በመካናቲሆሙ ፡'ወያ ዕርጉ ፡ በውስቴታ ፡ መሥዋዕተ ፡ ወዮርባነ ፡ እንተ ፡ ትበርህ ፡ ከመ ፡ ፀሐይ ፡ ዘያሐዩ ፡ ነፍሰ”! ፤ ወሥጋነ ። እሉ ፡ ንጹሐን ፡ እለ ፡ ኀረዮሙ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወከሠቶሙ ፡ ወረሰዮሙ ፡ መርሶ ፡ ለዕንቡዛን ፡ በዝ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ከመ ፡ ያድኅኑ ፡ ነፍሳተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዘይመጸእ ፡ ኀቤሆሙ ፡ በልብ ፡ ርቱዕ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሀገር ፡ 'ዘባቲ ፣ ጥቅመ ፡ ጽኑአ ፡ ወትሜግብ ፡ እለ ፡ ውስቴታ” ። ቀዲሙሰ” ፡ ዘሠርዐ” ፡ ሕገገ ፡ መንፈሳዊተ ፡ 'ዝንቱ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ አባ ፡ '(መቃሪ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዘወፅአ ፡ ስሙዓቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ምድር ። ወኮነ ፡ ምምሕፃነ ፡ ወመራሔ ፡ ሕይወት” ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ዘይነብር ፡ ውስተዝ ፡ ገዳም ። "ዝንቱ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ መቃሪቾ ። ወእምድኅሬሁ ፡ አነ ፡ ሕጹጽ ፡ ዮሐንስ ። ወለም ንት ፡ አነ ፡ እቄድስ ፡ ርእስየ ። 'ወአንሰ ፡ እጽሕፍ” ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ዘኀረዮ ፡ እግዚአብ ሔር ፡ዘተነበየ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በምዊ” ፡ በመካን ፡ እንተ ፡ ተከልኩ ፡ ባቲ ፡ ፅፀ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዘኒ ፡ አቡየ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ 'አባ ፡ ብሶይ” ፡ 'ዘሐይወ ፡ በሥርዐት ፤ ወፍድፍድና” ። ወእሉ ፡ አበው ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ አብሮም ፣ ወመክሰምያኖስ ፡ ወዱማድየስ ፡ እሉ ፡ ዘመርሖሙ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ” ፡ ወመሐሮሙ ፡ ሥርዐተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አርአያ ፡ ቅዱስ ። እሉ ፡ ፪ ቅዱሳን ፡ እለ ፡ አብርሁ ፡ በገዳመ ፡ አስቄጢ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዕንቄ ፣ ባሕርይ ፡ ዘእጹብ ፡ ጫጡ” ፡ ወከመ ፡ ፪ አርድእት ፡ እለ ፡ ዜነዉ ፡ በወንጌል ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዓለም ። ወረከብነ ፡ ጽሑፍ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ ከመ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ኀረየ ፡ ሮ"” ወ ፪አርድእት ፡ ወፈነዎሙ ፡ ይስብኩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ መካን ። ወኢከሠተ ፡ ለነ ፡ አስማቲሆሙ ። ' ወዘንተሰ ፡ ከሠተ ፡ ለነ ፡ አስማቲሆሙ ፡ ለ ፲ ወ ፪ሐዋርያት ፡ እስመ ፡ ቀዲሙ ፡ ኅረዮሙ ፡ ወረሰዮሙ ፡ ብርሃነ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ዓለም” ። ወከማሁ ፡ ከሠተ ፡ ለነ ፡ በዝየ ፡ አስማተ ፡ እሉ ፡ ፪ቅዱሳን ፡ ዓበይት ፡ 'ዘየዓብዩ ፡ እምካልኣን ፣ ቅዱሳን” ። "ወለአብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ወፅአ ፡ ዜናሁ ፤ ውስተ' ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወከሠተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ጸጋ ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ 'በውስተ ፡ መካኑ” ።
in the spiritual work and you call many people to eternal life." Our holy father did much in secret without it being known. Abba John said, "Many times, brothers became worthy, having encountered the excellence of our holy father." Indeed, many strong solitary hermits lived in this desert so that their name might be known and remain forever, and they received their reward. God willed that monasteries be built for them in each of their names. Churches are built for them at the site where they lived to offer there the sacrifice and the offering that shine like the sun which gives life to our soul and our body. These are the pure ones whom God chose; he revealed them and made them a haven for the weak-minded in this world so that they might save the soul of anyone who comes to them with a righteous heart, like the city that has a strong wall and protects those who are within it. First, he who instituted the spiritual law is this prophet Abba Macarius (Magari), he whose fame has spread throughout the earth. He was the refuge and the guide of life for anyone who lives in this desert. That one is our holy father Macarius. After him, I, John the Little. Why should I praise myself? I write for the one whom God chose, the one about whom our holy father Bamoy prophesied at the place where I planted a tree as my spiritual father had ordered me. It is Abba Bsoy who lived in discipline and excellence. These are the holy fathers Abrom, Maxime (Maksemyanos), and Domèce (Dumadyus), those whom Abba Macarius guided; he taught them the discipline of this holy example. These four saints are those who shone in the desert of Scetis like a pearl of great price and like the four apostles who announced the Gospel in the whole universe. We have found written in the Gospel that Christ chose seventy-two disciples and sent them to preach in every place. But he did not reveal their names to us. But he revealed this to us—the names of the twelve apostles—for he chose them first and made them light for the whole universe. Likewise, he revealed to us here the names of these four saints. As for the holy father Bsoy, his fame has spread over the whole earth and God has revealed a great grace in the place of his sojourn.


[p. 354] {#po.49.p354}
ወረከቡ ፡ በርበር ፡ ዳወ8ሮመነኮሳተ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ማኅፈድ ፡ ልዑል ፡ ዘይብልዎ ፡ ብይሞን ፡ ወቀተልዎሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ። 'ወእንዘ ፡ ይገብኡ ፡ ለብሔሮሙ ፡ አእረፉ ፡ ምስለ ፤ ሰረገላሆሙ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዐዘቅተ ፡ ማይ ፡ ዘአባ ፣ ብሶይ?” ። ወነሥኡ ፡ አስይ ፍቲሆሙ ፡ ዘምሉእ ፡ እምደመ ፡ እሉ ፡፤ መነኮሳት ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ወኀፀቡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዐዘቅት ። ወኮነ ፡ ምዩ ፡ ፈውሰ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ሕሙማን ፡ እስከ ፡ ዮም ። ስብሐት ፡ ለእግ ዚአብሔር ፡ ዘሠምሮሙ ፡ ለቅዱሳኒሁ”“ ።
Barbarians found forty-nine monks near a high tower called Pijamoun (Beymon) and they killed them all. As they were returning to their country, they rested with their carts near the well of Abba Bsoy. They took their swords, which were full of the blood of the martyr monks, and washed them in the well. Its water became a remedy for all the sick until today. Glory to God who takes pleasure in his saints!
"ወቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ወትረ ፡ የሐውር ፡ ኅበ ፡ አባ ፡ ባውሊ= ። ወሀሎ ፡ "ወልድ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ሲራስ ፡ ዘእምአንስና” ፡ ሀገር ። 'ወይፈቅድ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንሣእ ፡ በረከቶሙ” ፡ ወይፈቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይልበስ ፡ አልባሲሆሙ” ፡ ወይትጋደል ፡ በከመ ፡ ገድ ሎሙ ። ወብዙኃ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ወይነብር ፡ ህየ" ። ወሶበ ፡ መጽኡ ፣ እሙንቱ ፡ አበው ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ርእየ” ፡ ጸጋ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኅዱር ፡ ላዕ ሌሆሙ" ፣፡ ይትበሀሉ ፡ አባ ፡ ባውሊ ፡ ወአባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ሥጋነ ፡ በዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡፤ አሐደ ፡ ንብረተ ፡ ወነፍሳቲነ”፤ ፡ በዓለም ፤ ዘይመጽእ ፡ አሐደ ፡ ማኅደረ ። ወአእመሩ” ፡ ከመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ 'ያቀውም ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ወያስተጋብእ ፡ ሕዝበ ፡ ብዙኃ” ። ወብዙኃ ፡ ይትፋቀሩ ፡ አባ ፡ ፋውሊ ፡ ወአባ ፡ ብሶይ ። ወወትረ ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ፋውሊ” ፡ ወይትናገሩ ፡ እመጻሕፍት ፡ ቅዱሳት” ፡ ከመ ፡ ኤልያስ ፡ ወኤ ልሳዕ ፡ ነቢያት” ። እስመ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ይሰፍሕ ፡ ነፍሰ ፡ ነቢያት ። ወይቤ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ፋውሊ ፡'አእቱየ ፡ ፋውሊ ፡ ጽናዕ” ፡ ከመ ፡ ትግበር ፡ ወትጸሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎነ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ንፃእ ፡ እምኔሃ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አባ ፡ ፋውሊ ፡ ጸሊ ፡ ላዕሌየ ፡ ኦቅዱስ ፡ እትወከፍ ፡ እምኔከ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘአዘዝከኒ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ወኢያኀብእ ፡ እምኔከ ፡ ምንተኒ ፡ እምዘ ፡ ብየ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ናምልኮ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ አልባቢነ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘመሀረነ ፡ ወመጽአ ፡ ኅቤነ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ዘመን ፡ ሠናይ። ወንንግድ ፡ በኀምስቱ ፡ መክሊት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትመክአብ ፡ ለነ ፡ ኀበ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ወኢን ትሀከይ ፡ ከመ ፡ አብዳት”' ፡ ወላዕሌነ ፡ ፍቅረ ፡ እግዚእ ። ወንዕቀብ ፡ ፍቅሮ” ፡ ምስለ ፡ ኃምስ ፡ ደናግል ፡ ጠባባት ፡ እለ ፡ አብርሃ” ፡ ቅብአ ፡ መኃትዊሆን ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሣሀል ፡ ላዕሌነ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አባ ፡ ፋውሊ ፡ ጸሊ ፡ በእንቲአየ ፡ ከመ ፡ እትጋደል ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ጽን ፅየ ። ወኮነ ፡ በውእቱ ፡ ዘመን ፡ 'ቅዱስ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ከመ ፡ ቅጽረ ፡ ብርት” ፡ ለዙሉ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ እለ ፡ ይመጽኡ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትባረኩ ፡ እምኔሁ” ። 'ወይትናገሩ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ድን ፡ ወጸሊአ ፡ ዓለመች ። ወንሰግድ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ንሕነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይዘክሩነ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ አምላክነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ አመ ፡ ንቀውም ፡ ቅድሜሁ ።
The holy Abba Bsoy went constantly to Abba Paul (Bawli). There was a young boy by the name of Siras who was from the city of Antinoe (Ansena). He wanted to receive their blessing and desired to put on their monastic garment and to fight spiritually in accordance with their combat. Many times, he came to the desert and stayed there. When these holy fathers came, he saw the grace of God dwelling upon them; Abba Paul and Abba Bsoy said to each other, "May our bodies be on earth in the same sojourn and our souls in the world to come, in the same dwelling." They knew that God was making a numerous people rise for them and gathering them. Bsoy came constantly to Paul and they conversed about the Holy Scriptures like Elijah and Elisha the prophets: indeed, the Holy Spirit expands the soul of the prophets. Abba Bsoy said to Abba Paul, "My brother Paul, be strong to work and toil while we are in this world before we depart from it." Abba Paul said to him, "Pray for me, O saint, so that I may welcome from you everything you will order me, and I will hide nothing from you of what is in me." Abba Bsoy said to him, "Let us adore God with all our heart because he has instructed us and has come to us in this beautiful time. Let us trade our five talents so that they may be doubled for us before the Lord and let us not be idle like the foolish virgins while the love of the Lord is upon us. Let us keep his love with the five wise virgins who lit the oil of their lamps so that he may have mercy on us." Abba Paul said to him, "Pray for me so that I may fight spiritually with all my strength." At that time, the saint Bsoy was like a rampart of brass for all the monks who came to be blessed by him. They conversed about the word of salvation and the hatred of the world. We prostrate before them so that they may remember us before Christ our God so that he may have pity on us when we stand before him.


[p. 356] {#po.49.p356}
ወሶበ ፡ ቀርበ ፡ ወበጽሐ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ዕረፍቱ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ወኖኅ ፣፡ መዋዕ ሊሁ ፡ ወረስአ ፣ ብዙኀኞ ፡ ወአዕረገ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ መሥዋዕተ” ፣ ውኩፈ ፡ ለአምላኩ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ይቤለኒ ፡ በከመ ፡ ዓመታት ፡ እለ ፡ ነበርኩ ፡ በገዳመ ፡ አስቄጢ ፡ ከማሁ" ፣ ነበርኩ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ኅበ ፡ አበው ፡ ባኪሪዎ ፡ በማሪስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትለአኮሙ ፡ ወእ ሴብሖ ፡ ለእግዚእየ” ። ወነበረ” ፡ አባ ፡ ፋውሊ ፡ በገዳም ፡ ዓመታተ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይቄድስ ፡፤ ለአምላኩ ። ወሥርዐቱ፤ ፡ ወግብሩ ፡ በፍድፍድና” ። ይቤ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ "አልቦ ፡ ዘየዐቢ ፡ ገድል ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘኢያወፅእ ፡ ቃለ” ፡ ቅድመ ፡ 'ይተናገር ፡ እጉሁ” ። ካዕበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዓመታተ ፡እለ ፡ ነበርኩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምኔት ፡ 'ገበርኩ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ስዉረ” ፡ "ዘአልቦ ፡ ዘያአምረኒ” ። ወባቲ ፡ እሴፎ ፡ ዐስበ ። ወሶበ ፡ አእመሩኒ” ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ኢይ ሴፎ ፡ ምንተኒ ። ይቤሉ ፡ በእንቲአሁ” ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘይገብር ፡ ግብረ ፡ ለእመቦ ፡ ዘአእመ ርዎ ፡ ይዌልጥ ፡ ወይወጥን'” ፡ ካልአ ። ' ወይብል ፡ እስመ ፡ ዕራቁ ፡ ውዳሴ!" ፡ ፅሩቀ ፡ ይከውን ፡ 'ወይትነገፍ ፡ ቄጽሉ'” ፡ ወብዙኃን ፡ እለ ፡ ያፈቅርዎ ፡ ለዝ ። ወዝንቱሰ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኀደገ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ወተወከለ ፡ ላዕለ ፤ አግዚአብሔር ፡ እምንአእሱ ፡ ወወረሰ ፡ መንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ ወበጽሐ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰናያት ፡ ዘለዓለም ።
When the moment of the death of the holy Abba Bsoy approached and arrived, when his days had been long and he had become very old, he offered his body as a pleasing sacrifice to his God. Then, he said to me, "According to the years that I have lived in the desert of Scetis, so have I lived in this desert with the fathers... in the South, serving them and glorifying my Lord. Abba Paul lived in the desert for many years, sanctifying his God; his conduct and his actions were in excellence." Abba Bsoy said, "There is no greater spiritual combat than that of one who does not utter a word before his brother has spoken." He said again, "The years that I lived in the monastery, I did a hidden work that no one knew of me. Through it, I hope for a reward. If people had known it, I would hope for nothing." It is said of him that any action he did, if it became known, he would change it and undertake another. He said, "Vain praise is vain (?) and its foliage withers, but many are those who love it." This saint abandoned that, entrusted himself to God from his childhood, inherited the kingdom of heaven, and attained eternal goods.
ወይእዜ ፡ ሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ለርስአን ፡ 'አብ ፡፣ ቅዱስ'” ፡ በዝኃ ፡ መዋዕሊሁ ፡ ወተመ ሥወ ፡ ሥጋሁ" ፡ ወፈቀደ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያውፅኦ” ፡ እምአሰረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ዘምሉእ ፡ ሐዘነ ። ወፈለሰ ፡ አመ ፡ ቿ ለወርኃ ፡ ኀምሌ ፡ ወተወክፈ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ ንጽሕተ ። ወመጽአ ፡ መላእክት ፡፣ ለቀበላሁ'" ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትፌሥሑ ፡ ቦቱ ። ወይኬ ልልዎ ፡ ወያስተፌሥሕዎ ፡ እስከ ፡ አብጽሕዎ ፡ ኅበ ፡ መንግሥት ፡ ልዕልት ፡ በክብር ፡ ወበስብሐት""' ። ወአኃው ፡ እለ ፡ ይነብሩ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ጠብለሉ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በክብር ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ወቀበርዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካን ፡ ኅበ ፡ ይትጋብኡ ፡ ለጸሎት ፣ ወይሴ ብሑ ፡ ወያአዙቱ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ።
When therefore the holy father reached old age, when his days were many and his body weakened, our Lord willed to bring him out of the bonds of this world which is filled with affliction. He passed away on the eighth of the month of Hamle and our Lord welcomed his pure soul. The angels came to meet him, rejoicing over him; they crowned him and rejoiced him until they had brought him to the kingdom on high in honor and glory. The brothers who lived with him wrapped his holy body in great honor and buried him in a place where they gathered for prayer, glorifying and praising God.
ወለእመቦ ፡ 8እምሰብእ ፡ የአምር ፡ እስፍንቱ ፡ ዓመታት ፡ ዘነበረ ፡ በገዳም ፡ ወንሕ ነሰ ፡ ኢያእመርናሃ ። ወተስእልነ'” ፡ ብዙኀ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንግሩነ ፡ ኪያሃ ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ዘአእመራ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይጐይይ!'የ ፡ እምሰብእ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢያክብርዎ ። ወዘንተሰ ፡ ነገሩ ፡ ከመ ፡ ነበረ ፡ ፪ ዓመታተ ፡ በገዳም ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያመልኮ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ሠናየ ፣ አምልኮ ። ወካልአኒ ፡ ዓመታተ ፡ ነበረ ፡ ባሪሰ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ ተባይስ ። ወገብረ ፡ ብዙኀ ፡ ተአምረ ፡ ዘኢይትጐለቀ- ። ወእግዚአብሔር ፡ ባሕቲቱ ፡ ያአምሮ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ የዐስዮ ። ወንሕነሰ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ዘእምተነግረ ፣፤ ሕዳጠ ፡ ጸሐፍነ ፡ እምሥርዐቱ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ጽኑዕ ፡ ባዕ ፡ ምሉአ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኅሩይ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ። ወስብሐት ፤ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ለዘ ፡ አጽንዖ ። ወይደልወነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ንትሉ ፡ ሥርአቶ ፡ ቅድስተ።
If anyone among men knows how many years he lived in the desert, as for us, we do not know. We have inquired many times so that we might be told, but no one knew because he fled from men so that he might not be honored. This is what has been said: he lived seven years in the desert adoring God with a beautiful adoration; he lived other years also in the South in the desert of Thebaid. He performed many miracles, which cannot be counted. God alone knew it and he rewarded him. As for us, while there are many things that could have been told, we have written only a little of the conduct of this strong and useful man, full of the Holy Spirit, Abba Bsoy the elect. Glory to God who made him strong! We must follow his holy conduct

Abba Paul also died, on the seventh of Hamle, at Antorawinigi, in the desert where he was buried. When Abba Sirwes learned that these saints were dead, he boarded a boat and went to Mukyaduh; he took the body of the holy Abba Bsoy and placed it in the boat to bring it to Antinoe in order to place it in the place he had built. When they had entered the river and arrived at the place where Abba Paul was, the boat stopped in the middle of the river for two days and refused to move forward. The sailors grew weak from toiling to make it move forward and could not budge it. There was an old monk who lived in a cave, a spiritual man, his name was Jeremiah (Ermeyas). He rose, went toward the boat, and said to the people on the boat, "Do you know why your boat is not moving forward?" They said, "No." He said to them, "Because of the body of Abba Paul who is from Tammah (Tamwi); indeed, Abba Bsoy and Abba Paul made a pact together while they were alive: they said, 'May our bodies be in one and the same place in this world as well as our souls in the world to come.'" The next day, the holy Abba Barnabas (Barnabas) took people with him, went into the desert, brought the body of the saint Paul from where it was, and placed it in the boat with them. They departed and arrived at the city of Antinoe. They led them to a monastery in great honor and with garments whose price was high. They honored them according to what was fitting for them. People came, were blessed by them, and kissed their bodies. Whoever had the poison of a disease came there and was healed, as the faithful have told us, for they had come from the city of Antinoe to the sojourn of the saint Anthony (Entonyos) to pray there.


[p. 358] {#po.49.p358}
እንጦንዮስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጸልዩ ፡ በህየ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰማዕኩ ፡ ከመ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ቅዱሰ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ኀዘንኩ ፡ ብዙኀ ። ወባሕቱ ፡ ተናዘዝኩ ፡ በልብየ ፡ እስመ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ክብር ፡ ወትፍስሕት ፡ ዘነበረ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሴፎ ፡ ኪያሁ ፤ ወአእኩትክዎ ፡ ለአምላኪየ ፡ ወሰባሕኩ ፡ ስሞ ፡ ቅዱሰ ። ወበጊዜሃ ፡ ዘርእያ ፡ አዕይንትየ ፣፡ ወሰምዓ ፡ እዘንየ ፡ ቃላተ ፡ ወምግባረ ፡ ጸሐ ፍኩ ፡ለክሙ ፡ ኦመፍቅራነ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለበቀዔተ ፡ ነፍስክሙ ። ዝአብ!” ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘፈለሰ ፡ በአማን ፡ ውስተ ፡ መለኮት ፡ ኀበ ፡ መንግሥት ፤ ልዕልት ። ወንሕነኒ ፡ ንስአሎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይረስየነ ፡ አዝማድ ፡ ለዕበዩ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ወእግዝእትነ ፣፤ ማርያም ፡ ወላዲተ ፡ ፍሥሐ ። ወናቄርብ ፡ ስብሐተ ፡ ወውዳሴ'” ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ መሐሪ ፤ ወመስተሣህል ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ማሕየዊ ። ይእዜኒ ፡ ወዘልፈኒ ፡
When I learned that the saint of God Abba Bsoy was dead, I grieved much. But I consoled myself in my heart, for he had attained the honor and the joy that he had remained hoping for, and I praised God and glorified his holy name. Immediately, what my eyes had seen and my ears had heard, words and actions, I wrote it for you, friends of God, for the utility of your soul. This holy father Abba Bsoy is the one who passed in truth toward (?) the divinity on high. As for us, let us pray to God that he may make us relatives of his greatness through the prayer of the holy Abba Bsoy and of Our Lady Mary, mother of joy. We offer glory and praise to our Lord Jesus-Christ with his clement and merciful father and the life-giving Holy Spirit, now and always, in the generations of generations and in the centuries of centuries, amen and amen, so be it.
ወለትውልደ ፡ ትውልድ ፡ ወለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡ ወአሜን ፡ ለይኩን ። ጸሐፍክዋ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ መጽሐፍ ፡ አነ ፡ አብርሃም ፡ ኀጥእ ፡ ወአባሲ ፡ ዘገደፍኩ ፡ በቃ ልየ ፡ አርትዑ ፡ በቃላቲክሙ ። በእንተ ፡ ፍቅረ ፡ ተንስዐ'' ፡ ክርስቶስ ። አጽሐፈኒ ፡ አቡየ ፡ 'አባ ፡ ኖብ፤!' ። ኅቡረ ፡ ይምኀረነ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
I have written this book, I, Abraham the sinner and sinful. What I have omitted in my word, rectify it by your words. For the love of Tanse'a Krestos. My spiritual father Abba Nob made me write. May God have pity on us together in the kingdom of heaven, in the centuries of centuries, amen.
